1b1(rgyud): @##//rgya gar skad du /u d+h+wA dza Ta/mahA ka lpa mahA /bo d+hi sa twa ba ku r+b+ba Na pa tra la bi sa ra ta b+ha
1b2(rgyud): ga ba tI /Ar+ya tA rA /mu la ka lpa nA ma/bod skad du /ral pa gyen rjes kyi rtog pa chen po/byang chub
1b3(rgyud): sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po'i /rnam par 'phrul pa le'u rab 'byam las /bcom ldan 'das ma
1b4(rgyud): 'phags ma sgrol ma'i rtsa ba'i rtog pa zhes bya ba /'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug la phyag 'tshal
1b5(rgyud): lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa'i dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das ma 'phags ma sgrol ma'i gru'i
2a1(rgyud): @##//steng gyi nam mkha'i dkyil na rab tu bzhugs te/ngo mtshar rmad du byung ba/bsam gyis mi khyab pas rab tu phye ba'i byang chub
2a2(rgyud): sems dpa' 'dus pa'i dkyil 'khor dang ldan par bzhugs so//de la bcom ldan 'das kyis gnas gtsang ma'i ris kyi lha'i bu
2a3(rgyud): rnams la bka' stsal pa/lha'i bu khyed rnams nyon mongs cig/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po/spyan ras
2a4(rgyud): gzigs dbang phyug gi rmad du byung ba'i bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i cho 'phrul gyi spyod pa'i ting nge 'dzin dang/rdzu 'phrul gyi khyad
2a5(rgyud): par rnam par thar pa'i dkyil 'khor byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par 'phrul pa sems can thams cad nye bar 'tshe bar byed pa/tshe dang
2a6(rgyud): nad med pa dang/dbang phyug dang/yid kyi shing rta yongs su rdzogs par byed pa'i rigs sngags dang /gsang sngags kyi bzhi
2b1(rgyud): sems can thams cad la phan pa'i ched du bshad kyis /de legs par nyon la legs par yid la gyis shig/ngas bshad do
2b2(rgyud): de nas bcom ldan 'das la legs so zhes so//de nas gnas gtsang ma'i ris kyi lha'i bu de rnams thal mo sbyar ba btud nas
2b3(rgyud): 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyi spyod pa ting nge 'dzin

2b5(rgyud): skor bar byed pa /nyan thos dang/rang sangs rgyas thams cad nges par 'byung bar byed pa/lha dang mi rnams su skye bar byed pa /sdug bsngal
2b6(rgyud): thams cad rab tu zhi bar byed pa/dbul ba dang /nad kyis btab pa thams cad kyi nad sel bar byed pa nyid dang /'jig rten dang 'jig rten
3a1(rgyud): @#//las 'das pa'i thams cad kyi rig sngags dang/gsang sngags kyi spyod pa zil gyis mi non pa nyid dang/bsam pa thams

3a6(rgyud): so//snyoms par bzhugs ma thag tu bcom ldan 'das kyi mdzod spu nas/me tog lta bu'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams kun tu bskul bar byed pa 
3a7(rgyud): zhes bya ba'i 'od zer bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong gis yongs su bskor ba nyid kyis bcom ldan 'das la lan gsum bskor ba byas nas/stong gsum
3b1(rgyud): gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams mtha' yas pa 'das pa nas /nub phyogs kyi char gang ga'i klung gi brgya stong phrag gi bye ma snyed dang mnyam pa'i 'jig rten
3b2(rgyud): gyi khams las 'das pa na/bde ba can zhes bya ba'i 'jig rten gyi khams su rjes su zhugs te gang du de bzhin gshegs pa tshe dpag med bzhugs pa der
3b3(rgyud): byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug kyang sngon gyi smon lam gyi dbang gis byang chub sems dpa' chen po'i spyod pa la
3b4(rgyud): so sor gnas pa rnams kyi thabs gcig tu bzhug pa de 'od zer gyi snang ba mthong bas cung zad rab tu bshad zhal de nyid kyis byang chub sems
3b5(rgyud): dpa'i tshogs la bka' stsal to//kye rgyal ba'i sres rnams /'di ni bdag cag rnams kun du skul bar byed pa'i 'dir 'ongs pa yin pa khyed cag gis
3b6(rgyud): shes par gyis shig/de nas yang byang chub sems dpa' semasadapa' chen po rnams spyan gdangs nas/'di'i 'od zer gyi snang ba gang yin pa der mngon
3b7(rgyud): par phyogs te gnas so//de nas kun du skul bar byed 'do zer des bde ba can gyi 'jig rten gyi khams chen po kun du snang bas kun nas snang bar byas nas
4a1(rgyud): @#//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa tshe dpag med la lan gsum bskor ba byas nas/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po spyan ras gzigs
4a2(rgyud): kyi ye shes kyi spyi bor nub par gyur to//de nas byang chub sems dpa' semasadapa' chen po de bstan las langs nas/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa 'od dpag
4a3(rgyud): med la lan gsum skor ba byas nas mgo bos phyag 'tshal te/pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la btsugs nas bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/bcom
4a4(rgyud): ldan 'das dgongs su gsol//bdag cag bcom ldan 'das de bzhin bshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas shAkya thub pa gang
4a5(rgyud): na ba der song ste/bcom ldan 'das kyis mi 'jed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams de dang/bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pa la lta ba dang/phyag 'tshal zhing snyen bkur ba dang/rig
4a6(rgyud): sngags dang/gsang sngags thams cad kyi spyod pa dang/sgrub thabs kyi cho ga dang/dkyil 'khor gyi cho ga'i rtog pa dang/gsang ba'i ras ris kyi cho ga dang/zlas pa dang /rig pa'i
4a7(rgyud): dbang skur ba thams cad khyad par stan dus sems can thams cad kyi bsam pa yongs su rdzogs par 'tshal lo/de skad ces gsol pa dang/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs
4a8(rgyud): pa 'od dpag med pas /byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po de la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//sems dpa' chen po gang de lta de'i dus la brab par na song cig/nga'i tshig
4b1(rgyud): gis kyang bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pa la gnod pa chung ngam/nyams ba chung ba 'am/ldang ba yang ngam/reg par gnas sam /zhes smod par bya'o//de nas yang bcom ldan 'das de 
4b2(rgyud): bzhin gshegs pa 'od dpag tu med pas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/sems dpa' chen po gang ga'i 
4b3(rgyud): klu brgya stong phrag gi bye ma la sogs pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyis kyang khyod kyi rigs dang /gsang sngags kyi spyod pa dang /dkyil 
4b4(rgyud): 'khor gyi rtog pa dang /gsang ba'i dbang skur ba dang /phyag rgya dang /ras ris kyi cho ga dang /spyan bsreg dang /bzlas pa'i nges pa bsam pa thams cad yongs su rdzogs 
4b5(rgyud): par byed pa/sems can thams cad kun nas dag par byed pa rin po che snang ba'i le'u rab 'byam 'das pa dang /ma 'ongs pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i ye shes rnams kyis rig 
4b6(rgyud): sngags dang /gsang sngags la 'jug par byed pa /ston pa mthar thug par zin pa/nang gi dus dang ldan pa dang /dam tshig rab 'jam gyi le'u dang /'jig rten dang 'jig rten las 
4b7(rgyud): 'das pa mtha' dag ma dag ma lus par sdus pa/sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad dang /'phags pa nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas dang /byang chub sems dpa'i sa la 
4b8(rgyud): zhugs pa dang /spyod pa mthar thug par gsungs pa dang ldan pa dag gsungs pa la yang 'di la rjes su yid rang ba kho na'o/sems dpa' chen po/da lta de'i dus la bab par shes khyod song shig/
5a1(rgyud): //shAkya thub pa'i drung du de la mngon du phyogs pas dam pa'i chos kyi rnam grangs 'di nyon la khyod kyang smra bar 'gyur ro//'di la yang rtsa ba'i rig sngags su 'gyur pa ni/na mo rad
5a2(rgyud): na tra yA ya/na mo a mi tA b+ha ya/ta thA ga ta ya/ar ha te samya kaM b+hud d+ha ya/na mo ar+ya a ba lo ki tai shwa ra ya/bo d+hi si d+hA ya/mahA sa twa ya/na mo b+ha ga t+yai/ar+ya ta ra ye/bo d+hi si ta yai/mahA
5a3(rgyud): sa ta ye/tad+ya thA/ta re ri dzu ka d+harma na r+yi te/ta re tud tA re/shA t+na d+harma na r+ya te/tA re a ne b+hi lA r+ya/d+harma ni r+ya te/tud ta re/tu re tu re tu hi ni/tra sa ni pi tri sa ni/o ha ni mahA ni/dzam b+ha ni sta ma
5a4(rgyud): pa ni pra b+he nA pa b+hi/ti li ti li tsi li tsi li/be ga sA mi ni kun N+DI ku N+DI/mahA ku N+DA/d+ha ra d+ha ra/b+han pa tA hu ru hu ru/ku ru ku ru /a haM yA yA/tsin+ta yA mi tsin+ta ya taM mi ku ru swA dA yA
5a5(rgyud): swahA////'di ni sems dpa' chen po khyod kyi 'phags me sgrol ma'i rtsa ba'i rig pa yin te/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi snying po'o//de bzhin gshegs pa rnams
5a6(rgyud): thams cad kyis gsungs par 'gyur ba ste/khyod kyis kyang da lta smra bar 'gyur ro//mi 'jed gi 'jig rten gyi khams dri+eng rgyas pas rnam par phye ba'i mthar shAkya thub pa byon te/shAkya thub pas
5a7(rgyud): rjes su gnang ba'o//'di la mchog gi snying por 'gyur ba yang ////na mo rad na tu yA yA/na mo ar+ya ba lo ki ti shwa rwa ya/bo d+hi sa ta ya/mahA sa ta ya /ta re tu ta re tu te re swahA////'di la nye ba'i
5a8(rgyud): snying po'o//na mo rad na tra ya yA/na maHsarba bud d+ha bu d+hi sa ta naM/a ku ru ku ru le swahA//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po 'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug bcom
5b1(rgyud): ldan 'das kyis rjes su gnang nas byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa'i rgyu 'thun pa/byang chub kyi snying po kun du thob par byed pa/thams cad kyi bkod pa'i brgyan zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la 
5b2(rgyud): snyoms par bzhugs so//snyoms par bzhugs ma thag tu phyogs bzhi rnams dang/stong dang 'og dang/thad ka'i mthar thug pa thams cad du thams cad dang ldan pa'i phyogs kyi 'jig rten gyi
5b3(rgyud): khams su rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis kun du gang bar gyur to//rgyal ba'i sras po legs so legs so//gang khyod ting nge 'dzin gyi khyad par 'di lta bu la snyoms 
5b4(rgyud): par zhugs pa ni/nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas thams cad kyis mi nus te/byang chub sems dpa' spyod pa la rab tu bzhugs pas/sa bcu la rab tu bzhugs pa rnams kyis kyang
5b5(rgyud): snyoms par 'jug par mi nus so//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa 'od dpag med/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasade rnams kyis thabs gcig byams pa
5b6(rgyud): dang bcas pa/bcom ldan 'das 'phags ma sgrol ma'i mchog gi snying po mchog tu gsang ba/don thams cad sgrub par byed pa yi ge gcig pa zhes bya ba/mchog tu gsang ba'i sems can thams
5b7(rgyud): cad kyi las thams cad byed ma/rig sngags gzhan rnams kyis kyang /rig sngags dang gsang sngags kyi spyod pa'i khyad par sgrub par byed pa dang/'thun pa'i rig sngags gsung bar gyur to//de nas
5b8(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das de bzhin bshegs pa che dpag med yud tsam zhig ci yang mi gsung bar 'gyur to//thams cad du thams cad dang ldan pa'i 'jig rten la sangs rgyas kyi spyan gyis kun du gzigs
6a1(rgyud): @#//nas;sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi dgongs pa/byams pa'i bdag nyid kyi sems kyi rig sngags gsungs par gyur to//na mo sarba bu d+ha n-i/oM gIH'di ni bcom ldan
6a2(rgyud): 'das ma'i mchog gi snying po las thams cad byed pa'o//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug ting nge 'dzin de las langs nas 'di lta ste /skyes bu stobs
6a3(rgyud): dang ldan pa zhes bya ba lag pa skum pa las rkyang ba 'am/rkyang ba las skum pa dang/se gol rtog pa tsam gyis dang/mig 'byed 'dzum sam skad cig tsam gyi ting nge 'dzin/rdzu 'phrul gyi stobs
6a4(rgyud): 'gyogs pa'i blo zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyi khyad par rnam par 'phrul pa nyid ces bya ba la snyoms par bzhugs te/mi mjed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams su 'ongs kyang slad 'khod do

6a7(rgyud): zer chen po kun tu snang bas kun du snang bar byas nas /rin po che snang ba'i bdag po'i rgyan gsal bar byed pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par bzhugs par gyur to//byang chub sems
6a8(rgyud): dpa' sems dpa' chen po spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug ting nge 'dzin la snyoms pa zhugs ma thag tu khang pa rtsegs pa rin po che du mas rnam par phye ba dang/rin po che'i gdugs du mas dpag tshad
6a9(rgyud): brgya stong phrag du khyab pa dang/lha'i gos dang/dar gyi brgyan gyis nye bar mdzes par spras pa dang/lha'i me tog dang/ba dan dang/rgyal mtshan dang/phreng ba dang/rin po che g.yer kha'i dra ba bcings pa
6b1(rgyud): sgra snyan pa 'khrol ba 'jug pa dang ldan pa /byang chub sems dpa' so sor gnas par byed pa lha'i dri dang /phreng ba dang /byug pa dang /phye ma'i char 'bebs par byed pa mngon par sprul te/bcom ldan 'das
6b2(rgyud): shAkya thub pa la mchod pa'i las byed do/ngo mtshar rmad du byung ba'i cho 'phrul byang chub sems dpa'i rnam 'phrul mthong ngo//de nas gnas gtsang ma'i ris kyi lha'i bu de rnams dga' ba dang bcas pas /spu zing
6b3(rgyud): zhes byed do//lha'i gnas rnams thams cad rab tu g.yo ba dang ldan pa mthong nas skrag cing snying 'dar te 'on te 'byor pa yongs nyams sam/ci zhes phan tshun rings thabs su skad 'thon pos smra ba brtsom
6b4(rgyud): zhes 'di skad ces kyang smras so//bcom ldan 'das yongs su skyobs shig/shAkya thub pa yongasu skyobs shig/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis thams cad dang ldan pa'i lha'i 'khor la
6b5(rgyud): bka' stsal to//ma 'jigs shig 'di ni byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug /de bzhin gshegs pa tshe dpag med kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing kun
6b6(rgyud): nas me tog can lta bu nas nga nyid lta ba dang /phyag 'tshal zhing snyen bkur byed par 'dod te/don chen po'i spyod pa dang /rig sngags kyi tshig dang /chos kyi tshig rmad du byung ba yangs pa nges par bstan pa'i
6b7(rgyud): phyir 'ongs pa'o//de nas bcom ldan 'dasashAkya thub pa la lan gsum bskor ba byas nas /bcom ldan 'das la mig mi 'dzum par ltas te/zhabs gnyis la btud nas yi ge tshig rab tu smras pa
6b8(rgyud): 'di rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la mngon par stod do//phyag 'tshal skyes bu mkhyen khyod la /phyag 'tshal skyes bu mchog khyod la /spyod pa'i don kun sgrub pa po/phyag 'tshal skyes bu rtso khyod la//
7a1(rgyud): @#//don med thams cad zlog pa po//phyag 'tshal skyes bu seng ge khyod//ngan 'gro thams cad 'joms pa po//dpa' bo chen po khyod phyag 'tshal //bsod nams dri ni mtha' yas pa//phyag 'tshal 
7a2(rgyud): skyes bu pad dkar khyod//srid gsum 'dab ni rnam sbyong ba//phyag 'tshal skyes bu pad ma khyod//sdug bsngal thams cad rnam grol byed//thams cad grol khyod phyag 'tshal lo//gdung ba thams cad legs 'dul 
7a3(rgyud): byed//zhi ba khyod la phyag 'tshal lo//sngags kyi spyod don sgrub pa po//thams cad grub khyod phyag 'tshal lo//bkra shis kun gyis bkra shis pa//bkra shis khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/chos rnams thams cad kun du
7a4(rgyud): rtogs//sangs rgyas dam tshig khyod phyag 'tshal//chos rnams kun gyi de bzhin nyid//spros pa med pa'i rnam pa ni//yang dag rjes 'jug ston pa po//de bzhin gshegs pa khyod phyag 'tshal//shes dang 
7a5(rgyud): shes bya'i dngos po kun//legs par byas shing yang dag thos;/theg gsum lam dang myang 'das lam//rab tu gnas 'jug rab gnas pa//kun mkhyen khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/zhes yi
7a6(rgyud): ge'i tshig rab tu smras pa//'di rnams kyis bstod pa'i tshig gis bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar mngon par stod nas 'di skad ces kyang gsol to//bcom ldan 'das 'di nas gang ga'i klung brgya stong phrag
7a7(rgyud): gi bye ma dang /mnyam pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'das nas nub phyogs kyi char kun nas me tog ldan zhes bya ba'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing du gyur pa der bde ba can zhes bya ba'i 'jig rten gyi khams gang na/de
7a8(rgyud): bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rig pa dang zhabs su ldan pa//bde bar gshegs pa 'jig rten mkhyen pa bla na med pa/skyes bu 'dul ba'i kha lo sgyur ba /lha dang mi
7b1(rgyud): rnams kyi ston pa /tshe dpag tu med pa bzhugs te/des chos ston par mdzad pa ni/thog mar dge ba/bar du dge ba /tha mar dge ba/don bzang po tshig 'bru bzang po ma 'dres pa/yongs su rdzogasu pa yongasu
7b2(rgyud): dag pa yongasu byang ba tshangs par spyod pa yang dag par ston par mdzad cing //de 'di ltar bzhugs te/'tsho zhing bzhes la chos kyang ston par mdzad do//rig sngags dang gsang sngags kyi don gyi spyod pa rnams
7b3(rgyud): dang/byang chub sems dpa'i sde snod dang/lam gsum gyi spyod pa'i rjes su 'jug pa'i lam dang/skyob pa dang/gnas dang/skyabs dang/dpung gnyen dang/bde ba dang/shin du mthar thug pa dang/shin du yongasu
7b4(rgyud): mthar gtugs pas/sems can thams cad la ston te/bcom ldan 'das de nyid kyi mdzad pas rjes su gnang nas;bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs kyi drung dang nye ba 'dir 'ongs te/bcom
7b5(rgyud): ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa 'od dpag med de/bcom ldan 'das la gnod pa chung ngam/nyam nga ba chung ngam ldang ba yang ngam/snyung ba mi mnga' 'am/bde ba la reg par gnas saM
7b6(rgyud): zhes yongs su 'dri bar byed de//'di skad ces gsungs so//gang rnam pa 'di lta bu'i snyigs ma'i dus gang du bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pa'i sku de yang chos ston par mdzad cing rjes su rtogs par bya ba'i
7b7(rgyud): skye bo la lam gsum gyis byang grol dang lha dang mi rnams kyi skye ba rnyed par mdzad pa de ni ngo mtshar to//bcom ldan 'das shAkya thubapa'i sku de brtson 'grus kyis gang du skal ba med pa'i sems can gyi 
7b8(rgyud): tshogs srid pa gsum yang dag par 'joms pa'i lam du mthar thug pa/'jug par byed pa gtan du grub pa dang/bde ba'i rjes su 'thun pa'i mya ngan las 'das pa la/sems can rnams rab tu bgod
8a1(rgyud): @#//par mdzad pa ni ngo mtshar ro//bcom ldan 'das 'on kyang sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi thugs ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'daskho nas mkhyen gyis/bdag gis sangs rgyas 
8a2(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis ngo mtshar rmad du byung ba/bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa rnams//sangs rgyas kyi rnam 'phrul du shes par 'dod pa 'am /thugs kyi spyod pa la rab tu 'jug
8a3(rgyud): pa'i bsgrub pa dang/sems dpa' shes par 'dod pa 'am/sems dpa' shes par 'dod pa 'am/mtha' dag nges par ston pa ni bskal ba bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong phrag rnams kyis kyang brjod par
8a4(rgyud): yang ga la nus//gang yang de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi de bzhin gshegs pa rnam sgrub pa sdus ba dang /so so ba ma lus pa'i ngo bo 'dus ma byas pa'i chos nyid du lta bar
8a5(rgyud): bya ste/mthong bas spong bar bya ba spong ba dang/ring por mi thogs pa spyod pa smra bar 'dod pa 'am/yon tan ston par 'dod pa ni de bzhin gshegs pa bcom ldan 'das kho nas 'di
8a6(rgyud): mkhyen gyis/bdag gis ni ma yin no//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gis rdzu 'phrul pa sprul te/rin po che chen po'i pad ma la bcom ldan 'das shAkya 
8a7(rgyud): thub pa la lta bzhin du bzhugs so//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa shAkya thub pas /byang chub sems dpa' semsdpa' chen po spyan ras gzigis dbang phyug la rnam pa sna tshogs pa'i
8a8(rgyud): gtam gyi rjes su 'brangs ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i gtam gyis mthar gyis dri ba sngon du 'gro ba /phan tshun chos bstan pa'i mthar rigs kyi/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa sgrub pa'i don du nye bar tshogs
8b1(rgyud): pa rnams la tshangs pa'i dbyangs dang/ka la bing ka dang/ku ta ra dang/bi ta ras bsgrubs pa'i rnga bo che'i dbyangs su grags pa'i sgra dbyangs kyis/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po 'phags pa spyan
8b2(rgyud): ras gzigs dbang phyug la bka' stsal pa/sems dpa' chen po khyod legs par 'ongs sam/sems dpa' chen po'i spyod pa dang/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi byin rlabs bsgrubs pa dang/byang chub 
8b3(rgyud): sems dpa' thams cad don yang dag par thob par byed pa dang/rig sngags dang/gsang sngags kyi tshig thams cad dang/gsang ba'i dbang skur ba dang bcas pa dang/phyag rgya dang/dkyil 'khor dang rtog
8b4(rgyud): pa'i dbang skur ba dang/tshe dang nad med pa dang/dbang phyug dang bsam pa thams cad yongs su rdzogs par byed pa dang/bsgrub thabs thams cad kyi cho ga'i rgyud dang/shes pa dang shes bya
8b5(rgyud): dus kyi mthar nub pa dang/rgyal srid dang zhing 'das pa dang/ma 'ongs pa dang/da ltar byung ba mdor bsdus pa las sems can thams cad kyi bsam pa thams cad yongs
8b6(rgyud): par byed pa dang/rang gi yon tan 'byung ba gsang sngags kyi spyod pa'i mthar 'jug par byed pa/sems can gzhan dga' bar byed pa/mi snang ba dang nam mkha' 'gro bar byed la /rkang pa rab tu skus
8b7(rgyud): pa/yid gzhungs par byed pa/'gugs par byed pa/sa 'og tu rab tu 'jug par byed pa/mngon par spyod par byed pa/don thams cad thob pa/'bras bu dang bcas pa/gnod sbyin dang/gnod sbyin ma dang
8b8(rgyud): bcad kyi dang/sha za dang/'byung po thams cad 'gugs par byed pa/byis pa dang/rgan po dang/lang tsho ji ltar gnas pa bzhin du gnas par byed pa ste/bdor bsdus na las thams cad byed pa yid
9a1(rgyud): @#//kyi shing rta thams cad yongs su rdzogs par byed pa/rgyas pa can dang/zhi ba can gyi las thams cad kyis gsungs pa/dag pa'i sems can rig sngags dang/gsang sngags kyis spyod pa'i don chos 
9a2(rgyud): kyi mdzod/skye bo mang po la phan pa'i ched dang/skye bo mang po la bde ba'i ched du /lha dang mi rnams dang/sems can thams cad la ston cig/de nas yang byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po spyan ras gzig+sa
9a3(rgyud): dbang phyug sangs rgyas kyis byin gyis rlab kyis kun du skul bar byed pa/rgyan bkod pa'i 'od zer snang ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs so//snyoms par bzhugs ma thag tu dga' ba'i sems can
9a4(rgyud): du ma gang ga'i klung gi bye ma snyed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams dang/sangs rgyas kyi zhing rnams 'od zer gyi snang bas ji srid 'og min gyi srid pa nas/ji srid mnar med pa'i dmyal ba chen po'i bar gyi sems
9a5(rgyud): can gang la la sdug bsngal gyis shin du gdungs pa'i sdug bsngal thams cad rab tu zhi bar byed pa'i 'gro ba rnams zhi ba dang/nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas dang/byang chub sems dpa' dang/sangs
9a6(rgyud): rgyas thams cad bcom ldan 'das kyis yang dag par skul lam/slar yang 'od zer des byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po spyan ras gzigs pha dbyang phyug gi spyi bor nub par gyur to//'di la shar
9a7(rgyud): phyogs la sogs par rnam par gnas pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing gang yin pa der sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams bskul ba'i 'od zer des /rlung gi dkyil 'khor yang dag par bskul bas sgrub pa 'di 
9a8(rgyud): 'di lta ste/de bzhin gshegs pa zla ba nyid kyis snang ba dri kun du gsal ba'i dpal zhes bya ba dang/de bzhin gshegs pa sman gyi bla bA+ai dur+ya'i 'od kyi rgyal po zhes bya ba dang/de bzhinagashegs pa kun du snang ba'i
9b1(rgyud): dpal zhes bya ba dang/de bzhin gshegs pa yang dag par 'phags pa'i rgyal po zhes bya ba dang/de bzhinagashegs pa sa la'i dbang po'i rgyal po zhes bya ba dang /de bzhin gshegs pa 'jig rten dbang po'i
9b2(rgyud): rgyal po zhes bya ba dang/debazhin gshegs pa tshe dang ye shes dpag tu med pa'i rnam par nges pa'i rgyal po zhes bya ba dang/de bzhin gshegs pakun du snang mtha' yas pa'i rgyal po'i dbang po zhes bya ba dang
9b3(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa bA+ai dur+ya'i snang ba'i 'od zer gyi rgyal po'i dbang po zhes bya ba dang/de lta bu lasogs pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' yongs su bsku+or ba mtha' yas shing
9b4(rgyud): mu med pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams rnams su de bzhin gshegs pa dag bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams /mi mjed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams su sgrol ma'i
9b5(rgyud): gru'i tshal na gnas pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa dang bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas shAkya thub pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po spyan ras gzigs dbang
9b6(rgyud): phyug rig sngags dang/gsang sngags kyi spyod pa nges par ston pa'i tshig gi don gyi le'u rab 'byam gsungs pa de la/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasade rnams 'dzum pa yang dag par ston par mdzad do//
9b7(rgyud): de bzhin du lho dang nub dang/byang gi phyogs rnams dang/phyogs bral rnams su zhes pas steng dang 'og dang/thad ka thams cad dang ldan pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing rnams su snang bar byas nas /sangs rgyas
9b8(rgyud): kyi zhing rnams thams cad du yang bdud kyi gnas thams cad dpa' skong bar byas te/byang chub sems dpa'i tshogs kyis yongasu skor ba dang bcas pa nyan thos kyi dge 'dun dang bcas pa /mdun du bdar
10a1(rgyud): @#//nas de bzhin gshegs pa'i spyod yul gnas pa/sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul pa byang chub sems dpa'i bdag nyid chen po nyid lta bur 'dod pas mchog gi rig pa'i rjesu spyod pa/sgrub 
10a2(rgyud): pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyi khyad par gyi le'u rab 'byam de bzhin gshegs pas stan pa thogs pa med pa yang gsal bar byed par 'dod pa so sor 'khod par gyur to//'di lta ste lag bzangs dang/rin chen bzang 
10a3(rgyud): po dang/brtul shugs bzang po dang/spyan bzang po dang/dga' ba bzang po dang/chos bzang po dang/dngos grub char bzang po dang/thams cad 'phags dang/chos 'phags dang/rin chen 'phags dang/rin
10a4(rgyud): chen dpal dang/lhun po'i dpal dang/bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i dpal dang/'od byed dpal dang/rab snang dpal dang/don thams cad kyi dpal dang/lag na thams cad rin chen dang/rtsug
10a5(rgyud): na nor bu dang/lag na rin chen dang/lag na lhun po'i rgyal mtshan dang/rnam par snang mdzad snying po dang/rin chen snying po dang/ye shes snying po dang/don bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i snying po

10a7(rgyud): sgra dbyangs dang/rnga bo che 'i nga ro'i rgyal po dang/mtha' yas gsal ba'i ye shes rgyal po dang/mun pa mun nam thams cad rnam par sel ba'i rgyal po dang/rnam par g.yeng ba thams cad rnam par 'joms
10a8(rgyud): pa'i rgyal po dang/spyod pa thams cad phul du byung ba'i ye shes kyi rgyal po dang/'jig rten dbang po'i rgyal po dang/phul du byung ba'i dbang po'i rgyal po dang/rnam par sel ba'i rgyal po dang/rang gi
10b1(rgyud): mi g.yo ba'i rgyal po dang/nyi ma'i rgyal po dang/thams cad gsal ba'i yang dag par 'phags pa'i rgyal po dang/rang bzhin yang dag par 'phags pa'irgyal po dang/dngos po med pa yang dag par

10b4(rgyud): bde ba mu khyud dang/mu khyud kyi tog dang/'dod pa'i lha dang/lha'i lha dang/lte rgyas pa dang/'jig rten zhi ba dang/nye ba'i mchi ltas dang/rnga bo che dang/grub pa dang/dkar por grags pa dang/lha
10b5(rgyud): rab tu bzod dka' la gdul dka' ba dang/gsal dka' dang/ring du song ba dang/ring por len pa dang/ring por gnas pa dang/mtho zhing mtho ba'i mun pa dang/mkha' snang dang/

10b7(rgyud): po dang/dkar po dang/i mi ki mi dang/tha chung dang/dka' bas mtsho ba dang/bde bar mtsho ba dang/legs par skyes pa dang/du ba'i me tog dang/mchod sbyin tog dang/dkar po'i tog dang/tog bzang po dang

11a1(rgyud): @#//ba dang/ci bgyi dang/mnyam pa byed pa dang/'jig rten byed pa dang/snang ba byed pa dang/nyin mor byed pa dang/'byung bar byed pa dang/don thams cad byed pa dang/grub par byed pa dang/'od byed pa
11a2(rgyud): dang/kun du gsal bar byed pa dang/rnga bo ches dbyangs dang/dbyangs bzang dang/mtha' yas dbyangs dang/tog gi dbyangs dang/'byung ba'i thub pa dang/gser thub dang/log par dad sel dang/'od srungs

11b7(rgyud): ngan song bzod dang/rnam par 'gyur med dang/bkra shis rnam 'brel dang/nad thams cad srung ba zhes bya ba 'di rnams kyis kyang gzhan yang byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams dang/thabs gcig tu bcom
11b8(rgyud): ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa shAkya thub pa'i spyod yul du sgrol ma'i gru la bzhugs par gyur to//gzhan yang byang chub sems pa' sems dpa' chen po bud med kyi gzugs 'dzin pa rnams kyis 'jig rten
12a1(rgyud): @#//gyi spyod pa'i don mtha' yas pa sgrub pa /sems can mtha' dag gi bsam pa rnam par 'jug par byed pa'i lam la rab tu gnas su 'jug pa'i bya ba bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i rigasngags kyi bzhi 
12a2(rgyud): rjesu 'dzin par byed pa/sman gyi cha byad kyi gzugs 'dzin pa rnams dang/'dab chags kyi tshogs rnam pa sna tshogs pa dang/gnod sbyin pa dang/srin po dang/nor bu dang /sngags dang/rin po che'i rgyal po dang/sems
12a3(rgyud): can dang sems can ma yin par grangs med pa thams cad kun du 'jug cing/sems can gyi spyod pa'i rjesu 'jug pa rnams dang/bsam pa ji lta bas sems can 'dul ba de lta bu'i rjesu 'jug pa yin pa rnams

12a6(rgyud): 'chad par byed pa rnams te/'di lta ste/gtsug tor mngon par 'phags pa dang/lo ma mtha' yas dang/lo ma brgyad pa dang/gtsug rtor rgyal ba dang/mngon par 'phags pa'i gtsug tor dang/pad ma'i 'od zer dang
12a7(rgyud): gser gyi 'od zer dang/'od zer dkar po dang/gtsug tor dkod pa dang/gser gyi phung po dang/dkar po'i phung po dang/gzi brjid phung po dang/nor bu'i phung po dang/kun nas phung po dang/rnam par grags pa'i phung po dang
12a8(rgyud): 'byung ba'i phung po dang/bden pa'i phung po dang/dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid kyi phung po dang/ma nor ba de bzhin nyid kyi phung po ste/'di rnams dang gzhan yang gtsug tor gyi rgyal po chos kyi dbyings mtha' yas pa la rab tu
12b1(rgyud): du zhugs pa sems can rnams kyi bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du/mngon par 'dod pa yongs su skong bar byed pa/skye bo thams cad kyi yid kyi bsam pa rtogs pa bskal pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong phrag gis kyang mi
12b2(rgyud): nus pa'i gtsug tor gyi rgyal po nam mkha' mthar thug par brjod pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i stobs dang/pha rol gnon pa'i bdag nyid chen po lta bur ston par 'dod pas/mtha' dag rgyas par bshad pa las
12b3(rgyud): mdor bsdus nas bstan par bya ste/rig pa'i rgyal po rnams kyis 'dus par bshad par bya ba 'di lta ste/mdzod spu dang/smin ma dang/spyan dang/rdza ma dang/snyan dang/'grani pa dang/mi 'jigs pa dang/snying rje dang/
12b4(rgyud): byams pa dang/rtse ba'i tshim pa dang/shes rab dang 'od zer dang/sems dpa' dang/'od zer dri ma med pa dang/chos gos de 'di rnams dang/gzhan yang rig pa'i rgyal po mtha' yas mu med pa de bzhin
12b5(rgyud): gshegs pa'i spyi gtsug nas spros pa rnams 'di lta ste/de bzhin gshegs pa'i lhung bzed dang/chos kyi 'khor lo dang/de bzhin gshegs pa'i bizam cha dang/de bzhin gshegs pa 'i theg pa
12b6(rgyud): dang/de bzhin gshegs pa'ikun tu bsal ba dang/de bzhin gshegs pa' ིgsung dang/de bzhin gshegs pa'i mtha' dang/de bzhin gshegs dang/de bzhin gshegs dri ma dang/de bzhin gshegs
12b7(rgyud): rgyal mtshan dang/de bzhin gshegs ba'i tog dang/de bzhin gshegs  pa'i mtshan ma te/'di rnams dang gzhan yang de bzhin gshegs pa'i sngags gsungs pa rnams te/rig pa'i rgyal po dang rgyal mo dang
12b8(rgyud): ci bgyid dang bran pho dang bran mo dang /pho nya dang pha nya mo dang/gnod sbyin dang/gnod sbyin mo dang/sems can dang sems can ma yin pa so sor khyad par du 'phags pa'i brgyan bkod pa'i chos kyi sprin nges par
13a1(rgyud): @#//spros pa /ting nge 'dzin gyi khyad par gyi rgyu 'thun pa bye ba brgya stong phrag tshad med pas/yongs su skor ba rig pa'i tshogs thams cad gong nas gong du rab tu 'jug bzhin pa'i rig pa'i rgyal po de rnams 
13a2(rgyud): kyang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i spyod yul der rigs bzang por lhag par gnas pa dang ldan par 'gyur ro//rig pa'i rgyal mo 'di lta ste/de bzhin gshegs pa phyag bcu gnyis dang /phyag drug pa dang /phyag
13a3(rgyud): bzhi pa dang /hal ha la dang /don yod bzhags pa dang /rta mgrin dang /mgrin pa mtha' yas dang /mgrin pa sngon po dang /mgrin pa bzang po dang /'grul bzangs dang /mgrin dkar dang /mgrin sngon dang /

13a7(rgyud): gtsug tor la sogs pa rnams kyis chos kyi sprin gyi rgyu 'thun pa mtha' yas pa/bsgrub pa'i ting nge 'dzin du gyur pa brgya stong bye ba khrag khrig du ma'i rigs pas yongasu bskor ba dang /rig pa 'dzin pa'i
13a8(rgyud): rgyal mo du ma dang /'jig rten dbang phyug gi sku'i ting nge 'dzin gyi rnam par spros pa du ma rnams la 'di lta ste/sgrol ma dang bsam pa'i sgrol ma dang /gar ma dang /khro gnyer can ma dang /a nu t+ta ti
13b1(rgyud): dang /lau TI dang /sa thob par byed ma dring /dri ma dang bral ba dang /dkar mo dang /dkar sham ma dang /dkar sham chen mo dang /gos dkar mo dang /'jig rten gos dkar mo dang /dri med gos ma dang /byang chub yan lag

23b5(rgyud): gu dang /nye ba'i greng dang /'jig rten rab mchog dang /rab mchog ldan pa dang /dpal ldan dang /'jig rten mgon po dang /chad ldan dang /rgyal ba thob pa dang /nor ldan dang /

23b7(rgyud): der /'dus shing 'khod par gyur to//khyim sum bcu rtsa drug la 'di lta ste /lug dang /glang dang /'khrig pa dang /kar ka ta dang /seng ge dang /bu mo dang /srang dang /sdig pa dang /gzhu dang /
23b8(rgyud): chu srin dang /bum pa dang /nya dang /sre'u dang /nye ba'i bum pa dang /gsar bum ral gri dang /glang po che dang /ma he dang /lha dang /mi dang /dge mtshan dang /dri za dang /'jig rten skyong ba dang /rgyal
24a1(rgyud): @#//ba'i mchog dang/gzi brjid dang/nam mkha' dang /grib ma dang/sa bzhi dang /mun pa dang/bdul dang /nye ba'i bdul dang /sdug bsngal dang/bde ba dang /thar pa'i byang chub dang 
24a2(rgyud): rang byang chub dang/nyan thos dang/dmyal ba'i rigs 'dzin dang/gzi brjid chen po dang/bzi brjid che ldan dang /byol song dang/yid dgas dang /lha ma yin dang /she dang/sha za dang /gnod sbyin
24a3(rgyud): dang/srin po dang /'byung po thams cad dang/phyogs kyi gling po dang/steng 'gro dang /thams cad 'gro dang/rnam byed dang/rnal 'byor 'gro dang /rab tu gnas pa dang /mchog dang 'bring dang /tha ma zhes pa 'di
24a4(rgyud): rnams dang/khyim chen po brgyad stong phrag du mas yongs su skor ba 'di rnams 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor chen por 'dus pa gang na ba der nye bar phyin nas /bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la 
24a5(rgyud): phyag 'tshal nas /rang rang gi gnas su 'khod par gyur to//gang yang gnod sbyin mo chen mo/gnod sbyin mo brgya stong phrag bye bas skor ba 'di lta ste/mig bzangs ma
24a6(rgyud): dang/smin ma bzang mo dang/skra bzangs ma dang/dbyangs snyan ma dang/blo gros bzang mo dang/dbyig ldan ma dang /mig bkra ma dang/bsam rdzogs ma dang/gsang ba ma 
24a7(rgyud): dang/'od pag ma dang/'od bag bzang mo dang/pad ma'i dus dang/mi 'jigs sbyin ma dang/rgyal ma dang/rnam par rgyal ma dang /nam gru can ma dang/skra ldan ma dang/skra mtha'
24a8(rgyud): ma dang/rlung ma dang /yid 'phrog ma dang /yid ldan ma dang /me tog ldan ma dang/me tog grong gnas ma dang/skyems ma dang /'phrog ma dang/dpa' bo ldan ma dang/dpa' mo dang /dpa'
24b1(rgyud): bzangs ma dang /sna bzangs ma dang /sna ldan ma dang /lha mdzes ma dang /ro bzangs ma dang /gsang mchog ma dang /brjod pa la gnas ma dang /mya ngan med ma dang /mun pa mdzes ma dang
24b2(rgyud): snang bar mdzes ma dang /'od ldan ma dang /phul byung ldan ma dang /gzugs ldan ma dang /gzugs mdzes ma dang /dkar min ma dang /zhi ba ma dang /phra mo ma dang /sde ma dang /dga'
24b3(rgyud): byed ma dang /nye bar dga' byed ma dang /'jig rten 'das ma zhes pa zhes pa ste /gnod sbyin mo chen mo 'di rnams /gnod sbyin mo brgya stong phrag du mas yongs su bskor ba de 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor
24b4(rgyud): chen po der /bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pa la phyag 'tshal te /'khod par gyur to //gang yang sha za mo dang /sha za mo brgya stong phrag du mas yongs su bskor pa 
24b5(rgyud): de rnams kyang /bcom ldan 'das shA kya thub pa la phyag 'tshal te 'dus par gyur pa 'di lta ste /rgyan pa mo dang /phyag gdar gyi sha za mo dang /drag mo sha za mo
24b6(rgyud): dang /ta la'i sha za mo dang /'bar ba'i sha za mo dang /thal ba'i sha za mo dang /sha la za ba mo dang /'dzin dga' mo dang /'phrul byed ma dang /rmongs byed ma dang /sgrogs byed
24b7(rgyud): ma dang /skyed byed ldan ma dang /'jig rten mthar gnas ma dang /thal ba'i mtha' can ma dang /'thung gcod ldan ma dang /mang po ma dang /gdul dka' ma dang /e la mo dang /mtshan
24b8(rgyud): ma can ma dang /du ba'i mtha' can ma dang du ba ma dang /du ba bzang rten mo ma zhes bya ba sha za mo chen mo 'di rnams /sha za mo brgya stong phragadu mas yongs su bskor ba de rnams kyang 'khor
25a1(rgyud): @#//'dus pa'i dkyil 'khor chen po der rab tu zhugs par gyur to//gang yang ma mo dang/ma mo chen mo dag 'jig rten gyi rjes su spyod cing /sems can la rnam par 'tshe bar
25a2(rgyud): byed pa gtor ma dang/phreng ba nyid bsdud par byed pa la 'di lta ste/tshangs ma dang/dbang phyug chen mo dang/khyab 'jug ma dang/gzhon nu ma dang/rgan byed ma dang/phag mo dang /
25a3(rgyud): dbang mo dang /gshin rje ma dang/me mo dang /nyi ma'i bu mo dang/'jig rten bar ma dang/chu lha mo dang /dbang ldan mo dang/rlung lha mo dang/gzhan gyi srog 'phrog ma dang
25a4(rgyud): zhal rgyan pa mo dang/dge mtshan ma dang/dge mtshan ma chen mo dang/sbrul mo dang/lus sbrul mo/skye mched ma zhes ba ma mo chen mo 'di rnams /ma mo
25a5(rgyud): brgya stong phrag du mas /yongs su bskor ba de rnams kyang 'khor 'dus pa'i dkyil 'khor chenapo der/sangs rgyas la phyag 'tshal lo zhes tshig du 'don cing /
25a6(rgyud): 'khod par gyur to//de bzhin du mi dang mi ma yin pa rgya stong phrag du ma dang/sems can dang sems can ma yin pa ji srid/dmyal ba chen po gnar med pa nas /srid
25a7(rgyud): pa'i rtse mo'i bar skabs 'dir thams cad nam mkha'i dkyil du gsal bar gyur to//sems can gyi tshogs kyis srog chags 'ga' la yang gnon pa med par 'gyur to//sangs
25a8(rgyud): rgyas kyi byin gyis brlabs dang/byang chub sems dpa'i rdzu 'phrul gyi sems can thams cad spyi bo nas/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das dang/spyan ras gzigs
25b1(rgyud): dbang phyug bzhugs pa yang dag par mthong bar gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pa thams cad dang/ldan pa'i 'khor la/sangs rgyas kyi spyan gyis rnam par
25b2(rgyud): gzigs nas /sems dpa' chen po da la bka' stsal to//sems can dag pa gsang sngags kyi spyod pa'i don rnam par nges pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyi le'u rab 'jam /byang 
25b3(rgyud): chub sems dpa'i sde snod gang de lta de'i dus la bab par shes na shod cig/de nas yang byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po/spyan ras gzigs
25b4(rgyud): dbang phyug bcom ldan 'das shAkya thu ba pas rjes su gnang bas/nam mkha' rang bzhin gyi rgyan bkod pa rdo rje lta bu sdug cing sra ba'i
25b5(rgyud): rgyud bkod pa'i brgyan zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par bzhugs so///sems dpa' chen po snyoms par zhugs ma thag
25b6(rgyud): tu/'khor gyi dkyil 'khor chen po de thams cad dpag tshad brgya stong phrag du mar /rdo rje'i rang bzhin brgya che bar lhag par gnas par
25b7(rgyud): gyur to/der gnod sbyin dang/srin po dang /dri za dang/rlung lha /sha za de rnams mdor bsdus nas/sems can gyi khams thams cad byang 
25b8(rgyud): chub sems dpa'i byin gyis brlabs kyis gzhal yas khang rdo nar bu rin po che lta bur y+da dag par rab tu gan(sa)so//'akh+do par gyur pa rnaM phan tshun rnaM par 'tshe ba med do//de na(sa) seMs dpa' chen po de'i 'khor chen po 'dus pa(ra mkhyen nas khro bo'i rgyal po) mkhyen nas khro bo'i rgyal bo 
26a1(rgyud): sgrol mas non pa la bos par gyur to//kye kye khro bo chen po /sangs rgyas dang /byang chub sems dpa' thams cad 'dus pa 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor gyi chen po 'dus pa 'di yi sems can thams cad srungs shig
26a2(rgyud): dbang du gyis shig/dbang du gyis shig/bdug pa rnams thul cig/zhi ba rnams sad par gyis shig/ma dad pa rnams dad par gyis shig/ji srid nga'i rig sngags dang /gsang sngags spyod pa rjesu sgrub pa/byang chub sems dpa'i
26a3(rgyud): sde snod rgya chen po nyid kyi rig sngags dang /gsang sdags kyi spyod pa'i dkyil 'khor gyi cho ga 'chang ba de srid kyi bar du/'di dag gi phyi rol du song la srungs shig/de skad ces smras pa dang /khro bo'i rgyal po chen po bkang
26a4(rgyud): gnas nas rnam par 'gyur ba chen po'i gzugs yongasu btags te/sems can thams cad bsrung bar bya ba'i don dang /rtan par bya ba'i don du kun du kun nas bkod pa chen po'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor du /khro
26a5(rgyud): bo'i rgyal po sgrol mas non pa/khro bo brgya stong phrag du mas yongasu bskor ba/phyogs bzhi kun dang /de bzhin ltar steng dang 'og dang /thad kar sgra drag por 'don zhing pas rab tu gnas par gyur to//
26a6(rgyud): de nas sems can de rnams zhi ba nyid yid dang ldan pas/yang dag par 'jug pa'i bka' las mi 'da' bar gyur te/sgra 'di lta bu yang thos par gyur to//gang dam tshig 'di ni 'da' bar byed pa 'di dag ni ar rdza ka'i
26a7(rgyud): dog pa bzhin du mgo bo tshal pa brgyar bags par 'gyur to/byang chub sems dpa'i byin gyis rlabs kyang yin no//de nas yang sems dpa' chen po de rang gi rig sngags dang /gsang sngags kyi spyod pa'i don du chos
26a8(rgyud): kyi tshig gsungs pa/chos gcig gis byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po'i gsang sngags 'grub par 'gyur te/gcig po gang zhe na 'di lta ste/chos thams cad spos pa med pa'i rnam pa yang dag par rjesu
26b1(rgyud): mthong ba'o//chos gnyis pa 'di dag la rab tu gnas nas/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos gsang sngags grub par 'gyur te/gnyis dag zhe na byang chub kyi sems yongs su mi
26b2(rgyud): gtong ba dang /sems can thams cad mnyam pa nyid do//chos gsum po rnams kyi rang gi gsang sngags kyi spyod pa'i don nges par bstan pa yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur te/gsum gang zhe na
26b3(rgyud): sems can thams cad mi gtong ba nyid dang/byang chub sems dpa'i tshul khrims kyi sdom pa srung ba nyid dang/rang gi gsang sngags yongs su mi gtong nyid kyis so//chos bzhi dang yang dag par
26b4(rgyud): ldan pas sems dang po bskyed pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i rigs sngags 'grub par 'gyur te/bzhi gang zhe na/rang gi gsang sngags rdzogs pa yongs su mi gtong ba nyid dang /gzhan gyi gsang
26b5(rgyud): sngags nye bar spyod pa min pa nyid dang /sems can thams can la byams pa'i sems nye bar gnas pa nyid dang/snying rje chen po sgom pa'i sems te /chos bzhi po 'di dag dang
26b6(rgyud): yang dag par ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' gsang sngags 'grub par 'gyur ro//chos lngas byang chub sems dpa'i sde snod la yang dag par 'jug cing gsang sngags kyi spyod pa'i sgrub pa
26b7(rgyud): yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur to/lnga gang zhe na/rnam pa sna tshogs pa'i yul la sten pa nyid dang/bas mtha'i dgon pa rten pa nyid dang/sems can gzhan la zhe sdang med pa nyid dang/'jig rten pa'i
26b8(rgyud): sngags la mi lta ba nyid dang/tshul khrims dang/thos pa dang/spyod pa la gnas par byed pa nyid te/chos lnga po 'di dag gis sngags kyi spyod pa'i don gyi dngos grub yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur ro//chos
27a1(rgyud): @#//drug ni gsang sngags kyi spyod pa'i don gyi dngos grub yongasu rdzogs par 'gyur te/drug gang zhe na /dkon mchog gsum la dad pa nye bar spyod pa med pa nyid dang/byang chub sems
27a2(rgyud): dpa' la dad pa spyod pa nyid dang/'jig rten dang 'jig rten las 'das pa'i sngags la kun du dga' ba nyid dang /chos kyi dbyings spros pa la lta ba nyid dang /theg pa chen po'i mdo sde zab mo'i
27a3(rgyud): tshig gi don mi 'dor ba nyid dang/skyo ba med pa'i sems nyid kyis gsang sngags kyi spyod pa'i deni yongasu tshol zhing dge ba'i phyogs yongasu mi spong ba nyid te/chos drug po 'di ni rigs sngags kyi
27a4(rgyud): spyod pa dang /gsang sngags kyi dngos grub la yang dag par kun du 'jug pa nyid du 'gyur ro//chos bdun po rnams ni rig sngags grub ba'i dus kyi cho ga dang/gsang sngags kyi
27a5(rgyud): spyod pa la rjesu 'jug par byed pa nyid du 'gyur te/bdun gang zhe na shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa'i tshul zab mo sgom pa dang /zlog pa dang/nyen pa dang/kha thon byed pa dang /'bri bar byed
