27b1(rgyud): sems can gyi khams dang/chos kyi dbyings spros pa med pa'i de bzhin nyid la yang dag par 'jug pa gnyis su med pa'i rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes yongasu tshol ba las dang/seMs
27b2(rgyud): can thams cad yongasu mi gtong ba theg pa dman pa la dga' ba med pa las te/chos bdun po 'di rnams ni rigs sngags dang /gsang sngags kyi dngos grub yongasu rdzogs par 'gyur ro//
27b3(rgyud): chos brgyad ni rigs sngags dang/gsang sngags kyi spyod pa'i don gyi dngos grub yang dag par 'jug pa nyid du 'gyur te/brgyad gang zhe na/mthong ba dang ma mthong ba'i 'bras bu la dad pas ngo mtshar
27b4(rgyud): bar sems par 'dod pa nyid dang/byang chub sems dpa'i 'bras bus/rdzu 'phrul dang rnam par 'phrul pa la the tsom med pa las dang/phyin ci ma log pa'i gsang sngags 'dzin pa las bla
27b5(rgyud): ma la gus pa las dang/sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi rang gi sngags dang/rgyu dang spyod pa nye bar stan pa mi slu bar 'dzin cing bdog pa thams cad
27b6(rgyud): yongasu gtong ba las dang/dngos grub kyi zhing gnas dang gnas ma yin pa rmi lam du mthong ba ston pa la mkhas pa nyid dang/ser sna dang gnyid dang rmugs pa mtha' dag dang bral bar byed cing /rtag
27b7(rgyud): tu brtson 'grus brtsom la /sangs rgyas dang byang chubasems dpa' thams cad la bdag nyid 'bul bar byed pa las dang/mdor sdus na dge ba'i rtsa ba la ngoms mi shes pa'i go cha bgos te/bgegs
27b8(rgyud): thams cad rab tu sel bar 'dod pa las dang/byang chub kyi rgyud gnon par byed pa dang/long spyod chen po dang/dbang che bar grags pa bdag nyid so sor 'dod pa dang/dbang che bar gragasapa'i gang zag la
28a1(rgyud): //yang dag par 'grogs pa dang/dge ba'i gshes gnyen dang mi 'bral ba dang/byang chub sems dpa' chen po dang yang dag par 'grogs par byed pa ste/chos brgyad po 'di ni/
28a2(rgyud): gsang sngags kyi spyod pa'i don gyi dngos grub las yang dag par 'jug par 'gyur ro//grogs po dag mdor na byang chub kyi sems dang mi 'bral zhing/dkon mchog gsum la lhag par mos pa/mchog
28a3(rgyud): du tshul khrims 'chal pa yang skyo ba med pa'i yid kyis/rtag tu mngon par brtson pas nga'i sngags kyi le'u rab 'byam mtha' sa yas shing/rmad du byung ba byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa'i rgyu 'thun pa'i
28a4(rgyud): yid las byung ba 'grub par 'gyur te/de las gzhan du rtogs par bya ba ma yin no//rnam par rtog pa med pa'i yid du gyur pas nyams sad pa'i rgyur yang sgrub par bya ba yin no/de nas yang thams
28a5(rgyud): cad dang ldan pa'i 'khor rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas dang/byang chub sems dpa'i dang/rang sangs rgyas dang/'phags pa nyan thos kyi byin gyis brlabs rnams tshig 'di skad du brjod par
28a6(rgyud): byed te/kye rgyal ba'i sras sems can thams cad kyi don gyi ched du gsang sngags kyi spyod pa sna tshogs pa'i/don gyi bya ba'i chos kyi tshul la rab tu 'jug pa'i rjes su 'thun pa'i chos ston cing legs par
28a7(rgyud): bstan pa ni legs so legs so//kye ma byang chub sems dpa' chen po rnam pa sna tshogs pa'i chos ston pa'i rjes su 'jug par byed pa gsang sngags kyi spyod pa dge bar legs par gsungs so//rgyal po
28a8(rgyud): chen po la la zhig 'dus pa'i le'u 'di 'dzin par 'gyur ba dang/yid la byed par 'gyur ba dang/g.yul du yang thog mar blang po che'i steng du 'jog par 'gyur ba dang/me tog dang/bdug pa dang dri dang byug pa rnaM
28b1(rgyud): pa sna tshogs pas mchod par gyur pa/de la bdag cag mtshungs par brtag par bgyi phyir rgol ba dang/dgra bo dag dbang du bgyi/pha rol gyi dmag dpung dag 'jig cing bzlog par bgyi /gleg bam du bris nas
28b2(rgyud): rang gi khyim du 'jog par 'gyur ba'i rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo 'am /rgyal po chen pa 'am /rgyal mo chen mo 'am/dge slong ngam/dge slong ma 'am /de snyen nam;dge snyen me de la srung ba chen po dang/
28b3(rgyud): long spyod chen po dang/tshe ring por gyur pa dang/nad med pa dang/rtag tu long spyod 'phel ba nyid kyang bgyi'o//de skad smras nas thams cad dang ldan pa'i 'khor de bcang mi gsung bar gyur to/theg pa chen po 
28b4(rgyud): rigs sngags dang/gsang sngags spyod pa nye bar bstan pa las;ral pa gyen rjes gyi rtog pa chen po byang chub sems dpa' chen po'i rnam par 'phrul pa'i le'u rab 'byam las bcom ldan 'das ma
28b5(rgyud): 'phags ma sgrol ma'i rtsa ba'i rtog pa bdun pa las 'dus pa'i le'u ste/dang pa'o////de nas yang byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug thams cad
28b6(rgyud): dang/ldan pa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor la gzigs nas/sems can thams cad kyi dam tshig la rjes su 'jug cing/kun du lta ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par bzhugs so//byang chub sems dpa' spyan
28b7(rgyud): ras gzigs dbang phyug snyoms par zhugs ma thag tu lta ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi phyogs nas 'od zer bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong phrag du mas yongasu bskor ba'i 'od zer byung ste/sems can gyi khams thams cad
28b8(rgyud): kun nas snang bar byas nas sngar nyid du 'khor 'dus pa'i dkyil 'khor chen po kun du snang bar byas nas 'khod par gyur to/de nas yang byang chub semsdpa' chen po phyag na rdo rjes dag pa'i sems dpa'
29a1(rgyud): @#//spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug la smras pa/sems dpa' chen po'i dam tshig la rjes su 'jug pa zhes bya ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi le'u rab 'byam kyi spyod pa sgrub pa/sems can thams cad dang thun 
29a2(rgyud): mong du gyur pa khyod kyi sngags kyi tshogs gang du sems can rnams yang dag par rjes su zhugs nas/'jig rten dang 'jig rten las 'das pa'i rig sngags dang/gsang sngags kyi dngos grub yang dag par thob pa 
29a3(rgyud): dang ldan pa khyod smros shig/gsang ba'i bdag po gnod sbyin gyi dbang pos/de skad ces smras pa dang/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po spyan ras gzigs dbang phyag gi mchog tu gsang ba'i dkyil 
29a4(rgyud): 'khor rgyud gsungs pa/rigs sngags thams cad yang dag par bskul bar byed ba zhes bya ba'i rdzu 'phrul gyi rnam par ston par byed cing/lag pa g.yas pa bteg nas sor mo'i rtse mos 'khor
29a5(rgyud): gyi dkyil 'khor byed par byed cing/sor mo'i rtse mo der rig pa'i rgyal po bye ba khrag khrig stong phrag dus mas nges par phyung ste/phyung nas kyang thams cad dang ldan pa'i 'khor de snang ba chen pos
29a6(rgyud): snang bar byas nas /gnas par gyur to//de nas byang chub sems dpa' semasadapa' chen po /spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gis khro bo'i rgyal po sgril mas non pa'i snying po las thams cad pa dpa' bo gcig pas
29a7(rgyud): spyan drang ba dang/gshegs su gsol ba dang/zhi ba can dang/rgyas pa can dang/mngon spyod can dang /mi snang ba dang/nam mkha' la 'gro ba dang/sa 'og tu 'jug pa dang/rkang ba rab tu rgyu ba dang/dgug pa dang /rnam 
29a8(rgyud): par snang ba dang/dbang du byed pa dang/dri dang/phreng ba dang/mar me thams cad rang gi rgyud kyi gsang sngags kyi rab tu sbyin par byed te/mdor na ji ltar ji ltar sbyar ba de bzhin de lta de ltar bsgrub par bya bzhin pa ni /yi ge
29b1(rgyud): gsum zhes bya ba brtson 'grus chen pos don thams cad sgrub par byed pa'i khro bo'i rgyal po chen po'o//de gang zhe na/oM a hU~M/'di ni khro bo chen po de'i snying po las thams cad pa ste/dkyil 'khor thams cad
29b2(rgyud): dang/gsang sngags thams cad spyod pa rnams la byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos nges par bstan te/bgegs thams cad 'jig par 'byed pa'o//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa'
29b3(rgyud): chen po spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gis phyag g.yas pa bteg nas khro bo'i spyi bor bzhag ste/'di skad ces smras so//sangs rgyas thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams dgongs
29b4(rgyud): gsol//gang la la phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams na rnam par gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' mtha' yas shing mu med pa'i rdzu 'phrul chen po dang ldan pa rnams dam tshig la lhag par gnas
29b5(rgyud): par bgyis shig//de skad ces smras nas khro bo'i rgyal po de skor nas btang bar gyur to//khro bo'i rgyal po btang ma thag tu thams cad dang ldan pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams dag tu song nas skad
29b6(rgyud): cig tsam gyis gdugs sems can gyi sems can rdzu 'phrul chen po de rnams tshar bcad cing bkug nas 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor chen po dag pa'i sems can la yang dag par bcug te/gnas ji lta ba bzhin du gnas
29b7(rgyud): bar byas nas sems can gdug pa rnams kyi spyi bor kun 'bar ba'i phreng bas 'khrugs par gyur nas gnas par 'gyur ro//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gis
29b8(rgyud): slar yang /'khor gyi dkyil 'khor la gzigs nas sems can khyed cag thams cad nyon cig/gang yang nga'i dam tshig 'di las 'das bar byed pa de la khro bo'i rgyal pos tshar gcod par 'gyur te/gang gi phyir na sangs
30a1(rgyud): @#//rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi dam tshig ni 'dul bar bya ba ma yin pa'i phyir ro//rig sngags dang gsang sngags kyi don gyi tshig gsang chen ji ltar/byang chub sems dpa' rdzu 'phrul chen po dang
30a2(rgyud): ldan pa rnams kyi ting nge 'dzin nges par bstan pa 'chad par byed do/de legs par nyon la legs par yid la zung shig dang ngas bshad do//na mo rad na tra yA ya/na mo a ni drA d+hA yA /ta thA ga ta ya/ar
30a3(rgyud): ha te/se myag sam bu d+ha ya/na mo ar+ya a ba lo ki shwa rA ya/bo d+hi sa ta yA/na mo ba ga wa t+yai /ar+yA ta ra yai/bo ta sa twa yai/mahA sa twa yai /tad+ya thA/ri dzu ka r+d+harma/ni da yA te/hA re tA re tud tA re/
30a4(rgyud): shan+ta d+ha rmi ni r+ya te ta re a na b+hi lA bya d+harma /nid ya te/tud tA re tu hi ni tra sa ni/mahA sa twa ya b+hi Ta sa ni/oM ha mi /mo ha ni /dzam b+ha ni stam b+ha ni/pra pa ni/tsin ta pre b+he /hi li hi li /bi li 
30a5(rgyud): bi li ba li/be ga gA mi ni/ku N+Di ma hu kuN+Di/d+hA ra d+ha ra na pa ti/ta re hu ru hu ru/ku ru ku ra/a haM /yAM yAM/tsat+ta yA ma tsa d+ha taM /me ku ru swAhA////grogs po dag 'di ni nga'i bcom
30a6(rgyud): ldan 'das ma 'phags ma sgrol ma'i rtsa ba'i rig sngags las thams cad byed ma//srid pa gsum gyi chos nye bar bcod pa/ngan 'gro thams cad bzlog par byed pa mo'o//phyag rgya yang 'dir rtsa ba'i
30a7(rgyud): rig sngags shes pa rnam grags pa de la rab tu sbyar bar 'gyur ro//'dir mchog gi snying po las thams cad byed pa mya ngan las 'das pa'i bde ba thob par byed pa/zhi ba thams cad byed pa/sangs
30a8(rgyud): rgyas mngon sum du mthong ba bzhin du nye bar gnas pa /rang nyid nga'i grogs po byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' can thams cad kyi don gyi phyir/mchog tu gsang ba'i rig sngags kyi ngo bos nye
30b1(rgyud): bar gnas pa/bsam pa thams cad yongasu rdzogs par byed pa/gang dang gang dran pa nyid kyis las thams cad mthar byed pa/'jigs pa chen po rnams yongasu sbyong bar byed pa ste/bzlas pas lta smos kyang
30b2(rgyud): ci dgos/de gang zhe na//na mo rad na tra yA ya/na mo ar+yA pa lo ki tai shwa rA ya/bo d+hi sa twa ya/mahA sa twa ya /ta re tu tU re swahA//phyag rgya yang 'dir u ta pa la zhes kyang/rnam par grags pa long spyod thams cad
30b3(rgyud): mngon par 'phel bar byed pa'o//nye ba'i snying por gyur ba yang /mchog tu dpal dang ldan pa dag byed chen po ste/na mo rad na yA ya/na maHsarba bud+d+ha bo d+hi sa twA naM/oM ku ru ku ru lA+e swahA//phyag rgya yang 'di
30b4(rgyud): pad ma'i phreng ba zhes rnam par grags par gyur to/sems can thams cad dbang du byed ma'o//'dir mchog gi snying por gyur pa yang /na maHsarba bud+d+ha ni/oM gI 'di de snying po mchog te 'dor
30b5(rgyud): bsdus na bya ba thams cad la yon tan chen po rgyas pa ste/yon tan mar thug pa mthar thug par brjod par ni dus bskal pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong phrag du mas kyang mi nuso//phyag rgya yang
30b6(rgyud): 'dir brtson 'grus chen po zhes rnam par grags pa bsam pa thams cad yongasu rdzogs par byed pa'o//'dir yang spyan drang ba'i rig sngagasu gyur pa yang //na moHsarba bud d+hA naM/oM he he b+ha ga ba ti shwa ru pi ni sarba
30b7(rgyud): bA la/b+hA Shi ti/pra po d+hi na/A yA ni b+ha ga ba ti/kri ga u t+pa la d+hi ra Ni/ma ma shrad+d+hA yA /tiSh+tha tiSh+tha/sa ma ya nu sma ra /a tra ti ha ha shA sa na hI/ma bi la mA /ku ru ku ru phaT swAhA/'di ni bcom ldan 'das ma spyan
30b8(rgyud): drang ba'i rig sngags te/sangs rgyas thams cad dang /byang chub sems dpa' thams cad dang /rang sangs rgyas thams cad dang/'phags pa nyan thos dang/lha dang /klu dang/gnod sbyin pa dang /dri za dang/lha ma
31a1(rgyud): @#//yin dang࿒nam mkha' lding dang/mi 'am ci dang /lto 'phye chen po dang/sha za dang /srin po dang /'byung po thams cad kyi 'ang ma yin no//tsan dan gyi chu la lan bdun mngon par sngags pa byas te/
31a2(rgyud): phyogs bzhi dang /de bzhin du steng dang/'og dang/thad ka thams cad du gtor bar bya'o//sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad dang/bcom ldan 'das ma sgrol ma dang/'jig rten dang 'jig rten las 'das
31a3(rgyud): pa thams cad kyi 'ang gsang sngags 'di yin te/'byung po'i tshogs thams cad dang/sems can thams cad kyang 'ong bar 'gyur ro////na maHsarba bu d+hA nAM/a tra ti ha sha sA nA nAM/oM d+hu ra d+hu ra /b+hum bA si ni /ma mA ri Ni hUM/
31a4(rgyud): ta Tha sa ma ya nu sma ra swA hA //bdug pa'i rig sngags te/tsan na dan dang/gur gum dang/ga bur gcig tu byas nas/bdug pa phul bar bya'o//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa rnams dang/byang chub sems dpa' rnams
31a5(rgyud): thams cad byon pa rnams spyod kyi bdug pas/yid tshim nas khugs par 'gyur ro//'dir phyag rgyar gyur pa yang pad ma'i phreng ba zhe/grags pa sems can thams cad 'gugs cing zhi ba ste/spyan pa'i rig
31a6(rgyud): sngags la yang phyag rgya pad ma'i phreng ba bzang po 'di nyid do//sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad dang/sems cad thams cad byon pa la yang mchod yon dbul bar bya ste/ga bur dang /tsan dan dang/gur gum
31a7(rgyud): chu dang sbyar nas dz+ha ta'i me tog dang/mA li ka gsar pa dang/dbyar gyi me tog dang /phun nA ka dang/ba ku la dang/rgya spos rnams te 'di dag gang yang rung ba'i me tog gam/dus ji lta ba'i me tog gam/dri bzang po'i me tog
31a8(rgyud): rnams dang /bsres nas sngags 'dis mchod yon dbul bar bya'o//na maHsarba bud+hA nAM/a tra ti ha ta naM sha sA naM/ta d+ya thA/he he ma ma ku ru ni /ka pi shwa r+ca pa d+ha rI Ni/a rgaM pra ti ts+tsha pra ti ts+tsha ya sa ma ya ma nu sma ra /ta khwa nA
31b1(rgyud): mad+h+ya pra pe sha ya /pra pi sha sarba b+hu te/a mu kaM pa ka g+h+rah+na g+h+rah+na a m+ba ra bi tsa ri ni swahA//phyag rgya'i 'dir gang ba zhes grags te/sems can thams cad kyi rjesu 'jug cing brtan pa'o//'dir dra'i rig sngags su 'gyur pa
31b2(rgyud): yang /namaHsarba b+ha d+ha nAM/na maHsa man+ta g+han g+han d+ha/a b+ha Sha shir ye/ta thA gA ta ya/tad+ya thA/gan d+he gan+d+he/gan+d+ha na mo ra me tra tri tsha pra tI tsa tsha de/gan+d+ha sam tA nu tsa ri ni swahA//'dir phyag rgyar 'gyur ba yang /la ma
31b3(rgyud): zhes bya ba bsams pa thams cad yongs su rdzogs par byed pa'o//'dir me tog gi rig sngags su 'gyur ba yang /na maHsarba bud d+ha bo d+hi si to nAM/a pri ta ha ta shA sa nA nAM/na maHsaM ku sa mi ta ra dzA s+ya/ta thA ga ta s+ya/
31b4(rgyud): tad+ya thA/ku su me ku su me/ku su mA b+h+yo/ku su ma pu ra bA si ni/ku su mA ba ti swahA////bdug pa'i sngags 'di nyid kyis sngar bshad pa'i bdug par bya'o//rmad byung bsam mi khyab pa'i gsung /sangs rgyas
31b5(rgyud): kun la phyag byas nas/yang dag rdzogs sangs rgyas gsungs pa'i/gtor ma'i rig sngags rab 'chad byed/na maHsarba bud d+ha bo d+hi sa tA naM/a pra ti ha ta sha sa nA nAM/tad+ya thA/he he b+ha ga ba ti/mahA sa tA
31b6(rgyud): sarba bu b+hA a ba lo ki ti mi bi li mA i ti ba laM grin+d+ha pa ya grih+na hU~M hU~M hU~M hU~M/sarba ta ra phaT swahA/lha ba shos kyang 'dis dbul bar bya ste/'byung po thams cad ma'i gtor mar 'gyur ro//'di yang phyag rgya ni dbyug pa ste bdug
31b7(rgyud): pa thams cad bzlog par byed pa'o////na maHsarba bud d+ha bo d+hi sa twa nAM/a pra ti ha ta sha sanAM nAM/sarba ta mo am+b+ha ka ra bi d+h+yoM /si tA naM /na maHsarba sa man+ta dz+yo ti gan+d+ha/b+ha sa sI yA ya/ta thA ga ta ya/tad+ya thA/he ha b+ha ga bA
31b8(rgyud): na dz+yo te ri sma sha ta sa ha sra/pra ti man+Ta ta shi rI ra/bi kur ba bi kur ba/mahA bo d+hi sa twa sa man+ta/dz+wa lo dau ya ta ta mu d+hi/khu d+ha khu d+ha/a ba lo ka ya/a ba lo ka ya/man+d+ha laM sarba sa tA naM swahA//mar me'i rig sngags te /
32a1(rgyud): //'dir mar me dbul bar bya'o//phyag rgya ni kha phye ba sems can thams cad rnam par snang bar byed pa'o//na moHsa man ta bud d+ha n-I/a pra ti ha ta sha sa nA n-i/tad+ya thA /dzA la dzA la /dzA la dzA la /hu b+hi bo d+ha ka
32a2(rgyud): ha/ri tri Sha na paM ga la /me'i bye ba byed pa'i rig sngags su 'gyur ro //'dir yang phyag na kha sbyar zhes par 'jig rten grags /sems can kun 'od gyis gsal byed/byang chub sems dpa' po ldan dang /thub
32a3(rgyud): pa mchog gis mngon gsungs pa'o//de nas yang byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gis /byang chub sems dpa' phyag na rdo rje la smras pa //gsang ba'i bdag po sngags
32a4(rgyud): 'di ni gsang chen dang bcas shing mchog gis mngon gsungasapa'o/de nas yang byang chub sem du gsang ba yin te/bu khyod la phan drag po'i mtha' /mi zad pa'i rigs su grags /sngags rnams
32a5(rgyud): thams cad gang kho na //mkhas pa rnams kyi sgrub bya ba'o//spyi bo'i gdub po zhes grags pa//rdo rje pad ma'i rigs dag dang /de ni nges par gzhig don du /rig pa 'di ni yang dag brjod //na maH
32a6(rgyud): sarba bud d+ha bi d+hi sa twa naM/a pra ti ha ta sha sa nA nAM /oM ka ra ka ra /ku ru ku ru /ma ma ka t+yaM /dzA b+ha dzA sarba da ha da ha /sarba badzri bi nI siM ka /mU d+ha da ka dzI ban+ti ta ka ra mahA pi tI te ru pi ni ba /tsa pa tsa sa swa du Sh+TaM mahA ga Ni pi 
32a7(rgyud): ti dza b+hin+ta ta ka ra ban+d+ha /sarba gra ha //'ag mu kha /shan+d+ha bu dza Sha ga tse ra na rA tra mA ga na ya /ba Sh+Na ma na ya /bra h+ma d+yaM de ba nA na yA bi lam shA bi lam bi la b+hu Sha hriHman+ta la ma d+h+ye pra sha ba ye/sa ma ya ma nu sma ra /hU~M hU~M
32a8(rgyud): hU~M hU~M hU~M hU~M/phaT phaT phaT /swahA/gsang ba'i bdag po 'di ni mchog tu gsang ba brtson 'grus chen po sgrol ma'i yan lag zhal drug pa zhes bya ba khro bo'i rgyal po chen po thams cad rnam par 'joms pa po ste //'di
32b1(rgyud): glags pa tsam gyis byang chub sems dpa' sa bcu la gnas pa'i dbang phyug rnams kyang rnam par 'jig par byed na/ma rungs pa'i dgegs rnams lta ci smos//'di glags pa tsam 
32b2(rgyud): gyis bsrung ba chen po byas par 'gyur ro//'dir phyag rgya 'di yang rigs chen po zhes bya bar gyur te/bgegs thams cad rnam par byed pa'i khro bo'i rgyal po 'di nyid kyi snying po ni//oM hriHSh+Ti bi
32b3(rgyud): kri tA/nan hUM sarba shr-i truM/tam+na ya phaT phaT swAhA//sngags 'dis dgra thams cad la gzer chen po'i nad dang /yang na rims nyin bzhis zin par 'gyur ro//rtag tu bzlas pas ji srid snang bar byams pa nyid
32b4(rgyud): du mi 'gyur /yang na snying rje'i sems thob par mi 'gyur ro//bzlas pa zin par 'gyur ro//bar du grol bar mi 'gyur zhing mchi bar 'gyur ro//dkon mchog gsum la gnod par byed pa la 
32b5(rgyud): bya'o//zhi ba'i sems pa yod pa gzhan la ma yin no//phyag rgya mdun chen po yang sbyar bar bya'o//'dir nye ba'i snying por gyur pa yang /oM hriHka la ru paM hUM khaM swAhA////snga ma'i 'dung
32b6(rgyud): chen po yang sbyar bar bya'o //bdug pa thams cad la gang 'dod pa de byed par 'gyur ro//mchog gi snying po sangs rgyas thams cad kyis byin gyis brlabs pa /yi ge gcig pa zhes bya ba ni hUM/'di ni las thams
32b7(rgyud): cad pa ste/phyag rgya mdung chen po yang sbyar bar bya'o//phung khrol thams cad bzlog par byed pa /'byung po thams cad dbang du byed pa ste/mdor na khro bo'i rgyal po 'di ni/las thams cad la rab tu sbyar bar 
32b8(rgyud): bya'o//dkyil 'khor gyi dbus su bzlas pas /grub pa'i dus su yang khyad par du 'gyur ro//gshogasu gsol ba'i sngags su gyur pa yang /na maHsarba bud d+ha nAM/a pra ti ha ta sha sa nA nAM/tad+ya thA/dza ya dza yA
33a1(rgyud): @#//su dza ya mahA ka ru na ka /bi shwa rU pi ni/gats+tsha gats+tsha swa b+hA ba ba naM/sarba bu d+ha swa ba si rdza ya sa pa rI bA rAM /swa b+ha ba naM/tsa nu pra pa shes ya /sa ma ya ma nu sma ra /sarbaHsha mi si d+d+h+yaM tu /bi d+yaM pa daHma no
33a2(rgyud): ra tham me pa ri bU ra ya swahA //'di ni gshegs su gsol ba'i sngags te/las thams cad nga la sbyar bar bya'o//phyag rgya bzang po'i gdan zhes grags pa ste /'dis gdan yang sbyin par bya'o//yid kyis
33a3(rgyud): lan bdun bzlas pas thams cad gshegs su gsol lo//'jig rten dang 'jig rten las 'das pa'i dkyil 'khor rnams dang /sngags rnams nyid la yang /sngags kyi dngos grub dang /dam tshig dang 
33a4(rgyud): bzlas pa dang /dus dang nges par sdom pa rnams la yang rab tu sbyar bar bya'o//de nas yang sems dpa' chen pos slar yang /gnas gtsang ma'i lha'i bu rnams la kun du gzigs te /'khor kyi
33a5(rgyud): dkyil 'khor chen po la /rang gi rig pa'i tshogs sngags kyi le'u rab 'byam gsungs par gyur to//na mo //sa man+ta bud+d+ha nAM/a pra ti ha ta sha swA naM/oM ni tri 'di ni nye bar spyod pa'i skra can ma 
33a6(rgyud): zhes bya ba'i rig pa las thams cad pa ste/phyag rgya ni kha bye ba zhes pa gzigs las thams cad la sbyar bar bya'o//na maHsa man+ta bud+d+ha nAM//a pra ti ha ta sha sa nAM /oM ni rig pa 'di ni sdong bu can zhes bya ba spyod
33a7(rgyud): pa pa'i dge ba'i las thams cad byed pa ste/phyag rgya ni bzang po'i gdan gyis /gnod sbyin ma rnams 'gugs par byas pa la sbyar ro//na moHsa man+ta bud+d+ha naM/a tsin t+ya r+b+hu ta ru pa na o Sh+Ta swAhA/mkha'
33a8(rgyud): 'gro ma kun 'joms pa'i phyag rgya sbubs can yang dag sbyar/byang chub sems dpa' bshad pa yi/rig sngags thos pa can zhes bya/sangs rgyas kun gyi pad ma nyid /rab sngags lha'i gzugs can ma /na ma
33b1(rgyud): sa man+ta bud+ha n-i/a pra ti ha ta ga ta tsa ra nI/tad+ya thA/oM ba ra de ba can zhes bya/phyag rgya ud pal nyid ni/dpal gyi dbadag nyid can la sbyar /gzugs ma 'chang ma'i lha mo ni /myur du long spyod rab sgrub
33b2(rgyud): pa'o//na maHsa man+ta bud d+ha n-i/a tsi t+yi d+hu ta rU si ni/oM su ru swahA//phyag rgya mdung dang yang dag ldan /rigs rnams thams cad rnam par 'joms pa'o//na maHsaman+ta bud d+ha n-i/a tsa t+yi rgyu ta
33b3(rgyud): ru bi ni /a tu re swahA/bsams pa'i sgrol ma ldan ma zhes/rab sngags las rnams thams cad pa /dbyug pa'i phyag rgyar yang dag ldan /bgegs rnams 'joms byed /ne sbyar
33b4(rgyud): na maHsarba bud d+ha n-i/a tsi r+y-i dU da /ru bi Ni/tad+ya thA/oM ba'i lo ki ni swahA//rigs pa snang ldan ma zhes bya /'jig rten thos cad dbang du byed //bde ba thams cad rab sbyin pa//
33b5(rgyud): pad ma'i phyag rgyas sbyar bar bya //na maHsa man+ta bu d+ha n-I/a tsi t+ya d+hu ta rU pi nI /tad+ya thA /oM bi sre sa b+hi be /pi swA rU bi nai ka ha ka ha /a bi shA a bi shA/sa ma ya ma nu smar
33b6(rgyud): rU ri ta ShA sa hA////rig pa brtson 'grus chen mo ni //'jig rten pas bstan pa ste//snying po phyag rgya mnyam pa nyid/sems can thams cad 'bebs shing dge/'byung po kun la mchog
33b7(rgyud): sbyin ma/sne tshogs zhes bya yang dag bsgrags /na maHsa man+ta bud+ha n-i/a tsi t+yAM d+hu ta ru pa n-I/tad+ya thA /oM swe te swe ba su swahA//pad ma'i gdan gyis rnam par dgod/las rnams thams cad byed pa'o//
33b8(rgyud): na maHsa man+ta bud+ha naM/a pra ti ha ta ga ti pra tsA ri NI/tad+ya thA oM shI/rig pa 'di ni dpal chen mo //'jig rten mgon gyis bstan pa ste/phyag rgya kha sbyor ldan pa yi /rgyal chen po rab sbyin byed//
34a1(rgyud): //na maHsa man+ta bu d+ha naM/sarba sa tA b+ha ya pra drA yi ka /tad+ya thA /oM a dzi te ku mA ra rU pa ni/e hi A /gats+tsha ma ka ra y-i ku ru swA hA /mi thub ma zhes rnam grags pa /gzhon nu bdud rtsi las byung ba /gang ba'i 
34a2(rgyud): phyag rgyar ldan pa yi/dgra rnamas+tha cad bzlog par byed //na maHsa man+ta bud+ha naM/a tsi d+ya rmU ta ruM pi nI /tad+ya thA/oM dza ye swA hA /oM dza swA hA/bi dza ye swA hA/a pA ra dzi te swA hA/sring mo bzhi zhes rnam grags pa //byang
34a3(rgyud): chub sems dpa'i rjes 'gro ba/sa rnams mtha' dag yongs rgyu zhing /sems can rjes 'dzin byed pa/mang po dum bu ru zhes grags/'di rnams kyi ni rjesu spyod //gru'i gzhon pa mnyam zhon nas /rgya mtsho
34a4(rgyud): la ni nges par gnas /khu tshur phyag rgya rnam par dgod//bsam pa thams cad yongs rdzogs byed//na maHsa man+ta bu d+ha naM/lo ga g+ha d+hi pa tI nAM /tad+ya thA/oM ku mu ri ma hA /ku mA ri krI Ta krI Ta /mahA
34a5(rgyud): rAk+Sha si/bo d+hi sa d+ho nu dz+nyA ta ma yu ba sa ne sA shA/sa ra shA sA ni rak+ta gri /rak+ta gan+tu nu le pa na pri ye pri ye/kha kha khA hi khA hi/hU~M nr-i tu ra ka ta ra bu kha/Sh+pA rtsi ta mU d+hi /sa ma ya ma nu sma ra /b+h+ra ma b+h+ra
34a6(rgyud): ma/b+h+ra ma ya /b+h+ra ma ya /hu lu hu lu/mA ba laM pa sarba kA ra n+me mi /ku ru ku ru/bi rtsi da ru pa d+ha tri ni/tiSh+Ta tiSh+Ta /hU~M hU~M/sarba bu d+hA bu dz+nyA te swA hA////sems can rjesu 'dzin byed pa'i //byang chub sems dpas bshad
34a7(rgyud): pa na /sa rnams ma lus kun nas ni/rnam pa drug tu rab tu g.yos//sems can kun la phan don dang //gdug pa'i sems can bzlog byed pa//bdag gi rigs pa rtag tu drag /rtag tu srung ba yongs su grags
34a8(rgyud): bsrung ba ltos 'gro de nyid kyi //'dzin pa'i mtshan mas legs par mtshan //snying rjes thugs ni rlan pa yis //byang chub sems dpa' de nas gsungs //byang chub sems dpa'i bdag nyid che //byis pa rnams la phan
34b1(rgyud): don du//gang las sems can spyod gsungs pa//'gro ba kun la rnam spyod ldan//phyag rgya dbyug pa'i srog shing ldan//bdag nyid chen pos sbyar nas ni//tshangs pa laswogs 'gugs byed na//mi rnams 'bras bu
34b2(rgyud): smos mi dgos//gzhon nu'i sems su ma lus pa'i//rtog pa 'di ni bsdus su nas su//srin po rnams su rnam grags pa'i//rig sngags 'di ni yang dag brtan//sems can rjes bzung 'dod pa yi//byang chub
34b3(rgyud): sems dpa' de ltar bshad//yi ge g.yung zhes bya ba'i snying po ni//rig sngags 'di ni yang bshad//sems can kun la phan don du//nye bar longaspyod 'gugs pa'i chog/phyag rgya dbyug pa'i srog shong ni//rnam
34b4(rgyud): las rnams thams cad pa'o//oM hU~M dza//rig sngags 'di ni mdor bsdus nas//mi rnams 'bras bu byed pa yin//na ma;sa man+ta bu d+ha n-i/sa man+ta to ti ta mu d+hI nAM//oM bi kra ta ka hU~M phaT swAhA//nye ba'i
34b5(rgyud): snying po'o 'di yang ni/phyag rgya gra ste yang dag ldan//de bzhin 'byung po 'gugs par byed//de bzhin bza' dang ma mor bcas//thams cad phyag rgyas btab pa//dkon mchog 'bras bu bcas
34b6(rgyud): par 'gyur//'byung po rnams ni rnam skrag byed//bdug pa bzhugs pa rnam par grol//'di ni bcom ldan 'das ma 'phags ma sgrol ma'i pho nya mo chen mo las thams cad pa la ral pa gcig ma zhes bya ba
34b7(rgyud): rjesu spyod ma//las thams cad pa ste//bzlas pa tsam kho nas las thams cad byed do//'byung po thams cad skrag par byed pa dang//'gugs par byed pa dang//dbang du byed pa dang /rig pa 'dzin pa dang ji ltar
34b8(rgyud): 'dod pa de thams cad rdzogs par byed do//na ma sa man+ta bud+ha nAM/a pra ti ha ta sha sa nA nAM/tad+ya thA/oM bra h+mA su pra d+h+ma pra h+mA/brtsa se shA n+taM ku ru swAhA//sngags 'di tshangs pa chen po ste/byang chub sems dpas
35a1(rgyud): @#//bshad pa yin//'byung po zhi ba rab tu thob//skad cig nyid la gsil bar 'gyur phyag rgya pad mas yang dag ldan //myur du dge legs thob par 'gyur//mngon spyod kyi ni las rnams las//srid srung gi
35a2(rgyud): ni rigs byed 'dod//'di ni mdor bsdus brjod pa ste//go cha 'di ni mdor bsdus pa'o//na maHsarba bud d+hA naM/a pra ti ha ta shA sa nA nAM/tad+ya thA /oM ga ru Da ba ha na tsa prA ni tsa dur b+hu dzaHhU~M hU~M/sa ma ya ma nu smA ra /
35a3(rgyud): bo d+hi sa two a dz+nyA ba ya ti swAhA////byang chub sems dpa' bka' stsal pas//zhi ba'i don ni rgyu ru byed//'byung po rnams ni rnam 'jig byed//khyab 'jug kyis gzugs kyi lus can yin//phyag rgya'i 'khor la yang dag
35a4(rgyud): ldan//brtan pa'i don ni myur du byed//gang 'di khyab 'jug rgyud duni//chos ga rgyas par bstan pa de//sems can kun gyi sgrub bya de//byang chub sems dpa'as bshad pa yin//na maH
35a5(rgyud): sa man+ta nu hAM nAM/a pra ti ha ta sha sa nA nAM/tad+ya thA/mahA mahA+e/sarba b+hu tA d+hi ba ti/br-i Shi b+ha d+hA dza pra la ba dza tra ma ku Ta d+hA ri NI sa ta b+ha sma d+hA sa ri ta /mu d+hi ba oM phaT phaT /b+ho d+hi sa d+ha a dz+nyA ba ya ti
35a6(rgyud): swAhA//sems can rnams la phan 'dod pas//sngags 'di dag gis rab tu brjod//mdung gi phyag rgya mnyam par ldan //'byung po thams cad rnam 'jig pa //'di 'dra sngar bstan cho ga 'di//sems
35a7(rgyud): can sa stengs gnas pa la //ston ni zhi ba'i rgyud du bstan //yon tan sna tshogs rgya che ba//zhi ba'i rgyun gyis kyang brjod do//na maHsa man+ta bud+ha nAM//a pra ti ha ta shA swa na n-i/tad+ya thA/oM shA ku na pi ta ha pak+Sha
35a8(rgyud): Na sha ka kha kha khA hi khA hi/sa ma ya ma nu sma ra /hU~M tiSh+Tha/b+ho d+hi sa tA A /dz+nyA pa ti swAhA//sngags 'di brtson 'grus chen po ste//nam mkha' lding zhes rnam par grogs /gdul dka' 'dul ba rnams
35b1(rgyud): kyi mchog/gdengs gcan gyi dug rnams 'joms//phyag rgya de dang yang dag ldan//phung mi khrol mi zad bcas pa 'joms//brtan dang g.yo ba'i dug rnams ni//gso bar byed pa the tsom med//gdul dka' 
35b2(rgyud): bya'i sems can la rtse ba//byang chub sems dpa'i yang dag dka'//nam mkha' lding gi gzugs lding brjod//'dab chags rgyal po 'od chen ldan//ji srid nam mkha' lding rgyud du//bstan pa'i cho ga rgyas de 
35b3(rgyud): rnams//sems can kun la phan pa'i rgyur//bdag gis kyang ni brjod pa yin//mkha' lding mngon par brjod rgyud du//khro bo'i rgyal po ma bstan na//gdengs can dug ni gzhom pa'i phyir//'dab chags gzugs
35b4(rgyud): kyis rnam par rgyu//ji srid 'jig rten pa'i sngags//de rnams cho ga dper brjod pa//gdul bya'i sems can don du ni//de lta de ltar rnam par spyod//gang yang de bzhin gshegs sngags ba
35b5(rgyud): dang //rdo rje pa de ma'i rigs dag kyang de rnams cho ga rab 'byam 'dir//snga ma kho nar sngon gsungs yin//ji ltar ma ma rnam mang po//byis pa 'bad pas bsrung byed pa//de bzhin byis mtha' blo can la
35b6(rgyud): nga ni rig sngags gzugs kyi rgyu//stobs bcu ldan pas sngon gsungs pa//da lta nga'i bstan pa yin//sngags rgyud don ni ma lus pa//rtogs pas rab gsungs 'od chen po//rgyal ba mchog gis gang gsungs
35b7(rgyud): dang //stobs bcu pa'i sras kyis gsungs//rmad byung bsam mi khyab gzugs can//spyan ras gzigs kyis gsungs pa'o//de nas yang byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po//spyan ras gzigs
35b8(rgyud): dbang phyug thams cad dang ldan pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams der 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor chen po de la kun du gzigs nas//dam tshig thams cad yang dag par skul bar byed pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms
36a1(rgyud): @#//par zhugs so//gang du ting nge 'dzin de la gnas pas sems can ma lus pa'i sgrub pa'i spyod pa'i yid//sems can thams cad la rab tu gnas par gyur to//byang chub sems dpa' chen po snyoms
36a2(rgyud): par zhugs ma thag tu thams cad dang ldan pa'i 'khor chen po gnas par//rnam par bkra ba'i phyag na rin po che'i snying po //brgyan bkod pa'i dkyil 'khor bsam gyis mi khyab cing rmad du byung ba'i rnam par 'phrul
36a3(rgyud): pa//rang sangs rgyas thams cad dang/'phags pa nyan thos dang/spyod pa la zhugs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams dang/sa bcu dang ldan pa sa bcu'i dbang phyug la gnas pa rnams kyis kyang /dkyil 'khor de 
36a4(rgyud): bri ba 'am 'brir 'jug par mi nus pa//so so'i skye bor gyur ba'i sems can rnams kyis lta smos kyang ci dgos//'phags pa'i dkyil 'khor bzang po de'i dam tshig sgrub pa'i gnas skabs la
36a5(rgyud): gnas pa/byang chub sems dpa' chen pas gzigs nas//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das thams cad dang/rang sangs rgyas thams caddang/'phags pa nyan thos thams cad dang/byang chub sems
36a6(rgyud): dpa' sa bcu la rab tu gnas pa/rgyal tshab du mngon par dbang skur zhing//dam tshig thob pa thams cad dang/spyod pa la zhugs ba rnams zag pa dang bcas pa dang/zag pa med pa'i sems can thams cad /spyan ras 
36a7(rgyud): gzigs dbang phyug gis byin gyis brlabs kyis//sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i rgyu 'thun pa dang/ting nge 'dzin gyi khyad par gyis thugs la sa byung ba'i dkyil 'khor du rab
36a8(rgyud): tu zhugs pa/bdag nyid kyis yang dag par shes par gyur to//so so'i skye bo thams cad kyis de yid kyis dmigs par mi nus na bri bi 'am /'brir 'jug pa lta smos kyang ci dgos//de nas yang byang chub sems dpa'
36b1(rgyud): sems dpa' chen po spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gi dkyil 'khor chen po'i tshig la rjes su rab tu zhugs pa'i sems can de rnams la smras pa/grogs po dag nyon cig 'di ni de bzhin gshegs pa rnams
36b2(rgyud): dang/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis kyang 'da' bar mi bya ba'i dam tshig yin na/sems can rnams dang/'phags pa gzhan rnams kyis lta smos kyang ci dgos/de nas yang sems dpa' chen po phyag na rdo rje
36b3(rgyud): gsang ba'i bdag po la smras pa/kye rgyal ba'i sras /mi 'di las 'das pa'i dam tshig yid na/sems can rnams dad/'phags pa gzhan gyis lta smos kyang ci dgos //las byung ba ni nges par bstan to//mi 
36b4(rgyud): rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa rnam yongasu mya ngan las 'das pa na yang gang du sems can rnams yang dag par rjesu zhugs nas//'jig rten las 'das pa'i rig pa dang/sngags kyi dngos grub thams
36b5(rgyud): cad grub par 'gyur ba bshad par bya'o//de nas yang dag gsang ba'i bdag po phyag na rdo rjes byang chub sems dpa' chen po spyan ras gzig dbang phyug la smras pa/kye rgyal ba'isras gang da lta dus la bab
36b6(rgyud): par shes na gsungs shig gsungs shig/shAkya seng ge 'jig rten mgon//yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa'i//mi rnams mchog gi sangs rgyas zhing/sems can steng khyod dkyil 'khor//mthong ba tsam gyis 'jig
36b7(rgyud): rten na//sngags kyi dngos grub rab tu ste//mi shes cho ga nyams pa 'am//dam tshig bdag por ma gyur na//tshangs pa bdag nyid chen pos kyang//rig sngags dngos grub mi 'gyur ro//sngags la mngon par mi brtson
36b8(rgyud): dang/dam tshig bstan pa ma mthong na//rnam pa du mar 'bar byas kyang//rig pa'i dngos grub mi 'gyur ro //dam tshig sbyor ba nyams pa ni//brgya byin gyis kyang rab 'bad las//rig pa'i dngos grub mi'agyur na/
37a1(rgyud): @#//sa stengs mi rnams smos ci dgos//dam tshig stan chos ci ste shes//spyod pa'i las (rnams)grub pa de//'jig rten pa dang 'phags pa'i sngags//klags pa tsam gyis grub par 'gyur//byang chub sems dpa'i
37a2(rgyud): dkyil 'khor du//rab zhugs las rnams thams cad byed//byang chub sems dpa'i stan pa la//de'i rigs pa'i dngos grub rtag//de nas gsang ba'i bdag po phyag na rdo rjes//sems dpa' chen po la
37a3(rgyud): gsol btab par gyur to//kyai sems dpa' chen po//sems can rnams kyi don gyi phyir//dkyil 'khor gyi cho ga mdor bsdus pas gsungs shig ces so//gsang ba'i bdag pos de skad ces smras
37a4(rgyud): pa dang//byang chub sems dpa' chen po 'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gis //sems can gyi don gyi phyir dkyil 'khor cho ga gsungs so//dang po re shig cho 
37a5(rgyud): 'phrul gyi zla ba phyed dam /nam pa can nam/sa ga can gyi zla ba'i dkar po'i phyogs la /rab tu sngags pa'i nyi ma dang/bza' bzang po ltas te /rgyu skar dge ba dang /yang dag par
37a6(rgyud): ldan pa'i dkar po'i phyogs kyi chos gcig dang/zla ba nya ba 'am dus gzhan la dbyar gyi zla ba spengs /snga dro sa rab tu gnas par bya'o//grong khyer chen po la sten pa 'am //gang du dkyil
37a7(rgyud): 'khor gyi slob dpon rang nyid gnas par gyur te/rgya mtsho 'chu ba 'am /chu klung la rten pa 'am/rgya mtsho'i ngogs dang nye bar grong khyer chen po'i byang shar phyogs kyi char /ha cang yang mi nye
37a8(rgyud): bar dkyil 'khor gyi slob dpon song nas/nyi ma bdun n-i zla ba phyed du mtha' gcig tu snyung ba 'am /nye bar byas nas so sor gnas par bya'o//gang gnas de rnams su yang gtsang ba'i
37b1(rgyud): phyogs rdo ba dang/gyo mo dang /thal ba dang/sol ba dang /phub ma dang/thod pa dang/rus pa gnas pa dang bral ba //shin tu sbyang bar byas pa'i sa phyogs su//srog chags med pa'i chu la/ba byung lnga dang
37b2(rgyud): bsres pa'i tsan dan dang ga bur dang/gur gum gyi chus khro bo'i rgyal po sgrol mas non pa stong phrag brgyad kyis/mngon par sngags pa des bcom ldan 'das ma'i phyag rgya chen mo dang /yang 
37b3(rgyud): dag par ldan pas sa phyogs de la /phyogs bzhi rnams su mngon par gtor zhing/steng dang 'og dang /thad ka dang/phyogs bral rnams su yang kun nas gtor bar bya'o//de nas phyogs der 
37b4(rgyud): kun nas gru bzhir/khru bcu drug gam/khru bcu gnyis sam/khru brgyad bya ste/de la chen po ni khru bcu drug go//bar ma ni khru bcu gnyis so//chung ngu ni khru brgyad do/dkyil 'khor rnam pa gsum po 
37b5(rgyud): 'di /thams cad gzigs pas gsungs pa yin//rgyal srid 'dod phyir chen po de//bar ma long spyod 'phel bar byed//chung ngu bsngags kyi dam tshig ni//las rnams kun byed zhi ba yin
37b6(rgyud): de la yid la 'dod pa gang yang rung ba'i dkyil 'khor bri bar bya'o//de la sa'i phyogs der khru gnyis tsam rkos la/der rdo ba dang /sol ba dang /thal ba dang/rus pa dang/skrag la sogs pa dang /srog 
37b7(rgyud): chags kyi rigs rnam pa sna tshogs pa gal te mthong na sa'i phyogs gzhan du bskor bar bya'o//gnod pa med pa dang/nye bar 'tshe ba med pa der 'gyur ro//ra'i rtse mo dang chu klung gi dogs dang /brgya mtshor
37b8(rgyud): 'bab pa'i chu klung chen po'i chu ngogs rnams su yang gang rab tu 'bad pa chen pos shin tu so sor rtags te/shin tu gtsang shing srogs chags med par byas nas bra bar bya'o//sa'i phyogs der slar
38a1(rgyud): @#//yang srog chags med pa'i chu la /ba'i rnam lnga bsres pas chu klung gi 'gram gyis gtsang ma 'am/grogs mkhar gyi sa gang du srog chags med pa'i sa des gang bar bya'o//bkang nas kyang leg+sa
38a2(rgyud): par legs par gdungs te kun nas mnyam par byas la /ji ltar 'dod pa'i dkyil 'khor rnam pa gsum bya'o//seng ldeng gi phung bu bzhi la khro bo'i rgyal po nyid kyis lan bdun mngon par bsngags
38a3(rgyud): par byas nas /phyogs bzhi rnams su gdab par bya'o//mtshon sna lnga'i skud pa la khro bo'i snying pos lan bdun mngon par bsdags par byas nas /kun nas dkyil 'khor gru bzhi pa'i rnam pas bkri par
38a4(rgyud): bya'o//de bzhin du dbus kyi gnas su nang du yang zur bzhi'i rnam par bya'o//dbus kyi gnas na gnas pa'i dkyil 'khor gyi slob dpon gyis rtsa ba'i rig pa 'am/rtsa ba'i snying po
38a5(rgyud): stong phrag brgyad bzlas par bya'o//phyag rgya chen po dang rtsa ba'i sngags kyis gtsug tor bcings te/khro bo'i rtsa ba'i sngags kyis grogs bsrungs nas /bdag nyid bsrung ba byas ste/bzlas
38a6(rgyud): pa byed bzhin du phyi rol du phyung ste/dkyil da 'khor la g.yas su bskor ba byas nas/kha shar du phyogs te ku sha'i khres la nye bar 'dug nas sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad yid la byas
38a7(rgyud): te/dkyil 'khor de kun nas gru bzhi'i rnam par bri bar bya'o//de nyid kyi phyi rol du ba rnam zhag gcig snyung bar gnas pas grogs bzang po dang/rab tu gnas par bya'o//de la 'dir dkyil 'khor gyi slob
38a8(rgyud): dpon gyis sngon spyad pa rnams byas te/rang gi rig pa dang sngags dang rgyud la mkhas pa'i mkhan pos sems can thams cad kyi don gyi phyir /theg pa chen po la lhag par mos pas zhag gcig snyung bar
38b1(rgyud): gnas pas grogs bzang po dang/yang dag par ldan pa stobs rnam pa sna tshogs pa'i bya ba mthong bas /mtshon sna tshogs lnga'i phye ma bzhi po 'bar bas legs par spyad pa byas nas /rtsa ba'i
38b2(rgyud): rig pa'i sngags dang snying pos mngon par sngags par phye ma dkyil 'khor dbus su gdab par bya'o//pha rol du yang rgyal mtshan dang ba dan sgreng ba dang/rta babs bzhis brgyan pa dang /chu shing gi stong po
38b3(rgyud): 'bras bu'i tshogs kyis g.yur za zhing//kun nas 'bras bu'i tshogs gyis rab tu 'phyung bzhin pa dang/rnga bo che dang /rdza lnga dang/dung gi sgra nges par sgrogs pa'i sa'i phyogs su bsgrags par bya'o//
38b4(rgyud): ngos bzhir rjes su 'thun pa'i bar du bsngags pa'i sgra chos mnyan par bya'o//phyogs bzhi rnams su theg pa chen po'i mdo sde'i gleg bam klag par bya'o//'di lta ste /bcom ldan 'das 
38b5(rgyud): shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin ma lho phyogs su klag par bya'o//'phags pa zla ba'i sgron ma'i ting nge 'dzin nub phyogs su klag par bya'o//'phags pa stong po bkod pa byang phyogs su'o//
38b6(rgyud): 'phags pa gser 'od dam pa shar phyogasu'o//de ltar glegs bam klags pa po med na/mdo sde bzhi po lhag par rtogs pa'i gang zag chos smra ba po la/chos nyan pa'i phyir gsol ba btab par bya'o//
38b7(rgyud): de nas dkyil 'khor gyi slob dpon langs nas/tsan dan dang ga pur dang/gur gum pa rnam par bsres pa'i me tog dkar po dri zhim pa la rtsa ba'i rig pa bzlas te/dkyil 'khor de thams cad du gcal du
38b8(rgyud): bkram par bya'o//mngon par bkram nas phyi rol du phyung ste/zhag bdun gyi bar du 'dod pa'i kha zas sten pa/gnyis sam gsum byang chub kyi sems bskyed pa/gso sbyong nye bar gnas pa /sten pa la gnas
39a1(rgyud): @#//pa'i ri mo mkhan mkhas pa de grogs sam /slob ma des khas blangs pa rab tu bzhes par bya'o//gang rtsa ba'i rig pa nyid kyis gtsug tor bcing bar byas nas /de nas gser dang dngul dang /rin po 
39a2(rgyud): che rnam pa sna tshogs pa'i go 'phang rnam par bkra shing /'bar la mdzes pa'i zangs kyi phye ma zhib mo so sor bzung nas/long spyod chen po dang ldan zhing /chos dang ldan pa'i rgyal po rnams 'bri ru bzhug par bya'o/
39a3(rgyud): dkyil 'khor mthong ba nyid las /nges par byang chub la gzhol ba yin na/sngags sgrub par byed pa lta ci smos/shAkya seng ge gas mchog myang 'das nas //sems can bsod nams chung rnams la /'di lta'i longaspyod
39a4(rgyud): ga la yod //cho ga 'di ni stan par bya/byang chub sems dpa' 'od chen ldan //skye bo bkren pa gzigs nas ni//nyung zhing mdor bsdus dkyil 'khor ni//mdor bsdus nas ni bstan pa yin//
39a5(rgyud): 'bras bu lu dang /thug po che'i phye ma shin du zhib mo mtshon sna lnga ba 'bar bas /dkar po dang /ser po dang /dmar po dang /ljang kus kha dog du kha sgyur la sngar bzhag pa'i phye ma dkyil 'khor
39a6(rgyud): gyi slob dpon gyis blangs nas bcom ldan 'das ma'i phyag rgya chen po bcings te za rtsa ba'i rig pa zlas pas phye ma la phyag rgyas btab par bya'o//gzhan zhi ba grub pa po'i slob dpon gyis dkyil 'khor gyi phyi
39a7(rgyud): rol lho shar gyi phyogs las kyi cho ga me thab bya'o//khru gnyis kyi tshad la/dpangs khru gang tsam gyi tshad la /rlan dang yug tu pad ma'i ze ba rnam pa /phyi rol pad ma'i rnam par shing pa la yam shing rnams kyi 
39a8(rgyud): me rab tu sbar te/shing dpal 'bras gyi yam shing mtho gang tsam gyis tshad la/rlan dang bcas pa zho dang sbrang rtsi dang mar la rtags pa rnams kyis/rtsa ba'i snying pos bsam /phyag rgya dang rtsa ba'i rig sngags kyis
39b1(rgyud): me lha spyan drang bar bya'o//spyan grangs nas kyang sngar brjod pa'i yi ge gcig pa nyid kyi rig pa 'am /rtsa ba'i rig pa'i snying pos slar yang /brgya rtsa brgyad sbyin bsreg bya'o//de nas dkyil 'khor gyi slob dpon gyis
39b2(rgyud): gtsug tor bcing zhing yod byad tshogs par byas nas/bdag nyid dang ri mo mkhan ches mkhas pa 'am/slob ma byed du bzhug go/de nas dkyil 'khor gyi slobadapon gyis sangs rgyas dang /byang chub sems dpa' rnams

55b3(rgyud): yul dbus kyi skyes bu tshad dang ldan pa'i khru gcig te/'di ni bde bar gshegs pa'i mtho bo zhes sgrags te/tshad 'dis tshad kun bshad do//'di ni phur ba'i dngos grub du skyes bu mchog gis gsungs
55b4(rgyud): pa yin/thub pa mi snang 'gyur 'og tu/rgyal srid 'dod la 'bring po ste/longs spyod chen po don gnyer ba'i//skyes bu lha dang lha min dang//gdengs can tha ma grub par bshad//'bring la dngos
55b5(rgyud): grub 'bring po'o//ras 'bring po ni rtag par ru//sems can kun gyi las rnams grub//'phren tshegs las rnams sgrub bya la//ras tha ma ni rtag pa ru //las rnams thams cad sgrub par
55b6(rgyud): byed//rdzas rnams kun la rtag par ni//las rnams thams cad sgrub par byed//ras ris gsum gyis nges sten pa'i//dngos grub dpal don gnyer ba'i mi//brgya byin bde sogs
55b7(rgyud): bdag po 'ang//cho ga nyams na mi 'grub te//cho ga yang dag ldan 'gyur na//mi'i skye bo phal pa la 'ang//'bad pa yis ni las rnams kun//myur ba nyid du 'grub par 'gyur//rgyal dang
55b8(rgyud): rgyal mchog sras rnams kyis //sbyor 'di dag yang dag bshad//sems can bkren pa 'gon med pa//sdug bsngal gyis gdungs kun gyi dpal//byang chub lam du gang 'di ni//de nyid gzigs pa
56a1(rgyud): @#//rnams ba kyis stan//chos 'di byang chub rgyu yin par//sngags kyi lam gyis bstan pa yin//dkyil 'khor yang dag bstan pa gang//'jig rten dkyil 'khor thams cad dang//'jig 'das
56a2(rgyud): pa 'ang 'grub 'gyur te//'bad pa med par sngags rnams 'grub//gang zhig byang chub rgyur 'dod pa//de la rtag du dngos grub 'gyur//rtag tu 'gro la mi phan pa//gzhan la dngos grub med par gsungs//byang chub ched
56a3(rgyud): du 'jug kun la//ras ris thams cad 'grub par gsungs//spyan ras gzigs kyi dbang phyugi//dam tshig 'di ni khyad par du//myur bya ba'i don sgrub phyir//'dir ni mtha' dag thob par ru//bcom ldan
56a4(rgyud): 'das kyis gsungs pa yin//de nas rigs|gyis gzo bo yis//'bad pas ras ni btag par bya//nyin zhag lnga dang brgyad rnams dang//bcu drug gnyis dang bzhi dag dang//nyin
56a5(rgyud): mtshan nyid kyis myur bar ru//ras ni 'thag pa rdzogs pa dang//bza' bo mchog grub pa ni//nyin mtshan gyi ni dge ba yin//gtsang sgra'i spyod pa phun sum tshogs//bzo bos rtag tu byin
56a6(rgyud): brlabs pa//thag ring gnasu song nas ni//bshang dang lci ba yongasu der//'ongs pa'i gos dang bcas bkrus nas//gzhan pa'i gso gzhan 'go bya zhing//gos dkar 'chang zhing phreng bar ldan//yang dang
56a7(rgyud): yang du nye reg bya//lus la tsan dan dkar pos byug/gzo bo'i lag pa gtsang bar bya//rtag tu 'jam zhing legs sbyangs pas//slar yang 'bad pas brtag par bya//de lasogs pa'i rib sbyor ni
56a8(rgyud): rgyal ba bzhin gyis gsungs pa yin//'bad par rnam spyad dang tshul gyis//ras ni ma lus rtags par bya//ras kyi thags ni rdzogs pa dang//sngar gyi las kyis legs sbrul pa//de rnams tshad ldan
56b1(rgyud): ma nyams pa/bya'o bzang po nyin zhag la//rgyud dang rgyud du mnyam par 'jug//dkar po'i phyogs kyis mdzes pa la//ras ni yongasu gsal byas nas//kha ra tshar mdud na pa'i mtha' mdzes la//'od
56b2(rgyud): ma'i shing ni khru bzhi la//ras ni blangs nas de nas 'gro//nor rgya cher 'god pa yis//bzo bo bde legs byas nas ni//rtul shugs bzang po legs spyad pa'i//sdigs pa ji ltar 'dod song nas
56b3(rgyud): dri zhim me tog gis mchod dang//gtsang ma'i phyogasu rnam par dgod//'di nyid kyi ni sngags gyis ni//srung ba cho ga rab tu bya//gang gis r-is bal sngon du ni//slar yang rnam mang legs sbyangs pa//de nyid kyis
56b4(rgyud): ni bdag dang//ras nyid srung bar bya'o//byang chub seMda' dpa'o bo che//sngags bzlas pa yis gsungs pa yin//sargyas rnaMs ni mad pos dang//ngas kyang de ni slar yang ste//de nyid gsang sngags
56b5(rgyud): thams cad kyis//rnam par spyod pa'i sngags kyi gzugs//brtson 'grus chen po gzi brjid che//sngags kyi don du grub pa po//sna tshogs skyob pa'i rgyu rnams ni//rnam pa sna tshogs 'byung
56b6(rgyud): bar 'gyur//'dzam gling gnas pa'i seMn rnaMs//sems kyi spyod pa kun tu rmongs//dad pa med cing phyin ci log/log pa'i kun spyod la rab bskam//sngags rnams sgrub par mi byed pa'i//de nyid phyir na
56b7(rgyud): slar gsum pa//'khor ba mun pa'i btson rar 'khyim//gang yang rtag tu yid dag cing//dad ldan ltad mo bkra shis dang//sngags rnams kun la rtag du spro//bdag tu 'dzin cing 'chad pa dang//spro ba che
56b8(rgyud): zhing gzi brjid che//rgyal pas gsungs pa'i sngags rnaMs gang//'bad pa med par yang dag grub//dad pa med pa'i skye bo la//dkar po'i chos rnams mi skye ste//sa bon tsha sgor btab pa la//myu gu med cing
57a1(rgyud): @#//'bras med bzhin//dad pa rtag tu chos kyi rtsar//don kun gzigs pas gsungs pa yin//chos don dang tshul can de rnams//sngags kyi dngos grub rtag tu brjod//de nas sgrub pas bzo 
57a2(rgyud): bo ni//ri mo mkhan nam bdag kyang rung //ri mo mkhas pas ma 'dres pa'i//mtshon rtsi kha dog bsal ba dang //kha dog can gyi mtshon bdud rtsi nas//ji ltar tha ga ba ni//sngar gyi cho ga de nyid kyis//mtshan nyid yang dag
57a3(rgyud): ldan pa yis//ri mo mkhan la gong bzhin du//rgyas pa ru ni bya ba'o//ji ltar sngar gyi tha ga pa'i cho ga de nyid kyis ni de//ras la ri mo bri bar bya//yang na rang gis ri mor bri//ga bur gur gum tsan dan las//bsog+sa
57a4(rgyud): pas mtshon la bgo bar bya//bdug pa sregs shing sngags nyid kyis//lan gang rgya rtsa brgyad bzlas nas//na ge sar pun na ga//ba ku la dang tsam pa ka/bA ra shi ka d+ha nu kA/mA la ti yi me tog swo//ras
57a5(rgyud): de la ni mngon par gtor//shar du mngon phyogs ku sha'i//khres la nyer 'dug dang ba'i blos//sangs rgyas byang chub sems ka kun/sems par gyur pas 'di phra mo//lag pas blangs nas ma 'khrugs
57a6(rgyud): pa'i//sems kyis ras la ri mo bri//dang po she shig de bzhin gshegs pa shA kya thub pa bri bar bya ste/rnam pa thaMd kyi mchog dang ldan pa//skyes bu chen po'i mtshan sum bcu rtsa gnyis kyis mtshan pa
57a7(rgyud): dpe byad bzang po brgyad bcus nye bar mdzes pa'i sku//rin po che'i phad ma la zhugs pa kun nas 'od 'bar ba//kunas 'od 'dom gang gis nye bar mdzes pa'i sku//chos ston par mdzad bzhin
57a8(rgyud): pas rab tu dang ba'i sku//rnam pa thaMd kyi mchog dngaldan pa//dbus na gnas pa'i pad ma'i sdong po bye du r+ya'i sdong po can//'og na klu'i rgyal po sra gdan chen po pad ma'i sdong bu de la 'dzin
57b1(rgyud): bzhin pas//de bzhin gshegs pa la lta zhing lag pa g.yas pas phyag 'tshal zhing //dkar po brgyan thaMd kyis rnam par brgyan pa//lus byed mi'i rnam pa can gyi dga'o dang /nye dga' bri bar bya'o//
57b2(rgyud): pad ma'i mtsho de'i khor ra khor yug du pad ma'i 'dab ma dang /me tog cung zad kha bye ba dang /rnam par kha bye ba dang /chu la skyes pa'i bya dang /nya lasogs pas mngon par khyab pa ma lus par bkod
57b3(rgyud): pa;shin du sras pa mdzes pa'i rnaM par mngon par bri bar bya'o//gang bcoMs kyi rtsa ba'i pad ma'i sdong bu dang /'dab ma de nyid la rnaM par 'phros pa'i pad ma'i metog du rnams riM gyis mtho ba'o/g.yon gyi bzhogasu
57b4(rgyud): pad ma'i me tog brgyade/de rnaMs la yang pad ma rnaMs zhugs pa'i byang chub seMda' chen po rgyad kyi sku mngon par bri bar bya'odang po re shig 'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug pad ma'i ze ba ltar cung zud kar bI
57b5(rgyud): gur gum dang /gser gyi mdog can 'od phug tu med pa'i spyod pa na gyis dbu la rnaM par rgyan pa /phyag na ud pa la sngon po 'dzin pa/g.yas kyi phyag gis de bzhin gash+ye pa la phyag 'tshal bar byed bzhin pa/
57b6(rgyud): sku mdzes pa de bzhin gash+ye pa la lta ba zhi ba'i rnam pa bzhin/cung zad 'dzum pa kunas 'bar bas 'khrigs pa'i dkyi 'khor gyi dbusu gnas pa'o//pad ma gzhan la 'phags pa 'od gzhonu de bzhin du mngon p+Ra
57b7(rgyud): bri bar bya'o//gsum pa la nor bzangaso//bzhi pa la sgrib pa thabs cad rnaM par sel ba'o//lnga pa la namakha' mdzod//drug pa la sa'i snying po'o//bdun pa la 'joM dpalo//rgyad pa la spyan gzig+sa te/'di dag thaMd
57b8(rgyud): gzhonu'i cha byad can/gzhonu rgyan gyis mngon par rgyan pa mngon p+Ra bri bar bya'o//bac+doMs kyi g.yas kyi zhogasu byang chub seMda' chen po rgyad la/byams pa ma rtog+sa pa rgyan thaMd kyis rnam p+Ra rgyan pa ste/bcom
58a1(rgyud): @#//ldan 'das kyi nye logasu 'phags pa byams pa/tshangs par spyod pa'i cha byad 'dzin pa/dbu la ral pa'i cod pan bcings nas/gser mdog dur smig dmar po gsol pa/phyag g.yas pa dar la dmar ba'i stod
58a2(rgyud): g.yogs byas pa/chom bu gsum gyis mtshan ma byas pa/gzugs mdzes pa/dbyug pa dang ril ba spyi blugs g.yog gyi phyag du bkrod pa/kri sh+tsa swa ra'i pags pa phrag pa g.yon du bgos pa/phyag g.yas pa grang phreng bzung nas
58a3(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa la phyag 'tshal bar byed bzhin pa/der gtod pa'i lta ba bsam rtan la dmigs pa'i sems kyi spyod ba cano//pad ma gnyis la 'phags pa kun tu bzang po spyi yang ku ltar sngo bsangs kyi mdog/sku rgyan thaMd
58a4(rgyud): kyis brgyan pa/phyag g.yon pas yid bzhin gyi nor bu rin po che snams pa/phyagayas pas dpal 'bras snaMs nas mchog sbyin pa/mdzes pa'i gzugs can de bzhin du mngon par bri bar bya'o//gsu pa la 'phags pa spyan ras

58a6(rgyud): bcu pa/ral pa 'i nang du nye bar bzhugs pa/gzugs mdzes pa/phyag g.yon na pad ma snams pa/phyag g.yas mchog sbyin pa//bsam rtan la dmigs par gyur pa'i sems kyi spyod pa can/sku kunas gsal bar
58a7(rgyud): gyur ba'o//bzhi pa la 'phags pa phyag na da rdo rje g.yon pas rdo rje snams pa/phyag g.yas pa nye bar 'gog pa 'bras bu dang bcas pa snams nas/mchog sbyin pa/gser mdog rgyan thams cad kyis rnam par rgyan pa/
58a8(rgyud): mdzes pa'i gzugs can zhi ba'i lta ba dang ldan pa do zhal dang/do zhal phyed pas lus nye bar bkris pa/mu tigi do zhal gyi mchod phyir thogs byas pa/gcod pan ni rin po che 'bar bas rnaM par 'phro ba/dar sra ba
58b1(rgyud): mo'i sham thabs dang/dar dkar po'i bla gos byas pa'o//'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug dang /kun du bzang po'i sham thabs dang/bla gos kyang de bzhino//lus kyi rnam pa ni sngar stan pa ji lta ba bzhin no
58b2(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa'i pad ma lnga pa la/'phags pa bla gos chen po/drug pa la zhi ba'i blo gos /bdun pa la rnam par snang mdzad kyi snying pa'o//brgyad pa la ngan song spong ba zhes bya ba'i byang chub sems dpa' 'di rnams
58b3(rgyud): phyag na 'bras bu dang/gleg bam snams pa/rnam pa kun tu mdzes pa dar la'i stod g.yogs can/brgyan thams cad kyis rnam par brgyan pa/g.yo ba dang ldan pa'i gos snams pa rnams mngon par bri bar bya'o//de rnams
58b4(rgyud): kyi yang steng du rang sangs rgyas rgyad mngon par bri bar bya ste/dge slong gi cha byad 'chang ba/skyes bu chen po'i mtshan dang ldan pa'i sku du smig dmar po gsol ba/dkyil mo dkyung gis nye bar 'dug pa rin po che'i rdo leb

58b6(rgyud): pa'i me tog rnams kyis ras la kun nas bcal du bkram bzhin pas/mngon par bri'o/bcom ldan 'das shA kya thub pa'i g.yon gyi g.yo zhogasu /'phags pa 'jam dpal gang yin pa'i steng du rin po che
58b7(rgyud): du mas spras pa'i zhal mid shin du ring po'i zhal med khang gi dkyil 'khor /brag ri'i rgyal pos nye bar mdzes pa rin po che'i do leb kyis bkab pa/ri bo'i rnam pa lta bur mngon par bri bar bya'o//der
58b8(rgyud): gnas pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das brgyad mngon par bri bar bya la 'di lta ste/rin po che btsug gtor can/rin po che bA+ai du r+ya'i 'od rnam par 'phro ba/kun nas 'od 'phro ba//
59a1(rgyud): @#//kun nas 'od 'dom gang ba/pad ma rA ga dang /in+t+ra ni la dang /ma ra ka da la swogs pa dang /bA+ai du r+ya dang /rdo rje'i snying po la sogs pa nor bu rin po che chen po khyad par can rnams kyis kun nas rab tu 'bar bzhin pa/cung zad nyi ma 'dra 
59a2(rgyud): ka'i mdog 'dra ba/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku chos gos ser pos stod g.yogs babs pa/dkyil dkrungs kyis zhugs pa/chos ston par mdzad bzhin pa/shams thabs ser po banabs pa/sku la skyes bu chen po'i mtshan gyi go che ba'o/
59a3(rgyud): pa/dpe byad bzang po brgyad bcus nye bar mdzes pa'i sku/rab tu zhi ba'i gzigs stangs can/rnam pa thams cad kyi mchog dang ldan pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa rin chen gtsug tor can mngon par bri'o//gnyis pa de bzhin gshegs pa me tog 
59a4(rgyud): yang dag par skyes pa'i rgyal po'i dbang po gser gyi mdog du mngon par bri bar bya'o//na ga ge sar ches bzang ba dang /ba gu la la swogs pa'i me tog rnams kyis mngon par gtor la mngon par bri bar bya'o//'phags pa'i dzam dpal la mngon

59a6(rgyud): pa'o/bzhi pa la de bzhin gshegs pa spyan zangs mngon par bri bar bya'o/ /lnga pa la bzod par dka' ba'o//drug pa la rnam par snang mdzad rgyal po'o//bdun pa la sman gyi bla bA+ai da r+ya'i 'od kyi rgyal po'o//brgyad pa la de bzhin gshegs par sdug 
59a7(rgyud): bsngal thams cad rab tu zhi bar byed pa'i dba'i po'i rgyal po mngon par bru+i bar bya ste/thams cad gser gyi kha dog can de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku'i rnam pa mi 'jigs pa sbyin par mdzad pa'o//de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi steng du y+nga 
59a8(rgyud): gnyis pa sku'i steng du brus gnyis ka nas sprin gyi go bar gnas pa'i gnas gtsang ma'i ris kyi lha'i bu gnyis kyi me tog gi char 'bebs par byed bzhin pa bri'o//bar snang la gnas nas/sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' dang 
59b1(rgyud): rang sangs rgyas dang/'phags pa nyan thos thams cad la phyag 'tshal bar byed bzhin pa/mngon par bri bar bya'o//rang sangs rgyas rnams kyi byang chub tu 'phags pa nyan thos chen po brgyad byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi dbus steng du
59b2(rgyud): mngon par bri bar bya'o//'di lta ste/gnas gtan sha ri'i bu dang /me'u 'gal gyi bu chen po dang/'od sus chen po dang/rab 'byor dang sgra gcan 'dzin dang/dga'o dang/bzang ldan dang/ka pi na zhes pa'o//rang sangs rgyas kyi
59b3(rgyud): sa rnams 'di lta ste/dri ngan can dang/tsan dan dang/nye ba'i 'chi ltas can dang/dkar po dang/si ga dang/dbang po dang/mu khyud dang mu khyud bzang po zhes pa'o//thams cad mchog tu shin du mdzes pa'i zhi ba'i cha lugs/zhi ba'i bdag nyid
59b4(rgyud): rab tu dul ba'i rnam pa'o//nyan thos chen po rnams thal mo sbyar bar byas nas/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pa'i nges par rtogs par byed pa'o//stong ngos su yang gnas gtsang ma'i lha dang/nye bar gnas pa'i lha'i bu
59b5(rgyud): gzhan gnyis dar gyi bla re kun nas mtshur ring shing/shin du mdzes pa bzung nas/sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' dang/rang sangs rgyas dang/'phags pa nyan thos thams cad kyi steng du 'dzin par byed bzhin pa/lha'i phreng
59b6(rgyud): ba dang/gos 'chang ba'i lha'i bu mngon par bri'o//bcom ldan 'das shAkya thubapa'i steng ngosu/dbu la me tog gi do shal dang/rin po che'i skud pa la pad ma ra ga dang/in+t+ra ni la la sogs pa rnams kyis rgyus pa'i rin po
59b7(rgyud): che'i skud pa'i tshogs de la/dar gyi bla re legs par bkod cing/khor yug du yang mu tig gi do shal rab tu 'chang ba mdzes par gyur pa mngon par bri bar bya'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasakyis pad ma'i gdon gyi 'og tu
59b8(rgyud): byang chub sems dpa' chen po dang nye bar/klu'i rgyal po nye dga' bo'i gzhogasu rin po che'i ri bo//pad ma'i mtsho las mngon par 'phags pa/rin po che'i nyu gu dang/phug dang/ri su la dang/byi ru'i 'khril shing
60a1(rgyud): @#//yongs bkris pa'i rin po che'i shing dang /drang srong chen po nye bar gnas shing sten pa'i ri bo de'i steng du/khro bo'i rgyal po sgrol mas non pa/drag po'i gzugs can/phyag g.yon pas zhags pa bzung nas/g.yas p+sa
60a2(rgyud): dbyug pa 'dzin pa/bzhin khro gnyer can/byang chub sems dpa' chen po lnga lta bzhin bka' nod par byed bzhin pa/gsus pa glo ba/sgra gyen du rjes pa/mig sman nag po dang /sprin dang mtshungs pa/ag tsom ser skya dang skra ring 
60a3(rgyud): zhing sen mo ring ba/mig dmar ba/mgrin pa'i phyogs sprul gyis brgyan pa/stag gi lpags pa'i sham thabs can/bgegs thams cad rnam par 'joms pa/ches mi zad pa chen po/khro bo'i rgyal po chen po kun nas 'bar ba/
60a4(rgyud): khro bo'i rgyal po sgrol mas nor pa rnam pa 'di nyid kho nas bri bar bya'o//de'i 'og tu ral pa can de dang de'i 'o//ri bo'i 'og tu rdo rje'i steng du nye bar 'dug pa/pus mo sa la 'dud pa'i lus can/lag pa g.yas par thogs pa/cha byad
60a5(rgyud): dang dbyibs dang 'jug pa dang spyod pa ji lta ba bzhin du byas pa 'jom dpal la la bar gyur pa yang dag par bri bar bya'o//bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pa'i 'og/g.yas kyi gzhogasu klu'i dbang po'i rgyal po dga'o dang /nye logasu
60a6(rgyud): pad ma'i 'tsho las mngon par 'phags pa'i rin po che ri'i dbang po'i rgyal po'i gzhan gsungs pa/de bzhin du gnas mngon par bri ste/khro bo'i rgyal po 'phags ma sgral mas non pa phan pa/lha'i metog gcal du bkram pa mngon
60a7(rgyud): par bri ste/ri de ni spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gi 'og tu bre'o//ri de ni shin tu 'phro ba pad ma rA ga'i rdo le ba de nyid kyi myu gu dang /bA+ai du ru'i rang bzhin gyi rtse mo'i rnam par bri bar bya'o//de la rten pa'i lha mo/spyan ras gzigs dbang phyag
60a8(rgyud): gi thugs rje 'phags ma sgrol ma lus brgyan thams cad kyis brgyan pa/de la dmar po'i stod g.yogs pa dang /dar la khro bo'i sham thabs can/bud med kyi brgyan thams cad kyis rnam par brgyan pa/phyag g.yon
60b1(rgyud): na ud pa la sngon po snams pa/gser mdog can ma ha cang mi skye ma/ha cang mi sbom/ha mi rgan pa/sems bsam gtan la gnas pa bka' nod cing 'dug pa/phyag g.yas pas mchod sbyin par 'dod pa/lus cung zad

87a1(rgyud): @#//de yis dngos grub rnyed mi rga'//dper ni khur ni grang ba'i rgyu//shing rta yun rings gnas don du//shing rta'i mig ni sku bar byed//de bzhin sngags pa sngags rig pas//zas ni gnas pa'i ched du spyad//

97b3(rgyud): yin//rgyad rtsa rgyad dam nyi shu lnga/lnga bcu 'di dag par yin//sngags pa mnyam par bzhag pa yang //'bras bu'i tshad rnams rgyu bar bya//srang rtsa rgyad pa nyid bya ba//phreng ba 'di ni thu bo yin 
97b4(rgyud): phreng ba rnams pa 'di bzhin no//sngags pas rtag tu rgya bar bya//de nas sgyur de sngags rnams ni//phye ba gsum 'am gnyis nyid dang //rgyal lugs las byung gdu bu dang 
97b5(rgyud): de bzhin gser dang dngul dag dang //de nas zangs byung gdu bus kyang //phreng ba kun nas rgyu bar bya//de nas mtha' ru yang mig bya//de la ris kyis dgod par bya//'bad pas
97b6(rgyud): 'dzes par bkri bar bya//sangs rgyas ma lus zhugs par ni//yongs su gsal bar byas de nas//dkyil 'khor lta bur thong ba yin//sbrul gyi dengs ka'i rnam pa bzhin//yongasu kris pas 
97b7(rgyud): brgyan pa yin//mu tig do shal rnams mtshungs shing //mgul rgyan pa par sprul pa'o//de nas 'bab chu dge ba'i 'thar//dri ma med par khrus byas nas//go rims ji ba bzhin khrus byas te/chu'i 
97b8(rgyud): gnas la ldang bar bya//de bzhin brtson pas nye reg bya//bgrang phreng srad bus blangs nas ni//ba'i rnam las rib tu bkru//sa'i phye mas rab bkrus nas//gtsang ma'i gos la dri zangs kyis//
98a1(rgyud): @#//rjesu nye bar byugs pa dang //rab tu rtags pa'i kha dog kyang //tsan dan dkar po gur gum dang //'bad pas dran mos 'gru bar bkru bar bya//de bzhin gnyis 'gro bar 'gyur //ji lta'i

98a6(rgyud): rgyas nyan thos gse ru rnams //gal te rmi lam mthong ba gyur na //sngags pas 'bras bu bcas par 'grub //nges par de'i cho ga bya//dge slong gzugs dang bram ze yi//gzugs brnyan sna tshogs

102b8(rgyud): 'khor du//rgyal mchog rnams kyis dbang ba yi//phyir mi ldog pa'i chos tshan ldan//'di thos sangs rgyas spyod pa yis//sems can kun gyi sdig pa rnams//ma lus rab tu gsal nas ni//blo chen dri ma med par 'gyur//
103a1(rgyud): @#//zhi zhing dag byed bkra shis pa//rdul med rgyan te zhi bar byed//dge zhing mya ngan thams cad sel//sdug sngal thams cad nges par 'jos//dbang chen gsang sngags dam pa ste;;sa stengs sdig pa thams cad sel//ma smad 'bras bu
103a2(rgyud): nges par smin//bstan pa yin te sdang ba 'dul//nyes pa byas ba sel ba yi//gangs 'd-i gang gis thos 'gyur des//sangs rgyas rnyed par dka' ba dag//ring bo mi thogs thob p+Ra 'gyur//sgrib pa thams cad rab zhi zhing //khyab rdag nyon mongs
103a3(rgyud): kun sel ba//gang zhig nyon mongs zhi byed pa'i//gzungs 'di 'chang bar byed pa yis//kun tu bzang po nyid dang ni//lag na rdo rje nyid kyang thob//rig sngags 'chang gi bdag po yang //'di thos pos ni thob par 'gyur//'jig
103a4(rgyud): rten kun la lam me ba//bcom ldan rnam par snang 'dzad kyang //dkyil 'khor dam pa rting med pa//'di ni ji srid ma gan par//rab zhi nyon mongs 'dul ba'i//rang byung go 'phang ma rnyed de//rnam par dag phyir gnas gyur nas//gsan ma
103a5(rgyud): thag tu rnam par dag/de phyir phun sum tshogs pa dang //legs par 'dod pas rjod par bya//gzhan gyis mi thub gzungs gnas//thub pa kun gyi gang rnyes pa//'khor ba 'di ni nam du yang //bdud kyi las rnaMs 'khrug med dang //mya
103a6(rgyud): ngan med cing 'thab med pa//gzungs la gnas pas thob par 'gyur//gnas kyi dam pa thob nas kyang //thub pa dri ma med pa'i blo//gzigs pa bzang rnams gang gshegs pa'i//bzang 'gro der ni 'gro bar 'gyur//legs
103a7(rgyud): par 'dod pa rnams kyis ni//tshig gi mdo 'di nyan par gyis//nyon mongs zad par bya ba la//rgyal bas gsungs pa'i gzungs 'dis mchog/sdig pa'i byed cing dge ba sdang //sdug pa'i rang bzhin rab 'byungs pa//gang gis thos
103a8(rgyud): pas de spong pa//nga'i mngon sum 'byor bar 'gyur//sdig pa gzhan yang mi bya zhing //snga ma rnams kyang bshags par bya//gzungs mchog gi ni mthu stobs kyis//sdig pa thams cad byang bar 'gyur//tshe rabs gzhan ni
103b1(rgyud): stong dag dang //sdig pa mi dge gang byas pa //dag 'gyur 'di la the tsom med //yar gyi ngo na tshes brgyad dang //de bzhin tshes ni bcwo lnga la//snyen gnas len pa balhur blangs nas //cho ga bzhin du sangs rgyas mchod //de nas rmongs pa med p+Ra
103b2(rgyud): yang //nyon mongs dag ni rab 'joms shing //shin tu dka' ba (blo mas nye ba phrag sbyar yang //)dpe med la//nyes pa zlog pa'i gzungs brjod na//thub pa'i zhal gyi zla ba'i 'od//'bar ba'i nges par mthong bar 'gyur //de nas tshogs rnaMs rdzogs pa'i //rgyal ba'i 
103b3(rgyud): sras po nyid du 'gyur //'od dpag med la mnga' ba yi //yon ten mtha' yas rab rgyas pa //bskal pa bye ba stong du yang //mtha' ni rtogs par thob par 'gyur //de nas rdo rje 'chang gis tshig/dkyil 'khor dag ni
103b4(rgyud): gnas rnams kyis//thos nas brgyan rnams sna tshogs pa //bcom ldan 'das la dbul bar byed//rdo rje ral gri la sogs pa //lha bdag gsal bar thams cad dang //ri bo ti se ri rab la //gnas pa'i rigasngag+sa
103b5(rgyud): 'chang rnams dang //de bzhin dkyil 'khor chen po ni//'khod pa'i rigasngags 'chang rnams dang //ma la ya'i ra bo la //gnas shing brgyan 'bar thogs rnaMs dang //khyab 'jug lha chen la sogs pa //lha'i bdag po rnams dang ni/
103b6(rgyud): lag na rdo rje la sogs pa//byang chub sems dpa' stobs chen rnaMs //nor bu'i dbung rgyan rna cha dang //brgyan rnaMs rab tu g.yo ba'i lus //rab tu bskyod de langs nas ni//sdig bral mnga' bdag rgyal ba la//phyag 'tshal thal
103b7(rgyud): mo sbyar nas kyang //kun gyis sa'i dbang phyug po//thub mchog dam pa de la ni//mtshan brgyad rtsa brgyad dag gi stong //mnyam nyid chos de de bzhin nyid //shin tu rtogs pa lam me ba//dul ba rtan pa dri ma sel //dri ma med par
103b8(rgyud): skyon dral ba //thub pa sangs rgyas shin tu zhing //'jig rten 'gon po skye dgu'i bdag/de bzhin gshegs pa nyon mongs thul //de bzhin gshegs pa rdul gsal ba //ston pa ston 'dzad stan pa sten//sdig pa
104a1(rgyud): @#//med pa rdul dang bral//'jig rten mgon po snang 'dzad pa//dag bzhi ba zas rtsang spras//nyon mongs rab 'joMs phan 'dogs pa //ston pa po ste dri ma sel//spyod pa yul dri med tshul bzang ba//zag pa rnam par gnon
104a2(rgyud): pa rtan po rnam par thugs rtse rgyal//mgon po de bzhin mgon mdzad pa//zhi ba'i bdag nyid zhe sdang bral//bcings pa las grol nges par grol//'jig rten kun mchog phra ba'i sa//bdag gir mi 'dzin dri ma med//ngan song 
104a3(rgyud): sel ba dpal dang ldan//dpal mdzad de bzhin phan 'dzad//mnyam pa med pa dpe med pa //khyab bdag nyon mongs las rnam spong //khyengs pa med pa dman pa med//mnyam pa de bzhin mi mnyam mnyam //snang ba rnyes pa
104a4(rgyud): mnyam byed pas//'jig rten snang mdzad sdig med pa//sdig pa thams cad spangs pa ste;'jig rten kun la phan par brtson//dpa' po zhi ba zhi mdzad pa //nyon mongs dgra rnams zad par mdzad//mnga' bdag thabs mkhyen
104a5(rgyud): thabs che ba //sems can kun gyi 'phra sa lags //don yod snyes dka' blo dang ldan//rdo rje 'akyil 'khor thub pa dka' //skyob pa 'jigs sel tshangs pa ste;;de bzhin sangs rgyas chos 'dzin pa //dpa' ba chen po
104a6(rgyud): 'jigs pa med //gzugs bzang mtshan rnams gsal ba ste//sum bcu rtsa gnyis mtshan mnga' ba //chos kyi longaspyod rgya mtsho lags//dpe byad bzang po bkod po yis//spras pa'i bdag nyid ya mtshan ston/dka'
104a7(rgyud): ba med pa'i ston pa po//de bzhin gshegs pa bde ba sbyin//mthu chen yangs pa rgya che ba //bdud bzhi stobs rnams sel bar byed//rtson chen spro ba che ba ste;;dges pa chen po stobs po che//mthu rnyes rtan pa chen po ste//gnas
104a8(rgyud): ldan dbang gi dar ba lags//gsal ba gsung ldan dpe med pa//dge ba stong pa nyid kyis rgyal //rab tu byung ba mun pa sel//blo gros chen po chos 'dzin pa //phun sum tshogs ldan sa chen rnyes//'dod pa rnams ni 'dul
104b1(rgyud): 'dzad pa||dpal mdzad dpal mnga' dpal dang ldan//dpal gyi longs spyod shin tu rgyas //bcom ldan legs sgos sgo bar mdzad//gsung ldan bkra shis rgyal bar gnas //mnyam pa nyid kyis mtshan dpag med//rab
104b2(rgyud): tu dges pa longaspyod gsal //zhi ba cas sel ba mnyan pa la gas//blo dag blo ldan dri ma spangs //gnas chen btan ldan pa ste||rgyas pa longaspyod dri ma med//thub pa thugs rje'i rgyan mnga' ba //skal pa zang po
104b3(rgyud): 'jigs pa'i skyabs //de bzhin 'jigs med nyon mongs sel//dri med skyon bral longaspyod can//sna tshogs 'od cha ldan dkyil 'khor mdzes//kun bzang dge ba'i rig pa'i mchog/thugs rje'i 'od ldan 'od bzang ba //mi gnas longs
104b4(rgyud): spyod dam pa lags//chos kyi rgyu de yon tan gnas//brjod 'os mchod gnas longs spyod che//nam mkha'i mtshan de rnam dag blo //rin chen gzugs 'dzes kra shis blo//khyu mchog cha spyan ba rtsi ma//rgyal ba skyon bral shes
104b5(rgyud): shes rab thabs dang ldan pa ste//'jig rten mi dge kun las 'das//dag par longaspyod stan pa can //sa dang stobs dang ye shes bzod //pha rol phyin pa kun la mnga'//ting 'dzin mnyam par longaspyod pa //rnam thar sgom pa
104b6(rgyud): mngon sum pa //rtong ba sangs rgyas kun dang mnyam//chos chen bde bar 'char ba lags //thaMd mkhyen pa yul las 'das//spyod yul sgrib pa mi mnga' ba/byang chub sems dpa'i dbang po rgyal //dam pa'i don de tshul che ba
104b7(rgyud): shes bya'i yul rnams mthA dag la//sgrib pa med par 'char bar brnyes//rin chen 'thun par skyes pa ste//de bzhin gshegs pa'i dge ba'i gnas //khyad par rnyes pa mnyam nyid rnyes //mi mnyam sangs rgyas kun dang
104b8(rgyud): mnyam /don chen shin tu mthar phyin pa //nyon mongs bral ba shin thub par dka'//dge slong sdig pa spyod mdzad pa //gnod pa med par bde bar 'byin//blo rgya che ba thams cad mkhyen //thos pa'i mtshon cha ma lus
105a1(rgyud): @#//gsan//dkon pa bde ba thob par 'gyur//mnyam par khyad par yul dang ldan//'jig rten thams cad dar 'dzod mgon//zhi gnas mtshon cha thub par dka' //bsam gyis mi khyab bsam yas yul //dpe med pa ste
105a2(rgyud): dpe ru bya//'od bzang rab dges dpal dang ldan//legs gsungs blo '-i stobs kyis 'byungs//mkhas pa zhal zangs bde 'byin ba//rab dges 'das pa'i spyod yul can//gsung ba chen po ye shes che//gnas chen kra shis
105a3(rgyud): chen po che//mchod par gyur pa yul las 'das//bya'i mtshan nyid med pa'o//sangs rgyas yul du skyes pa ste//shes bya'i spyod yul rgya che ba //kun mkhyen longs spyod 'bras bu can//'jig rten dbang 'das spyod yul pa
105a4(rgyud): kun mkhyen yul gyi snang ste//thabs lnga phun sum tshogs ldan pa //rdzu 'phrul rol pa'i thabs dang ldan//chos kyi gar thabs ston pa po//rab ston yon tan sgyur mdzad pa//spyod pa lam lus 'dul mdzad pa//
105a5(rgyud): yongasu rdzogs pa rnam par dag/sangs rgyas spyod yul rnam sangs rgyas //bgegs byed ma legs byed ma bcom//bdud kyi grags pa sel bar mdzad//g.yul ngor rnam par brgyal ba brnyes//dpa' chen po thub med
105a6(rgyud): stobs//theg pa chen po shing rta chibs//blo chen dgal bar dka' ba'i//nyon mongs rgya mtsho'i pha rol gshegs //theg pa chen po'i tshul la gnas//theg pa mchog la longaspyod pa //nyan thos sa' spyod yul
105a7(rgyud): can/rang rgyal las kyang 'das pa ste;;sangs rgyas sa'i 'bras bur gnas//gong nas gong du khyad par mkhyen//khyod ni nam mkha'i gnas na zhugs//bde byung sa dang zhing rnams kyo//mtsho chen dag la longs par byed
105a8(rgyud): sangs rgyas dpang po rgyal pa la//mtshan brgyad rtsa brgyad kyis stod nas//de la lha'i tshogs rnams kyis//phyag byas ma go smad phyogs cig 'khod//de nas dkyil 'khor chen po na//gnas pa ma lus che mar
105b1(rgyud): 'khor //thod du rdo rje'i thod mching ba//langs nas bcod pas rab tu g.yos//dkyil 'khor 'thon po thams cad du//skad ber chen pos yongs bkad nas//nor bu'i rgyan ni rab g.yo zhing //ha ha sgrogs te
105b2(rgyud): sgra sgrogs te/kye ma sangs rgyas yon tan che//skal pa grangs med gsum bsags pa//byang chub sems stsogs mchog rnams kyis//mtshan ni rgya rtsa brgyad legs par rjod//kye ma rnyed dang //bde byung ba //mtshan
105b3(rgyud): rgyad rtsa rgyad thos kyang //gang zhig klog cing rgyal la stod//'bras bu chen po 'grub par 'gyur//mtshan brgya rtsa brgyad sngags bcas pa//bcom ldan 'das drung rjod nas ni//sgrib dang kun nas nyon mongs dang //
105b4(rgyud): sdig pa thams cad dag pa thob//mtshan rgya rtsa rgyad mchog bton pa//sgrib pa kun las nges grol zhing //nyon mongs mtha' dag mtshan rgya rtsa brgyad sngags bcas po//rnam par song //shes rab chen po nyid du 'gyur//
105b5(rgyud): de ni longs spyod chen po dang //bde ba las kyang ma nyams mi 'gyur//de tshe rabs thaMd du //blo ldan tshe rabs dran par 'gyur//de nas rdo rje'i thod 'ching ba//yon tan mtha' yas sras de nas//bcom ldan 'das rdud las grol
105b6(rgyud): bdud rgyal ba yis//mngon sum gzigs nas bka' stsal pa //rgyal ba'i sa 'di zhi ba ste//nga rgyal laswogs nyon mongs 'joms//'jig rten bde ba kun las 'das//de bzhin gshegs rje mngon du phyogs//
105b7(rgyud): gzhan gyis mi tshugs gzungs dang ni//mtshan rgya rtsa rgyad nyan byed pa//sems can kun la bde 'dod pa//lan cig thos pa tsam gyis kyang //de dag sa chen 'bras bur gnas //ring po mi dga' 'gyur na//chad stsog+sa
105b8(rgyud): lta la ci zhig smos//de phyir rtag tu rab sgrims la //brtan tu bde ba 'dod pa yis//yon tan rgya mtsho ldan pa'i gzungs//mnyan par bya zhing bcang bar bya//gang zhig nang par langs nas rjod //bdag nyid kyis
106a1(rgyud): //kyang gton na ni//de'i don kun grub 'gyur la//god pa dang 'phad mi 'gyur//'di ni yon tan kun gyi spos//dge ba phun sum tshogs sbyin pa//kun mkhyen 'bras bu bla med mchog/bde 'byung thob par
106a2(rgyud): byed pa yin//'bras bu 'byor ba ci yang rung //thob par mi 'gyur dogs pa med//kun mkhyen go 'phang thob 'gyur nas//dpe med gnasu 'gro bar 'gyur//'di ni dge zhing 'bras bur ldan//sangs rgyas sa'i
106a3(rgyud): 'bras bur smin//yid du 'ong zhing yon tan ldan//snying rje'i dri ma med sbyin pa//'di ni mnyam pa nyid kyi mdo//'jig rten kun na dga' ba ste//'jig rten kun gyi bde byung ba//'di dang 'dra ba gang yang med//ston pa sangs
106a4(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das kyi mtshan brgya rtsa brgyad pa gzungs sngags bcas pa rdzogas+h+yo////'phags pa byams pa'i mtshan rgya rtsa rgyad pa gzung sngags dang bcas pa//byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po
106a5(rgyud): rgyal chab du dbang skur ba//'phags pa byams pa la phyags 'tshalo//de nas byang chub sems dpa' byams pas rang gi snying po smras pa//nam ste yad tI ka nan/sarba ta thA ga tA nan/tad+ya thA//oM sa ra sa ra/su ru su ru/d+ha 
106a6(rgyud): ra d+ha ra/ma hA d+ha ra/b+ha ra b+ha ra mahA b+ha rA/kA b+ha gAM b+ha mahA kAM b+ha/hU~M mahA s+pha ra na//d+ha rma a ga tra sa ga ra swAhA//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//kye ma gzungs chen mo 'di ni yon
106a7(rgyud): dan cha ste/mtha' yas par khyab pa//da skal pa bye ba mang por yang thos par dka' ba//sangs rgyas 'byung ba thams cad stod pa de bzhin gshegs pa grangs med pa 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad snyed kyis snyen
106a8(rgyud): kun mdzad pa sgrib pa dang //sdug sngal ba dang /'khrug pa thams cad zhi bar byed pa/darmi lam ngan pa dang ltas ngan pa thams cad las thar bar byed pa/sems can gyi khams mtha' yas pa mu med pa yongasu thar
106b1(rgyud): byed pa ste/shin tu dkon pa'o//rdo rje'i dkyil 'khor gyi lha dang /lha ma yin du ba'i nor bu cod pan gyis sgyings pa/brgyan 'phreng rab tu g.yo ba rnams kyis spyi bor thal mo sbyar nas//byang chub sems dpa'i mchog 'phag+sa
106b2(rgyud): pa byams pa la mtshan rgya rtsa rgyad kyis mngon par stod pa//dpa bo chen po thabs rnyes pa//rgyal chab mi'i bdag po 'jig rten bdag po rnams par gsal//dar ba chen po mngon par rgyas //mtshan chen stobs ldan che ba ste//
106b3(rgyud): de bzhin g.yengs shing rdul dang bral//dbang chen rtse ltar 'od ldan pa//sa'i dbang phyug rnam par gnon//rgyal chen tshon cha brgya pa ste//mtshon cha chen po rdo rje'i mtshon//yon ngan 'od rgyas (byas ste/)ste//nor bu'i rna cha'i rgyan thogs pa
106b4(rgyud): srid gsum dag na spa ba'i sku//rgya mtsho lta bu'i spyod yul can//lhun po zla 'byin ti se yi//rtse nas dkyil 'khor char ba cho//dkyil 'khor chen po longaspyod pa//rab tu dga' ba de bzhin nges//yongasu dga' ldan chen po'i
106b5(rgyud): 'i lha//che ba lha rnams kyi mchog/sangs rgyas bdud rgyud rgyas mdzad cing //sangs rgyas dkyil 'khor rgyan dang ldan//sangs rgyas longs spyod longs par spyod//sangs rgyas ston pa ston pa po//ti se zla
106b6(rgyud): 'byin shin tu yangs//sa'i dbang phyug bde mdzad pa//pad ma 'dab stong rtse mo can//rdo rje pad ma legs par skyes//chos kyi longs spyod longs par spyod //sa dang mnyam par thug par dga'//snang bar
106b7(rgyud): byed par 'byung ba'o//srid gsum ye shes nyi ma lags//gtso bo chen po char grags pa//seng ge chen po stobs po che//cha lugs chen po zhal che ba//skye bo ngan dang sdug bsngal 'dul//nor bu dbu thogs rgyan
106b8(rgyud): du thogs//byi ru gser (kyi )bcal bkram pa//sku chen zhabs kyi stabs kyis gshegs//zhal gdangs nyi ma du ma'i gziM//ljang lo'i cod pan dbu thod can//rig sngags 'chang rgyas zhabs la 'dud
107a1(rgyud): @#//rjed par bya (bas)rnams kyis mchod//nor bu rgyan 'gul 'od zer gyi//drang gi phyogs rnams dag par byas//cod pan can te 'od 'tsher ba//nyi ma bye ba brgya stong ldan //rin chen sna tshogs 'od bkram sha'i//
107a2(rgyud): dri med dkyil 'khor rab tu 'khro//rlung gis ma lus bskyod pa'i//'bar ba chen po lta ba can //'od zer mang pos phog pa yi//phan pa'i thabs ni rab ston pa//brjid pa'i tshul gyis bzhed pa'i //'od kyi zhal gsal
107a3(rgyud): rnga yab skyod //dpung brgyan 'od zer spel mar bkod //mtshon dri shin du mdzes par 'char //lhun po 'phrad cing 'khrug pa ltar //ha ha zhes pa'i sgra chen sgrogs //rdo rje chen po rtse mo can //shin du dga' mdzad bde
107a4(rgyud): sbyin pa//byams pa'i dkyil 'khor go char bgos//brtsod pa kun las rnam par rgyal //snying rje'i stobs las byung ba ste //mtshon cha chen po shin du 'bar //byams pa byams pa'i rtog pa can //kun nas dpa' ba'i
107a5(rgyud): dkyil 'khor pa //ma 'pham thub med dga' ba ste //sangs rgyas cha lugs mnyam nyid che//byams pa'i ni snying po che //byams pa'i pad ma las byung ba //rdo rje (dbang gi )'chang chen rdo rje snyan pa'i dkyil 'khor ba//
107a6(rgyud): rigs sngags 'chang ba rnams kyis btud //de bzhin sten cing ston pa po//sgrub pa chen po rigs sngags grub //rdo rje dkyil 'khor mdzes par mdzad //thams cad grub mdzad grub pa po//sangs rgyas gdung
107a7(rgyud): gi sdong chen lags//gsang sngags chen po rnam par nges//mdo sde bkod pa mdo sdud pa //nges 'byung nges pa'i 'od sgron gyis//snang ba dri ma sel bar mdzad //'bar ba rtsa dmar 'od snang ba//dkyil 'khor dkar po che ba ste//
107a8(rgyud): gzhung dang 'thun pa'i chos dbyings dang //nye bar gyur cing shin tu des //swahA swahA'i bdag po mchog//'od zer bde legs bde legs mdzad //stobs chen 'chang ba bkra shis pa//nyi ma 'dra ste snyoms pa lags//stong chen rgyal ba'i longs
107b1(rgyud): spyod ldan //mkha' ltar dri med spyod yul can //dpa' bo rdo rjes 'joms par mdzad //rdo rje chen po rab sgrub pa //grub pa po ste brtson pa med //dkyil 'khor ldan pa rdo rjes ston //rlung chen shugs kyis skyod pa yi //g.yo dang 'brug sgrog+sa/
107b2(rgyud): sgra ldan pa //sangs rgyas spyod yul pad ma ste //de bzhin nyid kyi 'tsho bas skyes //mngon shes chen po mngon shes rnyes //khyab bdag sangs rgyas kun dang mnyaM //de nas sa'i snying dbang phyug po //byams pa'i dkyil 'khor byams pa la //stod nas
107b3(rgyud): kun nas gnod bral ba //thub pa'i nyi mas bkA stsal pa //kye ma ming rgya rtsa brgyad mchog //ming gi yon tan 'od ldan pa //bde dang phun sum tshogs sgrub pa //thabs chen bla na med pa ste //ming brgya rtsa brgyad 'di dag ni //'jig rten kun gyi
107b4(rgyud): bde skyid pa //'di ni ton pa tsam gyis kyang //sdig pa thaMs cad dag par 'gyur //sdig pa dag las rgyal ba yi //byams pa'i stod pa'i gnas pa la //ming brgya rtsa brgyad dam pa 'dis //mi ni la la gang stod pa //de la lha lasogs kun dang /
107b5(rgyud): lag na rdo rje stobs can rnams //rtag pa nyid du sdug bsngal kun //brtan du gsal phyir brtson par byed //gang zhig ming brgya rtsa brgyad mchog //myur du 'don pa'i ming de ni //sangs rgyas sa yang thob 'gyur te /'di la the tsom yod
107b6(rgyud): med do //nang par ma la nas langs nas ni //gang zhig byams pa'i sems kyis stod //de la byams par rtogs pa ni //byams pa bde legs byed par 'gyur //de phyir dri ma thams cad sel byed cing //sdig 'joms yon tan
107b7(rgyud): thams cad bskyed pa dang //de bzhin 'jig rten sdug bsngal sel ba yi //ming brgya rtsa brgyad ched du dag par lhogs //'phags pa byams pa'i mtshan brgya rtsa brgyad pa gzungs sngags dang bcas pa rdzogs s+ho////
107b8(rgyud): @#//'phags pa nam mkha'i snying po'i mtshan brgya rtsa brgyad pa bzungs sngags dang bcas pa/'phags pa nam mkha'i snyingapo li zhal ras rgya che zhing /spyan gyi 'od 'phro bas nam mkha'i dkyil 'khor gyi dri zhim
108a1(rgyud): /par mdzad pa la phyag 'tshal lo//nam mkha'i spyod yul can //sa'i dkyil 'khor snang bar mdzad pa la phyag 'tshal lo//oM swa sti /ka ma la k+sh+wi /bI phu rapa saM b+ha ra /d+harma d+har du /g+ho tsa ra swahA//
108a2(rgyud): de nas gzungs 'di brjod ma thag tu ngan song dang ngan 'gro thams cad ni rab tu zhi bar 'gyur /sa yang g.yos par gyur /rnga bo che dag gi sgra yang grag par gyur //sangs rgyas thams cad kyis kyang legaso zhes
108a3(rgyud): bya ba byin no //de nas bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyis bka' stsal pa /kye ma 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor na //'khod pa'i sems can nam mkha'i snying po dkyil 'khor mngon sum pa rnams kyis sa ha'i mchog gi 
108a4(rgyud): gzungs 'byung bar shin du dka' /bsod nams chen po sdig pa dang nyon mongs pa thams cad sreg pa /byang chub sems dpa' thams cad rin po che'i phung por gyur pa 'di nyon cig dang /'di thos pas dri ma med par
