108a6(rgyud): can thams cad kyis bskyod par mi nus so//thar pa lta bur gyur te bde ba dang sdug bsngal gyis mi gos so//de la sogs pa yon tan stong phrag gis grangs pa /tshad med grangs med pa dag thob par 'gyur ro //nyan pa dang 
108a7(rgyud): 'chad pa dang klog pa dang brjod pa dang /ston pa tsam dang /bzlas brjod byed pa tsam gyis gang ci yang rung ste /de'i 'dod pa dang smon pa dang /re ba thams cad rdzogs par 'gyur ro //bzlas brjod byed pa tsam gyis kyang /
108a8(rgyud): char ches pu dang /char mi 'bab pa dang /sdang bas bkrug pa dang /mi nad dang /rmi lam ngan pa dang /gnod pa 'byung ba dang /'jigs pa'i dus dang /'chi ba dang /tshom rkun dang /dgon pa dang /g.yul 'gyed 
108b1(rgyud): pa dang ;gcan zan dang /mu ge dang //sdug bsngal rnams las yongasu srung bar byed pa /bde ba dang 'byor pa dang ci 'dod pa dang /ci la dga' ba thams cad kyang 'grub par 'gyur ro //de nas 'khor de dag thams cad 
108b2(rgyud): kyis ha ha zhes ca co dang ga zho ton nas sgra ding zhes byas nas /gos dang brgyan sna tshogs bskyod de /snying nas phyag 'tshal nas /byang chub sems dpa' thams cad la dbang byed pa 'phags pa namakha'i snying po'i mtshan 
108b3(rgyud): brgya rtsa brgyad pa'i sngags pa rgya mtsho stod pa yang dag par brjod pa /nam mkha'i snying po srid gsum sems can mchog //'byor ldan blo gros rin chen rgya mtsho mnga' //sems can dag pa bla med rin chen mchog//
108b4(rgyud): thar pa'i sa bon 'khor ba'i sdug bsngal sel //kun mkhyen tshul mkhyen legs dang legs min mkhyen //thar mchog chos mkhyen bde dang mi bde mkhyen //lam mkhyen rjes mkhyen dge dang mi dge mkhyen //dus mkhyen
108b5(rgyud): dge mkhyen stobs dang stobs min mkhyen //'gro mchog chos mchog sems can yon tan mchog//lam mchog sngags mchog tshul dang tshul min mchog//rgyas mchog chos mchog 'dzin dang 'dzin min mchog //dga'
108b6(rgyud): mchog rtogs mchog mchog dang mchog min mchog//zhing rab snying rje'i rab ste 'khor gyi rab //'od rab thar pa'i rab ste rten gyi rab//rgya rab sngags kyi rab ste thugs rtse'i rab//mnyam rab sems can rab te gsum
108b7(rgyud): rig rab //legs sgron mchog sgron stobs dang stobs min sgron //thar sgron rigs sgron rgyal dang rgyal min sgron //dge sgron gzi sgron nor bu 'od kyi sgron //rgyu skar zla ba rgyal las shar ba'i sgron //mchog 'od yon tan

109a2(rgyud): rdo rje 'od can rdo 'dzin rdo rje drag //rdo rje rgya chen rab rdo rje rab mchog dbang //na bza' sngo zhing sku'i cha lugs sngo //sku mdog sngo zhing sngo ba'i 'od kyis brgyan //ral gri rno 'joms sngo zhing rtan pa snams//mkha' sngo zhal med khang
109a3(rgyud): 'bar nang na bzhugs //lha zhal shin du dri med mi bzad 'bar //rab tu grag la 'od zer 'phro zhing 'gyu //sdig sel rdul ltar 'khrul med phreng mchog can //klog gi stobs kyis snang bas gegs la brtan //ral gri mtshon
109a4(rgyud): thogs dbang ldan mthu stobs mtshon //rdo rje'i mtshon thogs mda' dang mdung thung 'jigs //'khor lo'i mtshon thogs bcings pa'i ri dang 'khrul//gsal shing mtshon thogs bgyegs kyi stobs 'joms pa //bde 'byung 'jig
109a5(rgyud): rten gsum kun rdo rje'i dbyug//sku rnams thams cad nyi ma'i 'od kyis rgyan //drags pa'i rigs su 'byung bas rgyal po mdzad //mu tig phreng bas sgyeg cing mdzes par bgyis//sku mdzes rnyes shing phun sum tshogs pa rnyes //
109a6(rgyud): rab 'jam mchog rnyes sa la 'jug pa rnyes//mthu stobs lhag par rnyes shing grub brnyes la//rig pa gsum gyi mthu rnyes stobs rnyes la //bde 'dren zhi ba 'dren cing 'od 'dren pa//la phyugs gsal mchog dar ba chen po
109a7(rgyud): 'dren;byams 'dren nyi ma 'dren cing skye dgu 'dren //legs 'dren zhing gi stobs 'dren snying rje can //dri med cha lugs gsal ba cha lugs 'bar //rnam drags sgyeg pas grags pa'i ba dan can //'gro ba'i skabs

109b1(rgyud): rigs sku rigs 'chang ba rin chen snams //rtsu ga gi nor bu rtsu ga can bcod pan can /cod pan can te chos 'dzin rdo rje mchog //dpal 'dzin rin chen dam pa 'chang ba'i mchog //sngags bzhi phyag rgya 'chang (phun )sum

109b3(rgyud): stug nor bu'i 'od //nyi ma'i nor bu tshul spyod nor bu 'bar //yid bzhin nor bu 'gro kun mgon gcig pa//nam mkha'i dkyil na nor bu gcig snang ltar //sems can rnams dag yangs shing rnam par grags //srid gsuM
109b4(rgyud): bsrungs ma gcig pu rgyal sras mchog //zhi ba rab tu zhi la skyabs 'os stag //skye dgu'i bdag po pad 'byung pad ma skyes//kun nas 'od bzang legs bshad chos mnga' ba//dar ba legs bshad rab dul legs bshad
109b5(rgyud): phyag;blo bzang 'od bzang bde ldan skye gnas bzang //mkhas rab zhi rab mchog gi chos dang ldan //zhi mdzad rgyal 'byung bde ba 'byung ba'i gnas//'od kyi 'byung gnas bde mdzad dbang du mdzad //legs 'byung shes
109b6(rgyud): rab 'byung gnas stobs bskyed pa//snying rje'i 'byung gnas blo bzangs rnam grags mdzad //rnam grol nges grol shin tu grol ba'i blo //blo yang rnam par bkra shing rnam par grags /kun tu long spyod sgo ste blo'i mchog;
109b7(rgyud): blo gros rtan pa dkon mchog gsum las bsgrubs //sangs rgyas pad ma 'dab rgyas 'dra ba'i zhal //dag cing spyan yangs cha lugs yid tu 'thad //snying po gsal zhing snying rje rtan zhing dul //ngo bo nyid kyi rgyun rnyes rab
109b8(rgyud): gsal ba //spyan zur mda' ni rab dul gtad legs pa //'jigs med zhal ras ston ka'i zla ba 'dra //mdung thung brgya'i mtshon mchog mtshon cha rdzogs//drag po 'joms pa'i sdig sel gsum las skyes //rab tu

110a2(rgyud): me tog mnga' //nam bza' bzang rnams g.yas 'phel tshabs chen 'phel //legs bris sna tshogs mtshon cha sna tshogs mnga' //nam bza' sna tshogs rgyan gyi dpal //zhal ras zla ba'i 'od zer rgyas pa 'dra //

110a6(rgyud): mnga' zhing spyan bzangs stug par ldan //ston pa bzang po stong zhing lam gyi stegs //gtsos gsum 'phel ba'i rgyu la 'jug par mdzad //brtan po rgyu la rab tu nges 'gyur ba//dkyil 'khor dam pa kun gyi slob dpon 
110a7(rgyud): lags//gcig pus skye dgu'i dkyil 'khor cho ga mkhyen //rnam par snang mdzad zhing gi gnas nas skyes//dbang skur brnyes shing sa bcur legs gnas pa //gnas chen son pa mchog gnas gcig pu lags;
110a8(rgyud): skye dgu rnams kyi nyon mongs sel ba dang //sdig 'joms mtshan brgya rtsa brgyad 'di thos nas //bde gshegs sras mchog su zhig 'don byed pa //'jig rten zla ba gcig shar bzhin du snang //nam mkha'i snying po'i 
110b1(rgyud): sems can mchog gcig pa'i //mdun du nang par stod pa 'di brjod gang //de ni srid gsum sems can mchog gyur zhing //dri ma'i gnas kun las kyang thar par gyur //g.yul dang 'thab rtsod shin tu mi 
110b2(rgyud): bzad dang //mu ge'i gnod pa mi nad byung ba na //mkhas pa su zhig sngar langs dang bskyed nas //nam mkha'i snying po la ni stod byed pa //de ni 'jigs pa'i 'byung gnas thams cad dang //sdug bsngal

110b4(rgyud): nor thob dang //rigs thob yon tan kun gyi gzhi sgrub phyir //bton na 'grub 'gyur 'di la the tsom med //de phyir ci ste rtag tu bde 'dod kun //'dod na 'gro ba kun la gcig dpa' ba //nam mkha'i snying po srid

110b8(rgyud): dam pa sbyin pa yon tan chen po'i skus kun du mnga' mdzad pa la phyag 'tshal lo //de nas de'i tshe byang chub seMs dpa' kun tu bzang pos /gzugs sna tshogs can byad chub seMs dpa' rnams kyi ting nge 'dzin rab 'jaM 
111a1(rgyud): //sa'i dbang phyug chen po zhes bya ba la snyoms par bzhugaso //de nas byang chub sems dpa' chen po kun tu bzang po snyoms par zhugs ma thag pa de'i tshe /stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi khaMs kyi rdul
111a2(rgyud): phra rab kyi rdul snyed kyi sku rab 'jam kun tu yongasu rgyas par gyur te /nam mkha'i khams kyi khong du chud pa'i sku rnams sa'i dkyil 'khor du ma dag tu kun nas 'khod par gyur to //de nas sangs rgyas kyi zhing rab 'jam

111a5(rgyud): nas|gzungs 'di bshad do //tad+ya thA /oM na mo /na ma shri b+hu ba ni shwa rI /mahA b+ho gA yA /a sa ma sa ma /a myi ta sa ma /a nan ta sa ma /ga ga na sa ma hri b+hu ba na sa ma /a she Sha sa ma /b+he sha sa ma /ba ra ma r+tha sa ma

112a1(rgyud): //'gyuro //lan gnyis pa brjod pa tsam gyis sdig pa thams cad las grol bar 'gyuro//lan gsum brjod pa tsam gyis nyon mongs pa thams cad las grol bar 'gyuro//de ltar na brjod pa tsam gyis

112a5(rgyud): lus ngal bar gyur gyi bar du gzungs chen mo 'di brjod la ngal bar gyur nas de nyid du nyal/rmi lam du byang chub sems dpa' kun du bzang po mthong bar 'gyur te/phyag g.yas pas de la byugs nas yang dag par dbugs

112a7(rgyud): nas sa chen po thob par 'gyur ro//zhes gsung ngo//de nas lus bde ba dang/sems bde ba dang/dga' ba chen po dang/dang ba dang/mchog tu dga' ba chen po dang ldan bzhin du sad par 'gyuro//mthu che ba dang che bar yang grags par
112a8(rgyud): 'gyuro//lha rnams kyang de la lta ba'i phyir 'ong ngo//nang par langs nas byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis yang dag par bsdus pa'i mdo sde rnams klag par bya'o//sems can thams cad la phan pa dang bde ba'i seMs skyed par bya'o/

112b2(rgyud): de la ji skad bstan par bzhin du byed pa'i rgyal ba'i sras de ni ring po mi thogs par kun du bzang po'i sa chen po la 'jug par 'gyuro//de nas rdo rje gtsug phud lasogs pa stong gsum gyi bdag po rigs sngags 'chang rnams dang/spyan chen po la 
112b3(rgyud): sogs pa rigs sngags 'chang chen po byang chub sems dpa' chen po rnams dang/rdo rje zhags pa la swogs pa rigs sngags kyi lha chen po rnams dang/rin chen dra ba lasogs pa rigs sngags kyi lha mo chen po rnams dang/lha mo sgrol ma 
112b4(rgyud): lasogs pa byang chub sems dpa' chenapo'i dkyil 'khor kyi lha rnams dang/dar chen dang/gzugs bzang dang/shin du lta na sdug pa la swogs pa gzhonu rnams dang/gzhonu ma rnams dang/bran rnams dang/bran mo rnams dang/mi 
112b5(rgyud): 'khrugs pa la swogs pa log 'dren chen po rnams dang/lus mchog lasogs pa srin po'i bdag po 'khor gyi lha rnams dang/ma mo lasogs pa masrin mo rnams dang/dbang phyug chen po lasogs pa lha'i dbang phyug rnams dang/
112b6(rgyud): rdo rje me tog thod lasogs pa dkyil 'khor chen por 'dus pa'i byang chub sems dpa' thams cad langs nas/byang chub sems dpa'i mchog 'phags pa kun du bzang po la mtshan brgya rtsa brgyad bla na med pa'i yon tan gyi snang ba zhes bya bas 
112b7(rgyud): mngon par bstod pa/kun du bzang po kun gyi mchog//sna tshogs kun gyi 'byor pa che//dkyil 'khor chen po'i bdag nyid mchog//sna tshogs gzugs can phyogs kyi bdag//rdo rje chen po dges pa ste//gzi brjid chen po stobs 
112b8(rgyud): po che//gnon dang rab gnon dpal gyi rab//khyad par rgyal ba'i blo gros rgyal//rdo rje chen po rab tu gzhad//sgrub dka' chen po sgrub 'dzad pa//rol mo chen pos rnam par rol//rab dga' chen po 'dul ba po//lha chen//
113a1(rgyud): @#//dbang phyug chen po ste//rtsan po khyab 'jug skye dgu'i bdag //sa stengs sa 'og sa la dges //de bzhin sa 'dzin sa gsum pa//dul dang sbyin pa'i go rims thabs//rab mkhyen stong gi mnga'

120b1(rgyud): nyon mongs khams rnams dag pa'i bdag/las kyi khams rnams zad byed pa//chu bo brgya mtsho kun la rgal//sbyor ba'i dgon pa las byung ba//nyon mongs nye ba kun nyongs//bag chags bcas pa brtan spangs pa//snying rje chen po'i

121a1(rgyud): @#//srid pa'i dgon pa las rgal ba //ston pa gcig pur 'gro ba'i bla//'jig rten phyogs bcur rab grags pa//chos kyi sbyin bdag chen po pa//byams pa'i go chas chas pa ste//snying rje yi ni ya lang dgos//shes rab

121a8(rgyud): byung khyod la 'dud//sgyu 'phrul dru ba khyod la 'dud//sangs rgya' rol ston khyod la 'dud//thaMd thaMd khyod la 'dud//ye shes sku nyid khyod la 'dud////lan rdo rje 'chang 'di ni//bcom ldan 'das kyi ye shes sku//de bzhin gash+yeng
121b1(rgyud): thams cad kyi ye shes sku//'jam dpal ye shes sems dpa'i yongasu dge ba'i thun mong ma yin pa'i mtshan yang dag par brjod pa yin te/khyod kyis dga' ba dang /dang ba dang/mgu ba chen po bla na med pa skyed pa dang/lus

121b8(rgyud): sum tshogs pa'o//theg pa chen po'i bzhi'o//byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa thaMd kyi 'byung gnaso//'phags pa'i lam thams cad kyi mtha'o//rnam par grol ba rnams rtags pa'o//des par 'byung ba'i lam

122b1(rgyud): yongasu rdzogs pa'o//'phags pa'i bden pa bzhi po rnams kyi so sor rtogs pa'o//chos thams cad kyi dran pa nye bar gzhag pa rnams sems gcig gis rtogs pa'o//ming yang dag par brjod pa 'di ni sangs

122b7(rgyud): 'joms par byed pa'o//sdug bsngal ba dang yid mi bde ba thams cad mi skye bar byed pa'o//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi snying por gyur pa'o//byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi gsang bar gyur pa'o//
122b8(rgyud): nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas thams cad kyi gsang chen du gyur pa'o//phyag rgya dang gsang sngags thams cad du gyur pa'o//chos thams cad rjod du med par smra ba rnams kyi dran pa dang shes bzhin dang
123a1(rgyud): @#//yang dag par bskyed pa'o//bla na med pa'i shes rab dang/yid gzhungs par byed pa'o//nad med pa dang /stobs dang dbang phyug phun sum 'tshogs par byed pa'o//dpal dang dge ba dang zhi ba dang

123a8(rgyud): bsod nams dang ye shes kyi tshogs dang/ting 'dzin yongasu rdzogs pa'i sa bcu pa'o//chos nyid dang bral ba'i phyir gnyisu med pa'i chos nyid do//sgro btags pa med pa'i phyir de bzhin nyid kyi rang bzhin dang
123b1(rgyud): rang bzhin ma yin pa'i chos nyid do//de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes yongasu dag pa'i ngo bo nyid yin pa'i phyir yang dag pa'i mtha'i rang bzhin no//lta ba ngan pa tshang tshing brgya stong ma lus par sel ba'i phyir /rnaM pa
123b2(rgyud): thams cad rnam med pa stong pa nyid kyi rang bzhin nyid do//ming yang dag par brjod pa 'di lta ste/gnyisu med pa'i chos nyid kyi don yang dag par 'dzin pa ston pa'i phyir //chos thams cad brjod du med pa'i 
123b3(rgyud): rang bzhin nyid do//lag na rdo rje 'chang gzhan yang rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo gsang sngags kyi sgo'i spyod pa spyod pa gang la bcom ldan 'das 'jam dpal ye shes sems dpa' de bzhin gsheg+sa
123b4(rgyud): pa thams cad kyi ye shes kyi sku gnyisu med pa'i don dam pa'i ming yang dag par brjod ba migi gi tsug gi nor bu 'di thams cad yongasu rdzogs pa dang /ma nyams shing lhag chad med pa tshig+sa

192a1(rgyud): @#//sku dang gsung dang/yang dag byung //rtul shugs kyis ni yang dag rgyan //rtag par thams cad skyong bar byed//de ltar yon tan mdor sdus pa'i //mtshan nyid bdag gis bstan pa yin//'di rnam+sa
192a2(rgyud): yang dag sngos shes pa//slob dpon du ni rab tu sngags//bsngags kyis zlas pa'i mchog ni//gzhan du ni 'bris med bu//gsang sngags rang gis mi 'grub cing//gzhan la bstan kyang 'grub pa med//dkyil 'khor

192b7(rgyud): rab tu bcing bar bya//rang nyid go cha bgos nas gnas//lus la gtsug tor yang dag rgyan //lag na rdo rje thogs nas ni//lha ni yid kyis byon par gnas //de ltar bdag nyid gnas nas su//skye ba srung//

193a1(rgyud): @#//btul bar bya//ba yi rnam lngas byugs nas ni//me tog bdug pas dge bar mchod//sa bzhi 'di ni phyogs kun du//gtor ma rab tu btang bar bya//me tog sil ma gcal du bkram //dri dang 
193a2(rgyud): bdug pa cho ga rims//sbyin sreg cho ga bzhin du bya//rnal 'byor dge ba 'dod pa yis//de ltar rab tu 'bad pa yis//gnas su nyi ma gcig tu bya//legs par sbyang bar gyur pa la//lha ni spyan yang

193a7(rgyud): rgyas bstan pa yin//gnyis pa rgyal ba'i sras po ste//mdo ru bsdus nas bstan pa yin//gnyis ba'i mi rol gnas pa'i cha//der yang de bzhin rab tu rtag//khru tshad gang du mdor bsdus na//cha ni thams cad

193b1(rgyud): rdo rje rta babs bcings pa dang/zhags pas bcings pas rnam par rgyan //de ltar 'di dag kun byas nas//dbus su pad ma 'dab brgyad byas //de'i snying po mdzes par ni //de la bum pa yang dag gnas //

193b4(rgyud): rten nas ni//skyob pa 'jig rten dbang phyug bzhugs //pad ma'i steng du rdo rje ni//kha dog dkar po gzi brjid che //byams pa yi ni go cha bgos//byams pa'i bdag nyid las byung ba'i//dbang sngon

197a4(rgyud): pa//bskal pa chen por bsam par bya//de ltar dkyil du mgon po ni//mchod sbyin bdag nyid bsam pa ni//gtso bo bstan la sman mdzad pa//rnam pa kun du bsam par bya//de nas mkhas pas
197a5(rgyud): mar me dang//mchod yon zhal gsil shin du mdzes//dri dang me tog mar me dang//bdug pa gtor ma de bzhin du//zhal zas de la dbul bar bya//chan dang 'o thug de bzhin no//ras
197a6(rgyud): kyis mgrin pa bcings pa yi//bum pa bcu drug bzhag par bya//gdugs dang rgyal mtshan ba dan dang//rta dang glang po gzhon pa'i mchog//dril bu'i sgra ni rab tu bstan//rol mo rin chen khyad par du//
197a7(rgyud): bye ba grangs ni med pa yi//mchog nyid kyang ni dbul bar bya//khyad par yang ni snyan pa'i sgra//dbyangs khugs sos dal yid 'phrog pa//dril bu'i rol mo khyad par du 'ang //dkyil 'khor dge ba 
197a8(rgyud): mchod par bya//de ltar cho ga bzhin mchod nas//ser mo bcu'i thal sbyar te//smon lam rab tu 'phel bya'i phyir//rgyal mchog la sogs rab tu bdud//de nas de byung slob ma ni//blo ldan dkyil 'khor du bos te/
197b1(rgyud): de tshe gsum la skyabs 'gro sbyin//dkon mchog gsum la gus pas ldan//byang chub tshul khrims phun sum tshogs//gtong la dga' zhing shin du brtson//dbang po drug ni kun du ldan//sgyu rtsal cho ga rnam par 
197b2(rgyud): mkhas//spro ba che nang sems dpa' ldan//byas pa bzo zhing byas pa bzo//pha dang ma la shin du gus//dkon mchog gsum la shin du des//sangs rgyas nyan thos chos rnams dang //'jig rten pa rnams mchod
197b3(rgyud): par byed//dad pa dang ni thugs rjes ldan//nad med dbang po ma tshang med//sems can phan par yang dag ldan//sdig pa dag ni rab tu 'joms//log pa'i lta ba rnam par bral//yang dag lta ba yang dag
197b4(rgyud): lta ba yang dag chen//ser zhing ljang khu dkar ba dang //nag par gyur kyang mdangs bzang ba//gang yang lus ni rab du drang //mgo bo shin du rgyas bcas pa//sna ldan smin 'tshams bar mchad
197b5(rgyud): mig dang rdzi ma yang dag ldan//gang yang mnyam pa nyid du gan//de bzhin mgo bo gdugs lta bu//skyed pa rgyas shing yang dag ldan//rkang pa shin du gnas pa dang //de sogs yon tan yang 
197b6(rgyud): dag pa//de tshe bla mas rtags nas ni//lnga brgya 'am brgyad ni bzung bar bya//grangs med yang dag 'dod chad ni//de nas de la sbyin pa bya//gsum la skyabs 'gro sdig pa bshags//bdag nyid 'bul ba de
197b7(rgyud): dag la//sangs rgyas byang chub ched du sbyin//byang chub sems ni ma lus pa//blo gros ldan pa'i mi yis bzung //de ltar ma lus yang dag bya//sbyar yang dam chos bshad par bya//bskal pa bye ba grangs med du
197b8(rgyud): dal 'byor shin du brnyed par dka'//u dum 'bar wa ba'i me tog ltar//rgya lam na ni res 'ga' zhig//de bas kyang ni 'jig rten mgon//skyob pa shin du rnyed pa dka'//gtso bos gsang sngags
198a1(rgyud): @#//tshal yang ni//sa stengs gsum yang de bzhin no//shin du sdug bsngal 'jigs pa yis//'khor ba'i rgya mtsho 'di ru bu //sa stengs gsang sngags ma rtogs pa//skyob pa su yang ma mthong ngo//de
198a2(rgyud): bas rnam pa thams cad kyi//'khor ba'i skyon ni skyod ni spong 'dod pas//rgas dang na dang 'jigs pa dang /'chi ba'i 'jigs pa rtsub ltas nas//lam mchog 'di ni rten pa na//sdug bsngal ba'i tshogs//lam 
198a3(rgyud): thams cad rgyun chad 'gyur //bdos dang chad pas bcings pa dang //gzhan yang sdug bsngal ba'i tshogs//lam mchog 'di ni bstan pa na//gang du mi 'byung shin du dkon//nye du mdza' lags sdug bsngal
198a4(rgyud): dang //grogs po gnyen dang snag gnyen 'tshams //long spyod pa phun tshogs sdug bsngal ba //nad kyi sdug bsngal gyis gdungs dang //zas kyis 'jigs pa'i sdug bsngal dang //gang yang sa stengs
198a5(rgyud): 'khor ba pa//tshul 'di sten par gyur pa na//'byung ba ru ni mi 'gyur ro//bcu dang brgyad dang de nas lnga//gsum la skyabs su 'gro 'am dus khrims ni//slab pa byin te blang bar bya//sangs
198a6(rgyud): rgyas bstan pa shin du dkon//sdom pa yang dag blangs nas su//lus can rnams kyi srog bzhin du//sdams par gyur pa srung bar bya//dal 'byor phun sum tshogs pa dkon//gsang sngags spyod pa las byung
198a7(rgyud): pa'i//dang por nyams cig sten par bya//yid bzhin nor bu lta ba'i ska//zhi ba sangs rgyas go 'phang thob//lam 'di mchog ste dpal dang ldan//bsam gyis mi khyab 'bras bu rtsol//mi g.yeng bas ni/
198a8(rgyud): sten 'gyur na//bla na med pa'i go 'phang 'gyur//de de ni mchog gi de nyid zhi//sdug bsngal thams cad rnam par sbyangs//shin du gus pa dang ldan na//theg pa chen po thob par 'gyur//de bas khyod kyis theg
198b1(rgyud): chen la //slu ba med par gus par gyis //gang gis khyod ni 'gro bas na //de bzhin gshegs pa 'byung bar 'gyur //sems can kun la phan pa'i yid //bsam pa thams cad rdzogs par byed//khyod
198b2(rgyud): ni theg pa gsum 'chad par //tshul la gnas par de nas 'byung //zab mo yi ni blo gros ldan//zab mo'i don ni ston byed pa//rgyal ba'i tshul ni rten pa ni//de las the tsom med par 'byung //gdod nas
198b3(rgyud): zhi la gnas med pa'i//dam pa'i chos ni de nas bshad //sems can sus thos thams cad kyang //rgyal ba yis skye bar 'gyur//'di na bdag med srog kyang med//dbang phyug kyang med byed pa lang //
198b4(rgyud): 'jig rten byed pa sems can med//skyes pa med cing ma ning med//gang zag kyang gnas kyang med//gang du tshor ba byed pa med//gtso bo yod min khyab pa 'ang min//phra ba med cing
198b5(rgyud): bsal ba med//rgyu 'am gzhan yang med pa ste//rang gi ngo bo rang bzhin med//bdag gi ba'i rang bzhin med//sa stengs na ni rtag pa med//thams cad sgyu ma las byung ltar//byed pa laswogs
198b6(rgyud): rnam par spangs //'di dag thams cad stong par lha //ye shes go 'phang du ni rtag //bdag dang srog laswogs pa kun //byis pa 'ba' zhig gis ni rtags //'di ni bdag med sems can med //
198b7(rgyud): 'dod med 'ba' zhig las byung ba//sgyu ma dang ni smig sgyu dang //dri za'i grong khyer rmi lam ltar //dngos po thams cad stong pa nyid //chu shing lta bur yang dag gnas//gzung du med cing brjod du med //
198b8(rgyud): dbu lta bur gnas //sgyu ma skye ba skye ba med//nam mkha' lta bur stong pa nyid //nam mkha' lta bur gos pa med//dag pa kun du yang dag gnas //zhi ba'i mchog gi de nyid du //rnam pa mchog
199a1(rgyud): @#//gi rtag tu gsungs //de 'dir ye shes mchog thob pa'i //de bzhin gshegs par 'byung 'gyur ro//de nas sngags pas gsol 'debs pa'i//slob ma'i sdig dang bral byed pa//ral gri'i
199a2(rgyud): phyag rgyas srung bar byas //de'i rjesu go cha bgo //go cha shes pas srung bar bya//slob ma la ni phan 'dod pas//sngags kyis tshim par 'dod pa yis//yi ge bco lnga sngags pa yis//thams cad du ni rab
199a3(rgyud): brjod do//yang dag sngags kyis gsang gtor bya//bdod ni ras kyis bcings nas su//mgo bo sa la btud par bya//dkon mchog gsum la sems phyogs te//snying rje dang ni ldan pa'i sems //
199a4(rgyud): skyob pa thams cad mngon gsum du//gyur par bsams te bzhugs par bya//kun kyang dbang phyug go 'phang thob//'jigs pa thams cad rnam par spangs //ci bzhin 'dod pa'i sangs 
199a5(rgyud): rgyas kyang //the tsom med par 'byung bar 'gyur //skyob pa thams cad mngon sum du //gyur par bsams te gzhugs par bya//kun kyang bang phyug go 'phang thob //'jigs pa 
199a6(rgyud): thams cad rnam par spangs//ci bzhin 'dod pa'i sadas rgyas kyang //the tsom med par 'byung bar 'gyur//bla ma la ni yon phul nas//dbang skur ba ni gsol ba gdab //gus par gyur pa'i rang srog
199a7(rgyud): gis//bla ma lnga ni gdud par bya//bla ma la ni dbul ba'i yon//rang gi lus ni snyan mnan te//bdag nyid dngos po dbul bar bya//gzhan du 'bras bu med par 'gyur //rin chen glang po rta dang phyugs
199a8(rgyud): gzhan yang yid la gang bzhed pa//slob ma'i blo bdag gus pa yis//de dag thams cad dbul bar byas//de bzhin bza' rab tu dbul //bla ma gang gis mnyes byas shing //bla ma la ni yon
199b1(rgyud): pul bas//bsam gyis mi khyab 'bras bu gsungs //de bas rnam pa thams cad kyis//rgyal ba'i skyes mchog 'di mchod bya//rdzogs sangs rgyas su de 'dir bshad //sa bcu'i gnas
199b2(rgyud): kyi dbang phyug dang//'jig rten skyong ba thams cad bshad //brgya byin la sogs bdud swogs bzhin//tshangs pa khyab 'jug drag po dang//klu mo 'dul ba'i sras po dang//bza' dang rgyu skar tshogs
199b3(rgyud): thams cad //bung po yang ni rdzu 'phrul che //zag pa zad par bshad par dang//thams cad de nyid du brjod do//dpal ldan 'dul dka' 'dul mdzad pa//phyag na rdo rje sa stengs 'dir//dpal
199b4(rgyud): ldan bstan pa'i don mdzad pa//dam pa'i lam gyi gdam ngag can //rdzogs sangs rgyas kyi ji bzhin sku //yon tan kun cig yang dag bshad //slob dpon gyis ni
199b5(rgyud): yon tan bzung //skyon ni nam yang ma yin te//yon tan gzung bas dngos grub thob//skyon rnams kyis ni dngos grub rlag//de'i grib ma mgon pa ni//mchod rten bshig pa zhes
199b6(rgyud): su bshad //bsam mi khyab pa'i 'bras ster ba//'di ni sangs rgyas lta bur lta//brtan yang g.yo ba'i gsang ba pa//mchog ni thams cad ston par byed//khyod kyis slob dpon smod pa dang/rmi lam du 'ang
199b7(rgyud): 'phrad mi bya//gang yang slob smod pa dang//de ru yang dag 'chad par bya//bgegs dang log par 'dren pas 'dzin //sdig can sems can thag ring du//sa stengs su ni spang bar bya//mnar med la sogs
199b8(rgyud): 'jigs rung bar //dmyal bar yang dag bstan pa gang//slob dpon la ni smod pa gang//de ru yang dag mchod par 'gyur //de bas rnam pa thams cad du//sangs rgyas 'bras bu thob pa yis//
200a1(rgyud): @#//bsam gyis mi khyab 'bras ster ba//'di ni rmad 'byung gus pas mchod //dbang skur cho ga rgyas par ni//bshad par bya yis bu khyod nyon//gang du dbang skur par 'gyur nas//'jig rten sangs
200a2(rgyud): rgyas lta bur lta//de ru pad ma 'dab brgyad byas//khru gang tshad du brjod pa yin//nang na gnas pa'i rdo rje ni//'od zer gyis ni rnam par brgyan //bum pa brgyad ni yongs rdzogs par //gartsang ma'i chu yis yongs su
200a3(rgyud): bkang //mgrin pa ras yug bzang pos bcings//lo 'dab bzang pos kha rgyan byas//khro bo'i rgyal po yis sngags nas//sman dang rin chen kun gyis bkang //gdugs dang rgyal mtshan ba dan dang /rol mo'i
200a4(rgyud): sgra swogs yid 'phrog pa//snyan cing rgya che ba'i tshogs//rab sngags kun du mngon dga' bas//sngags pas de tshe dbang skur te//skyob pa mngon gsum bzhin du bya//dbang skur byas ma thag
200a5(rgyud): du ni//dpal ldan byang chub rtogs phyir byin//de nas bzung ste bu khyod 'di//sa stengs sangs rgyas sras po ni//brtan dang g.yeng bas mchod par ru//byang chub sems dpas bzung bar bya
200a6(rgyud): de ni mi mtheng par gyur nas//byang chub sems dpa' brjod pa yin//bdud dang bgegs dang dmyal dang ni//gzhan yang log par 'dren pa yis//rgyal sras byang chub sems dpa'i ni//de phyir mthong
200a7(rgyud): bar mi 'gyur ro//ma grub pa 'ang myur du 'grub//bdud rnams thams cad 'joms par byed//dkyil 'khor 'dir ni dbang skur ba//de ni nges par sangs rgyas nyid//'dir ni sdug bsngal 'jigs rung //
200a8(rgyud): bar//sdug bsngal slar yang 'byung mi 'gyur//thams cad dbang phyug go 'phang thob//'jigs pa thams cad rnam par spangs//yon tan kun gyis de bzhin rgyan//slob ma la ni sman bya'i phyir
200b1(rgyud): de nas sbyin sreg rab tu rtsam//thab khung dag kyang bya ba ste//de la kha ran dang bcas nas//de'i dbus nas rdo rje ni//zhi ba phyed du rmad du phyung //de'i thab khung khor yug du//bris kyang rjes su 'jug par
200b2(rgyud): bya//me'i thib ni khru gang du//de bzhin gshegs pa yang dag gsungs//me ni kun du 'byung ba'i shing //me thab 'di rangi ri me sbar bya//de nas mchod yon zhal bsil bya//gsang gtor su stengs su ni brjod
200b3(rgyud): ku sha rtsa mo 'gram par bya//slar yang rtsa bas bskor bar bya//de ltar 'di dag thams cad //mar dang sprang rtsi byugs pas mdzes//gang zhig yam shing las rnams ni//rnam par mkhas pas sbyin sreg
200b4(rgyud): bya//sems ni snying rje dang ldan pa//slob ma re re brjod nas su//dpal ldan sreg lugs sreg par bya//nyi shu tham pa nyid kyang ngo //'di dag thams cad byas nasu//sems ni snying rje dang lngan
200b5(rgyud): pa//rgyal mchog thams cad rab btud nas//de nas smon lam gdab par bya//bcom ldan 'das kyis bsags pa yi//bsod nams grangs med gzhal mi lang //des ni 'gro
200b6(rgyud): ba ma lus pa//bla med go 'phang thob par shog//sems can dmyal ba grangs med kyang //gzhan yang ngan 'gror 'gyur ba gang //bdag gis bsod nams mthu yis ni//thams cad bde bar ldan gyur
200b7(rgyud): cig//gang zhig yid dgas sdug bsngal dang //gang yang ngan 'gror gyur pa rnams//bdag gi bsod nams mthu yis ni //de dag thams cad bral 'gyur shog//ji snyed sems can thams cad dag //
200b8(rgyud): sdug bsngal sna tshogs nyid dag dang //de dag thams cad zhi gyur nas//'ga' yang sdug bsngal ma gyur cig//ji snyed sems can nyes spyad las//lus dang ngag dang yid las byung //sems can thaMd
201a1(rgyud): //de ma gyur //kun kyang bde dang ldan gyur cig//de nas rgyal mchog kun btud nas //dri dang me tog swogs thogs te//steng dang 'og bltas gyur pas //de nas rab tu sdu ba'i phyir//de ltar slar yang 
201a2(rgyud): brjod par bya//skyob pa thams cad mngon gsum du//bdag la yang yang rtse ba'i phyir //bcom ldan 'das sa dang bcas pa yang //ji srid bdag gi don grub par //bdag nyid chen po slar byon mdzod//de nas gtor
201a3(rgyud): ma me tog dang //dri dang bcas pa'i rtul mtshon la//sngags pa dngos grub mchog 'dod pas//lag pa sa reg par mi bya'o//de nas rang gi byin brlabs te//sa la 'dug nas gos 'phur bas //'bad pas gyur pas/
201a4(rgyud): bsdus nas ni//chu bo la ni bskur bar bya//de ltar rab tu 'bad pas kyang //nyi ma gnyis pa shar ba la//de tshe dngos grub kun byed pa'i//phyag rgya shes rab can gyis bstan//gang zhig bcings pa tsam gyis ni

201a7(rgyud): bya yis nyon//mgo bor yang ni thob par bcas//spyi bor rdo ba dag dang ldan //bka' rtags phyag rgya snying khar ste//phyogs bcing bar ni rab tu bya//de bzhin mar me dri phyag rgya//gtor ma'i phyag rgya bdug
201a8(rgyud): pa yang //me tog zhal zas phyag rgya dang //spyan drang ba ni nyid dag dang //de bzhin gshegs pa rgyal sras dang //nyan thos dang yang pho nya swogs //de bzhin 'jig rten thams cad pa//de bzhin drang srong
201b1(rgyud): tshogs rnams dang //srid mo yang dag bshad pa dang //ma mo'i ni bdun nyid dang //de dag rnams ni ma lus pa//sa stengs su ni rgyas par nyon //tshang ngam rkang cig mi g.yo bar//phan pa'i sems

201b6(rgyud): mthe bo nang du ni//bcug pa sdig 'dzub shugs yin te//bcug pa 'di ni zhags par brjod //dpal ldan phyag rgya 'di 'chang ba//zhabs pa'i phyag rgya zhes su grags //sa bcu gnas kyi dbang
201b7(rgyud): phyug rnams //kun nas rab tu 'gugs 'gyur na//bdug pa'i sems can ras bal ltar //gang las ci'i phyir mi lta//gung mo'i sor mo yang dag sbyar //phan tshun du ni rtse sprad de //'ju ba
201b8(rgyud): mo dang ni ming med dag //bkug nas lag pa'i nang du bcug //mthe bo de la kha stan gnan//mthe'u chung drang por gzhag pa ni//rdzogs sangs rkang gnyis mchog gis ni//'di ni cho ga'i
202a1(rgyud): @#//phyag rgyar gsungs //ye shes ral gri snying khar ni//yan lag lnga pa 'ang rnam par dgod //de tshe phyag rgya go cha bgo //phyag rgya sems can sdom pa yin //de ltar rab tu 'bad

202a3(rgyud): rnams dang ni//gzhan yang dgra'o 'byung ba la//bdag dang gzhan ni sgrub bya'i phyir//'di ni rtag tu sbyar bar bya//lag pa phan tshun bzung nas su//lag pa gnyis ni brtan por bya//

202a5(rgyud): nas bzung ste//g.yas pa'i mthe bong gyen du sgreng //rdo rje ltar bur gzhal ba ni//me ni chen po lta bur 'bar;lag pa rdo rje lta bur gnas //sngags pa gang la 'khros gyur

202b1(rgyud): par bya//g.yas pa'i phyag rgya khu tshur bcing //gung mo gyen du rnam par sgreng //rdzogs sangs rgyas kyi dbang phyug gis//'di ni spyi gcug dag du gsungs //lag pa g.yon pa khu tshur bcing //'dzub mo gung
202b2(rgyud): mo sgrengs nas ni//de na g.yon pas rtag tu bcing //'di ni bka' rtags phyag rgyar grags//lag pa gnyis ni khu tshur ni//bcings nas phan tshun bcing bar bya//gcig du rtan par gtugs pa ni//rdo ba

202b5(rgyud): sdugs kyi bzlas pa bya//lag pa gnyis ni thal sbyar nas //gung mo nang du sbyar bar bya//rdzogs sangs rgyas rkang gnyis dam pa yis//dri'i phyag rgya zhes su bshad//lag pa'i phyi rol 
202b6(rgyud): sgril nas ni//mthe bong gung mo gyen du sgreng //mthe'u chung yang sgreng nas gzhag //rgyal dang rgyal sras rnams kyis kyang //mar me'i phyag rgya zhes su bshad //slar yang lag pa phyir bkrol nas //
202b7(rgyud): phyin ci log bcas gung mo dang //'dzum mo mthe bong snga ma bzhin//tshogs pa shin du sten par bya//sngags dang lhan cig bcas pa ni//gtor ma'i phyag rgya zhes su brjod //lag pa'i shugs
202b8(rgyud): kyis phyag rgya ru//shugs ni lta bur rab tu mdzes//gdug spos phyag rgya mdor sdus na//rgyal ba skyes bu mchog gis gsungs//gung mo ming med sbyar bkug nas//mthe bong steng nas rnam par

203a4(rgyud): shin du mnyam par gzhag pa yis//shes can gyis rtags bsams na//rnag pa'i 'bras bu thob par 'gyur//lag pa gnyis ni thal sbyar nas//mkhas pas 'dzub mo bkug nas ni//rtse mor
203a5(rgyud): mthe bong gzhag pa ni//rgyal sras rtag tu dgug par byed//lag pa pad ma ltar byas nas//sor mo dang ni ming med dag;;'di rnams pa thams cad 'gugs par byed//nyan thos rnaMs
203a6(rgyud): dang sa stengs bzhugs//gang yang pho nya mo sogs bshad//pho nya dang ni rdzu 'phrul che//de rnams phyag rgya bshad pa ni//sa stengs lcags kyus 'gugs par byed//g.yas pa khu tshur
203a7(rgyud): dmar por bcing//brtan po rab tu ldan byas te//'dzub mo shes rab can gyis rkyang //tshig pa gsum pa bcag par bya//lag pa g.yas pas g.yon pa yi//'khrig ma nas ni bzung ba yis//sa stengs pho
203a8(rgyud): nya ma la sogs//bsdams par gyur pas dgug par bya//lag pa'i phyi rol bkris nas su//gung mo gyen du rnam par sgreng//'dzub mo drang por byas nas ni//de dang rab tu sprad par bya//nang gi chu ru bkug n+sa

203b2(rgyud): 'di pa dgug pa yin//lag pa g.yon pa drang bcas nas//mkhas pas gang mo dgug pa yin//lag pa g.yon pa drang bcas nas//mkhas pas gung mo dgug par bya//'jub mo sor mo yang bkug nas

203b5(rgyud): yis//phyag rgya 'di ni yang dag gsungs //rtag tu bag yod par gyur pas //sa bzhi'i dkyil 'khor srung bar bya//phyag rgya 'di ni 'chang la dkon//rgyal ba mgo gis rab tu

209a1(rgyud): @#//ltung //'jigs su rung ba'i klog kyang 'khrugs //sa bzhi thams cad 'bar ba ltar //sa stengs kun ni me phung po//phyogs kun 'jigs pa chen por 'gyur //sa'i bzhi'i dkyil 'khor kun
209a2(rgyud): tu 'khrugs //mun nag 'jigs pa chen po ru //sa bzhi dkyil 'khor mthong bar 'gyur //sangs rgyas nyan thos lha dang ni//byang chub sems dpa' rdzu 'phrul che //lam gol ba'i yid can de//
209a3(rgyud): ral gro'i phyag rgya bsnams nas ni//sngags pa hUM ni sgrogs pas kyang //de ni skams nas brlag par ni//the tsom med par 'gyur ba ru//rgyal ba mchog gis rtag tu gsungs //rkang ba mnyam
209a4(rgyud): por gzhag nas gnas//mchan khung dag tu lag pa bzhug //slar yang lag pas dgab bar bya//so yis mchu mnan de bzhin no//slar yang g.yon rkyang ba yis gnas //lag pa 
209a5(rgyud): g.yas pas char nas ni//g.yon pa snying khar rnam par dgod //bskal pa'i me ni lta bur byung //bgegs kyi gnas su bsnun nas ni//de ma thag tu thal bar rlog //rkang 
209a6(rgyud): pa kyog por byas nas ni//lag pa g.yas dang g.yon pa nyid //brla la bzung nas stong par bya//khros pas ya sos ma mchu mnan//'og tu lta stengs dbab par bya//gegs rnams thams
209a7(rgyud): cad gdugs nas ni//blo dang ldan pas mchan khung bzhug//thams cad gdung ba nyid du 'gyur //gang yang rlung dang srin po dang //'jigs pa chen po'i bgegs dang ni//gang yang skye bo rnam 'tshe ba//
209a8(rgyud): phyag rgya 'di ni sbyar ba yis//de dag thams cad rlag par 'gyur //lag pa rgyab tu btang nas ni//g.yas kyis rnam par sgyur bar bya//yang ni mi 'thun gya tshogs gnas //rdo rje tho ba brdeg
209b1(rgyud): par bya//bgegs kyi gnas su nyer song ste//khro bo lta stengs dbab par bya//gal te sa stengs rdo rje 'dzin//bgegs kyi gzugs la snun pa ni//'di ni the tsom med par 'chi//g.yon pa rkyang ba'i rkang pa
209b2(rgyud): yis//sngags pa shar du phyogs gyur pas//zhags pa'i phyag rgya go cha bzang //me'i 'od dang 'tshungs par 'bar//sa bcu'i gnas kyi dbang phyug gis//bgegs rnams thams cad 'gugs par
209b3(rgyud): byed//kun nas dgug par byas nas ni//ral gri'i phyag rgya cod pan byed//thams cad 'gugs par gyur pa na//shin du mi bzad cho nges 'debes//de ring bdag cag rnams kyis dran//ma la bu dang
209b4(rgyud): bu mo rnams//yang ni sdug bsngal 'di khyed gzigs//'di ni 'chi ba nyid du 'gyur//gal te gson por 'dod na ni//bgegs ni rab tu ma byed cig//sngags pa bdag nyid chen po yi//bka'
209b5(rgyud): sgo bcag par mi bya'o//bgegs kyi lags tu sog par bzhag//slar yang g.yas kyis song ba yis//rkad pas myur du mnan par bya//bdag nyid ri rab tu 'gyur par//bsam nas
209b6(rgyud): bdag nyid chen po 'di//khams gsum dag ni ma lus par//thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du 'gyur//gang yang de gegs dang log 'dren gzhan//sngags g.yas kyis song ba yis pas hU~M sgra bsgrogs
209b7(rgyud): pa las//de ni slar yang klags mi tshol//gnan pa yang dag bshad pa 'di//gsang sngags kun gyis gsang ba yin//gal te sngags kyi 'bras 'dod na//'di ni gang la'ang mi bshad do//rang 
209b8(rgyud): gis bdag nyid lha'i sku//de ni phyag rgya dkyil 'khor ldan//rang gi mtshan ma sa bon dang //rang bzhin phyi ru bcos par ni//rtag par rgyun du bsnun par bya//gzhan du na ni 'bras bu med//mdor sdus
210a1(rgyud): //na ni 'di ni bu//bdag gis gsang ba gsungs pa yin//phyag rgya tshang dam cho ga ni//de ni kun du ma chad cig //gang yang sdig can sems can gzhan //bla ma la smod 'jigs su rung
210a2(rgyud): pha dang ma ni mi shes shing//gang yang dkon mchog gsum la gnod //'jigs pa'i gzugs can dad pa med //gang yang 'tshams med lnga byed pa//sems dpa' med cing snyoms pa dang //
210a3(rgyud): spros ba med cing snyoms las ldan //ser sna'i dri ma'i 'dam 'dir bying//gang yang rtag tu gnas pa ni//sangs rgyas kyis gsungs gsang ba ni//de rnams la ni bshad mi bya//gang yang bdug pa'i
210a4(rgyud): sems can gzhan //rtag tu sdig pa'i las can te//khro bo'i rgyal po mi bzla zhing//dkyil 'khor yang ni bstan mi bya//gsang sngags mi shes phyag rgya med//gang yang bla ma gsum byin
210a5(rgyud): ba//de rnams 'ga' lang rgyal ba yis//gsungs pa'i nus pa kun mi shes//gsang ba cung zad gang yod pa//khro bo'i ming can snying po 'di//sems can rnams la phan 'dod
210a6(rgyud): pas //de ni ma lus bdag gis bshad //gsang ba 'di la rten nas ni//gang yang 'das pa'i sangs rgyas dang/'gro la phan phyir 'byung 'gyur pa'i//gang yang mgon po ma byon ba//
210a7(rgyud): 'gro la phan pa'i tshul gyis ni//mgon po da ltar bzhugs pa gang //de kun mgon po bsam du med//ye shes rtul phod mtshungs pa med//rnam par spyad na bsam du med //rnyog pa med pa'i
210a8(rgyud): dam chos ni //zhing du bshad par gyur pa yang //thams cad du ni bdud pas phan//bzod nas zhi la gnas med pa//chos kyi 'khor lo bskor bar 'gyur //sbas gsang mnyam pa'i ye shes
210b1(rgyud): ni//bdag gis gang yang ma bstan pa//sa stengs dkyil 'khor gsang ba de //ma lus bar ni bdag gis gsungs //mgon po grangs med 'das pa dang //grang yang 'byung bar 'gyur ba gzhan
210b2(rgyud): 'gro la phan phyir 'dir bzhugs pa'i //gang yang da ltar byung gyur pa//kun gyi gsang ba'i ye shes gang //bsam gyis mi khyab 'bras ster ba //gsang ba'i ye shes pha rol mnon //de dag ma
210b3(rgyud): bdag gis gsungs //gang zhig rgyud kun du gsungs pa//gsang ba mchog gi rmad du byung //de dag ma lus pa ru gsungs//bsam gyi mi khyab pa'i 'bras rtsol ba //thams cad dbang phyug
210b4(rgyud): go 'phang thob//bde ba thams cad yang dag btang //'jigs pa thams cad rnam gral bar //lus can thams cad 'byung bar 'gyur //'di ni myur du dngos grub byed//bsam 
210b5(rgyud): gyis mi khyab 'bras bu rtsol //sems can thams cad srung ba yi//gsang ba'i pha rol gnon pa gsungs //de bas rnam pa thams cad du//gsang ba'i gsang ba yang dag 'byung //
210b6(rgyud): rtag tu bag yod par gyur pas //srung ba'i don du rgyal bas gsungs //sngags pas bdag nyid chen po yis //mchog tu yid 'ong yang dag bshad //gsang ba'i ye shes rtul phod pa //sngags 
210b7(rgyud): las ma sgrubs pa yang 'grub //gsang ba'i ye shes pha rol gnon//gang la bshad par mi bya ste //rdzogs sangs rkang gnyis mchog gis ni//mgon po bsam gyis mi khyab gsungs //
210b8(rgyud): rgyal po med cing bdug pa 'ang med //bgegs dang log par 'dren pa'ang med//srin po gnod sbyin dbang po med//gdug pa'i bza' dang ma mo med //dus min 'chi dang 'jigs pa med//
211a1(rgyud): @#//nad kyis kun du 'khrugs pa la //de dag thams cad ngo mtshar 'gyur //kun mkhyen sngar 'dug nas ni//snyan pa yi ni tshig 'di gsol//kun mkhyen bkod pa bsdu bar mdzod//sems can
211a2(rgyud): sdug bsngal kun tu 'khrugs//sa bzhi snying por gyur pa 'di //tshul 'di bzod par mi nus so//de dag kun mkhyen bzhengs nas ni//ting nge 'dzin ni gtor gyur pas//skyob pa byang chub sems dpa'i //
211a3(rgyud): snyan pa'i tshig gis bka' stsal pa//byang chub sems dpa' ma 'jigs shig//gang yang 'jigs pas gzir ba'i lha//gzhan yang bsdus pa'i sems can ni//'jig rten pa dang rgyu mi rgyu //rdzogs
211a4(rgyud): sangs rkang gnyis dam pa yis//'di ni bsam du med par bshad //de nas sems can thams cad kyis//bde ba thams cad yang dag thob//thams cad dbang phyug go 'phang thob//sdug bsngal
211a5(rgyud): thams cad rnam par spangs //bsam pa thams cad rdzogs nas ni//de ma thag tu sa stengs 'gyur //khro bo'i snying rgyal po'i snying po 'di//rgyal ba mchog gis rtag tu gsungs //stobs chen 
211a6(rgyud): 'di ni mi mthong bar //khro bo'i rgyal po rang nyid gnas //gnas med pa la sbyar mi bya//gsang sngags gsang ba pha rol gnon//rang nyid de bzhin gshegs pa ste//'di ni khro bo'i gzugs
211a7(rgyud): kyis bzhugs //las rnams sna tshogs yang dag ldan//gsang sngags 'di ni klags pas 'grub //'tshams med lnga po byas pa yang //'di'i myur du 'grub par 'gyur //chos grangs med
211a8(rgyud): cing sgyu skar med//snyung bar yang ni med par brjod//khrus kyi bya ba thams cad med //dri sogs cho ga'i rim pa med//ras ris med cing gnas yod min//dus kyi cho ga'i snying rim pa med//
211b1(rgyud): sdig pa kun dang ldan pas kyang //gang du yang dag gnas bsgrub pa//slar yang bcom ldan 'das kyis ni//thugs rje dang ni ldan pa'i thugs //rdor 'byung che ba'i ba dag nyid la //
211b2(rgyud): snyan pa'i tshig gis bka' stsal ba//gsang sngags rgyal po rdzu 'phrul che //khro rgyal nye ba'i snying po ni//dngos grub myur du stsol mdzad pa//rgyal ba mchog gis rtag tu gsungs //na ma sarba
211b3(rgyud): pA ri pU ra ke b+h+ya /sarba ta thA ga te b+h+ya /sarba thA tra Ta/a mo ga tsa na/ma hA ro sha na phaT ya hU~M //tra sa ya hU~M tra kra hA~M mo////phyag na rdo rje 'dir ni khro bo chen po'i snying po yin no//las kun
211b4(rgyud): nus pa can 'di ni//sngags kyi rgyal po rdzu 'phrul che ////yid 'dod bsam gyis mi khyab pa//ma sgrubs pa'i las kyang byed//rgyud rgyal gzhan pa rnams su ni//cho ga rgyal ba bshad
211b5(rgyud): pa gang //'di nyid kyi ni cho ga yis//de yang grangs med grub par bya//khro ba'i mngag gzhug bka' mnyan pa//pho nya pho nya rdzu 'phrul che//cho ga 'di yi rim pa yis//de yang ji ltar rigs
211b6(rgyud): pas sgrubs //gang yang 'jig rten pa'i lha//gang yang khyab 'jug la sogs klu//gza' dang rgyu skar tshogs thams cad //grul bum dang ni lus sprul po//gnod sbyin lha min mi 'am ci//
211b7(rgyud): gnod sbyin mo dang sha za dang //gang yang srin po dang ni mi //drang srong mkha' 'gro ma de bzhin //ma mo dang ni srin mo nyid //gang yang 'jig rten bar bshad pa//de yang cho ga 'di nyid kyis//
211b8(rgyud): the tsom med par sgrub par bya//na ma sarba pA ri phU ra ke b+h+ya//sarba ta thA ga te b+h+ya /sarba thA /ha na ha na/ma ra ya dz+nya na khad ga hU~M phaT//ral gri'i phyag rgya'i sngags so//na ma sarba pA ri pU ra ke b+h+ya//sarba
212a1(rgyud): @#//sata thA ga te b+ha//sarba thA /oM g+h+ri h+na g+h+ri hi/kad d+ha kad d+ha/pa se na paM d+ha paM d+ha/mahA ma t+ye te d+ha ma te swahA///zhags pa'i phyag rgya'i sngags so//na ma sarba pA ri pU ra ke b+h+ya /sarba ta thA ga ta b+h+ya
212a2(rgyud): sarba thA/rak+sha rak+sha/ha na ha na//kud d+ha kud d+ha/ma ra ya duSh+TaM bi na aM kraM hUM dza//go cha'i phyag rgya'i sngags so/namaHsarba sa pA ri pU ra ke b+ha //sarba ta thA ga te b+h+ya /sarba ta thA ga te b+h+ya//sarba thA/d+ha ra d+ha ra /badzra na li
212a3(rgyud): khad ga pa sha d+ha ra swahA//spyi rtsug gi phyag rgya'i sngags so//namaHsarba sa pA ri pUr ke b+h+ya /sarba ta tha ga te b+h+ya/sarba thA/b+haM dza b+haM dza/d+ha ha pa tsa b+ha mi ku ru /ha na ha na//badzra g+h+ri na hUM phaT/tha ba'i phyag rgya'i
212a4(rgyud): sngags so//na maHsarba sa pA ri pU rake b+h+ya//sarba ta thA ga te b+h+ya /sarba thA/Sh+TAM traM mur te/da ha da ha/sad+ha ya sa d+ha ya/bi dza ya bi dza ya bi dza ya ki ti swahA/mgo bo phyag rgya'i sngagaso//
212a5(rgyud): na maHsarba sa pA ri pU ru ke b+h+ya//sarba ta thA ba te b+h+ya/sarba thA/b+hur d+ha rA/a tsi na t+ya pa lang bi kra ma /oM b+hur bu pa la ya rak+sha rak+sha //sarba swahA/rdo ba'i phyag rgya'i sngags so//
212a6(rgyud): na maHsarba sa pA ri prU ra ke b+h+ya/sarba thA /badzra hri da ma da ma /hUM badzra ma tri ki swahA//snying kha'i phyag rgya'i sngags so/na ma sarba sa pA ri pU ra ke b+h+ya /sarba ta thA ga te b+h+ya//sarba thA /hUM da ha da ha /
212a7(rgyud): ki tsi ra ya/si a na yaM/sha g+h+raM rak+sha rak+sha/baM d+ha bA d+ha/duSh+Ta da ma da ma swahA//phyogs bcing ba'i phyag rgya'i sngags so//na maHsarba sa pA ri pUr ke b+h+ya /sarba ta tha ga te b+h+ya//sarba thA/oM 'dun na d+hu na
212a8(rgyud): mahA pa sha khad ga d+ha ra /sarba pA ri pU ra ke b+ha bya hA//bka' stegs kyi phyag rgya'i sngagaso//na ma sarba pA ri pU ra ke ba d+h+ya /sarba ta thA ga te ba ta b+h+ya/sarba thA/ga ga na gA d+hod b+ha ba mahA sa ma ya pA ri pu ra
212b1(rgyud): ke swahA//dri'i phyag rgya'i sngags so//na ma sarba sa pA ri pU ra ke b+h+ya /sarba ta thA ga te b+h+ya //sarba thA/bi shud d+hi dz+nyA na tsi sa ba bI ga na naM ta swahA//mar me'i phyag rgya'i sngags so//nam sarba sa pA ri pU 
212b2(rgyud): ke b+h+ya//sarba ta thA ga te b+h+ya/sarba thA ga te b+h+ya/sarba thA/shud d+ha d+harma te shud d+h+ya //g+h+ri h+na g+h+ri na/sarba sa pA ri pU ra ko b+h+ya swahA//gtor ma'i phyag rgya'i sngags so//na ma sarba sa pA ri pA ra ke b+h+ya/sarba 
212b3(rgyud): ta
212b4(rgyud): thA ga te b+h+ya/sarba thA/bi shud d+ha ma hA dz+nya na sam b+ha be/dwI ste dI ste/mahA na ye swahA/bdug pa'i phyag rgya'i sngags so//ma sarba sa pA ri pUr ke b+h+ya //sarba thA/ga ga na bi ku ta ga ma ni mahA shud d+ha
212b5(rgyud): na ye swahA//me tog gi phyag rgya'i sngags so//naM maHsarba sA pA ri pUr ke b+h+ya/sarba ta thA ga te b+h+ya//sarba thA/ha na ha na ba ra ma bi shud d+he d+harma na ya pA ri pu ra ke swahA//zhal zas kyi phyag rgya'i
212b6(rgyud): sngags so//na maHsarba sA pA ri pu ra ke b+h+ya/sarba ta tha ga te b+h+ya/sarba thA/a sra pra ha tod t+ya ta da ma da ma/sarba thA sa pA ri pU ra ke swahA//dpa' bo chen po spyan drang ba'i sngags so//
212b7(rgyud): na ma sarba sA pA ri pU ra ke b+h+ya/sarba ta thAg te b+h+ya/sarba tha/bi shud d+ha bishud d+ha/sa tra sra b+ha be/a na ya a na ya/mahA na me swahA//byang chub sems dpa' spyan drang ba'i sngags so//na maHsarba sA pA
212b8(rgyud): ri pU ra ke b+h+ya/sarbata thA ga te b+h+ya/sarba thA/b+hi shud d+ha/swa ta/ha na ha na/da ma da ma/mahA na ya swahA//nyan thos spyan drang ba'i sngags so/na ma sarba sA pA ro pI ra ke b+h+ya/sarba ta thA ga te b+h+ya/sarba tha 
212b9(rgyud): oM badzra kar sha ya//shi g+h+ri da ha da ha/pa tsa pa tsa/mahA na yA pA ri b+ha re swahA//pho nya mo la sogs pa spyan drang ba'i sngags so/na maHsarba swa pA ri pU ra ke b+ha /sarba ta thA ga te b+h+ya /sarba thA ma thA ma thA//
213a1(rgyud): //a na ya a na ya/pra pe sha ya pra pe sha ya/sarba bud d+harma /sarba de bam /ta ma ta ma /maha na ya pA re b+ha re swahA//'jig rten pa rnams spyan drang ba'i sngags so//na maHsarba sA pA ri pU ra ke b+h+ya
213a2(rgyud): sarba ta thA ga te b+h+ya/sarba thA sad+ya sat+ya /mahA sat+ya /da ma da ma /mahA da ma /A kar sha yA /a kar sha yA /sat+ya b+ha ma hA na ya shud d+he swahA//drang srong rnams spyan drang ba'i sngags//na maHsarba
213a3(rgyud): sA pA ri pU ra keb+h+ya /sarba ta thA ga te b+ha/sarba thA /dza ya dza ya /bi dza ya bi dza ya/mah+ya sat+ya b+ha di ni /sarba hri te swahA//sring mo rnams spyan drang ba'i sngagasaso//na mo sarba pa ri pu ra ke b+h+ya/sarba ta tha ga
213a4(rgyud): ta b+he /hra ha ha /ma thA ma thA/da ma da ma /ha na ha na /mahA ma ye ma ra ma ra swahA//ma mo rnams spyan drang ba'i sdigs so//na ma sarba sA pA ri pU rake b+h+ye/sarba oM ha na ha na /badzra hUM
213a5(rgyud): phaT/sarba du ShA ga hUM phaT /tha ma tha ma /b+haM dza b+haM dza //sarba bo g+h+ra da ma da ma /sarba du Sh+Ta /oM hUM phaT swahA////thun mong gi ni tshim ngam du/rigs 'di
213a6(rgyud): 'jigs rten gtse bis gsungs //phyag rgya rnams ni ma lus dang //sngags 'di rgyas bar gsungs pa yin//don kun grub par byed pa'i sngags //'di ni myur du 'grub byed cing //
213a7(rgyud): bsam gyis mi khyab 'bras rtsol ba//shin du gsang ba yang dag gsungs //phyag rgya dang ni lhan cig sbyar //dri dang me tog bdug pa dang //gtor ma yang ni ma lus pa//sus kyang gzhon bar
213a8(rgyud): mi nus so //phyags bcing ba dang de nas bzang //gzhan yang yid la ci 'dod 'byung //de kun sngags dang bcas pa yi//phyag rgyar sbyar bas 'grub par 'gyur //de nas bcom ldan 'das
213b1(rgyud): kyis ni //phyag na rdo rje la bka' stsal //khro bo'i rgyal po rnam par 'phrul //'di ni khyed la rjes su sbyin //zhi ba dang ni rgyas pa dang //de bzhin mngon spyod dbang du bya//de bzhin sna tshogs 
213b2(rgyud): gyur pa dang //khyod la rag lus mchog tu 'dod //bsod nams du ma las 'khrungs pa//mtshan dang dpe byad bzang pos 'bar //mtshan rnams thams cad yongs su rdzogs//phyag rgya brgyad
213b3(rgyud): pa sngon 'gro bas//bdag gis rdzogs sangs rgyas dbang skur //bde gshegs las 'khrungs rdo rje can //zab mo dbyangs kyi sgra yis ni//bde bar gshegs pas kun gsungs pa//khro ba'i 
213b4(rgyud): rgyal po cho ga'i rigs//rim pa ji bzhin gsungs pa nyid khyod gcig pu brtan g.yo ba'i//bstan pa dag ni srung bar gnas dam pa'i chos ni sprul pa'i sku//sa snyengs na ni zhi bar
213b5(rgyud): 'gyur //sangs rgyas kun gyi spyan sngar ni//sa stengs khyod nyid gcig pu gnas //'das dang ma byon par gyur dang //'gro la phan phyir bzhugs rnams kyi//kha lo sbyar 
213b6(rgyud): ba khyod gcig pu//bla na med pa'i go 'phang 'thob//rdo rje sems dpa' blo ldan pa//slar yang phyag dag gzhag pas kyang //dbang chen thams cad g.yos nas ni//sa bzhi rnam pa drug 
213b7(rgyud): du bskyod//de dag thams cad sngon 'gyur te//sangs rgyas dam chos bsam med par//bsam gyis mi khyab 'bras bu thob //sa stengs rdo rje sems dpa'i bdag //nam mkha'
213b8(rgyud): mdzad kyi 'od zer thob //'phags pa chen po rmad byung bar//'jig rten yang dag 'byung 'gyur te//sa bzhi snying po 'dir ru yin//de nas bcom ldan rgyal gyur pas//sa bzhi
214a1(rgyud): @#//byang chub sems dpa' la //rten dang g.yo ba'i gsang ba rnams//snyan pa'i gsung gis bka' stsal pa//bdag gis khyod ni tshim par byas//bdag nyid chen po rdo rje 'chang
214a2(rgyud): rtag tu bag yod par gyur par //bdag nyid chen pos de mnyes bya//de nas bcom ldan 'das zhi ba//chos kyi dbyings la rab tu gzhog//chos nyid gcig po thugs chud nas //kun mkhyen
214a3(rgyud): cang mi gsung bar 'gyur //de nas 'khor rnams thams cad kyis//ngo mtshar ba'i tshig gsol pa//sangs rgyas che ba bsam mi khyab //'jig rten mgon pos bstan pa nyid//de nas bzung
214a4(rgyud): ste bdag cag kun //rtog pa gcig tu mthar phyin 'gyur//bdag nyid chen po rdo rja can//bde bar gshegs pas gtad par 'gyur //'khor rnams shin du dga' ba 
214a5(rgyud): yis//snyan pa'i yi ni tshig gis smras //skal pa chen po mchog tu ni//ci bgyi ba'i dka' sgo stsol //de nas dpal ldan rdo rje 'chang //me stag 'phro bar gyur pa 
214a6(rgyud): yis//thugs rje ldan pa'i thugs kyis su//'khor rnams la ni bka' stsal pa//khro rgyal ras ris cho ga ni//rdzu 'phrul chen po de nyon cig//gang zhig sngags kyi sgo nas ni//byang

220b2(rgyud): gzhag par bya//bzhi pa yungs mar gyis bkang ba//de'i steng du gzhag par bya//skal gyi shubs ni blangs nas su//mi de nang du bzhug par bya//dum pu re rer brtubs nas su//thod pa re rer bzhug par bya//
220b3(rgyud): brgyad rtsa brgyad ni mngon sngags nas//ras ris byang du zhal gzigs nas //sngags pa lho ru phyogs par bya//khros pas btsog btsog par 'dug ste//skra bkrol ba'i khro bo nyid //sngags pas cug pa tsog
